# FEDERAL PENAL CODE

FEDERAL PENAL CODE
New Code Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on August 14, 1931 CURRENT TEXT
Last reform published DOF 05-08-2023

In the margin a seal that reads: Federal Executive Power.- United Mexican States.-Mexico.- Ministry of the Interior.

The Constitutional President of the United Mexican States has been pleased to address me the following Decree:
PASCUAL ORTIZ RUBIO, Constitutional President of the United Mexican States, to its inhabitants, know ye:
That in use of the powers granted to him by Decree of January 2, 1931, he has seen fit to issue the following
FEDERAL PENAL CODE
Name of the Code reformed: DOF 12-23-1974 (formerly "Penal Code for the Federal District and Territories in Matters of Common Jurisdiction, and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction"), DOF 12-31-1974, 05-18-1999 (formerly "Penal Code for the Federal District
in matters of Common Jurisdiction, and for the entire Republic in matters of Federal Jurisdiction")

## FIRST BOOK PRELIMINARY TITLE

Article 1.- This Code shall apply throughout the Republic for crimes of federal jurisdiction.
Article reformed DOF 12-23-1974, 05-18-1999

Article 2.- It shall also apply:
I. For crimes that are initiated, prepared or committed abroad, when they produce or are intended to have effects in the territory of the Republic; or, for crimes that are initiated, prepared or committed abroad, provided that a treaty binding on Mexico provides for the obligation to extradite or prosecute, the requirements provided in Article 4 of this Code are met and the probable perpetrator is not extradited to the State that has requested him, and
Section reformed DOF 06-28-2007

II.- For crimes committed in Mexican consulates or against their personnel, when they have not been tried in the country where they were committed.
Article 3.- Continuous crimes committed abroad, which continue to be committed in the Republic, shall be prosecuted in accordance with the laws of the Republic, whether the offenders are Mexican or foreign.
The same rule shall apply in the case of continuing crimes.
Paragraph added DOF 01-14-1985

Article 4.- Crimes committed in foreign territory by a Mexican against Mexicans or against foreigners, or by a foreigner against Mexicans, shall be punished in the Republic, in accordance with federal laws, if the following requirements are met:

I.- That the accused is found in the Republic;

II.- That the offender has not been definitively tried in the country where he committed the crime, and
III.- That the infraction of which he is accused has the character of a crime in the country where it was committed and in the Republic.
Article 5.- The following shall be considered as executed in the territory of the Republic:
I.- Crimes committed by Mexicans or by foreigners on the high seas, aboard national vessels;

II.- Those executed aboard a national warship anchored in port or in territorial waters of another nation. This extends to the case where the vessel is a merchant ship, if the offender has not been tried in the nation to which the port belongs;
III.- Those committed aboard a foreign vessel anchored in a national port or in territorial waters of the Republic, if public tranquility is disturbed or if the offender or the victim are not part of the crew. Otherwise, action shall be taken in accordance with the right of reciprocity;
IV.- Those committed aboard national or foreign aircraft that are in national or foreign territory or atmosphere or territorial waters, in cases analogous to those indicated for vessels in the preceding sections, and
V.- Those committed in Mexican embassies and legations.

Article 6.- When a crime not provided for in this Code is committed, but is provided for in a special law or in an international treaty of mandatory observance in Mexico, these shall be applied, taking into account the provisions of the First Book of this Code and, where applicable, the relevant provisions of the Second Book.

When the same matter appears regulated by various provisions, the special shall prevail over the general.
In case of crimes committed against girls, boys and adolescents, the best interest of children, which must prevail in all application of law, shall always be sought.

### FIRST TITLE

Criminal Liability

Paragraph added DOF 08-19-2010 Article reformed DOF 01-14-1985

#### CHAPTER I

General rules on crimes and liability
Article 7.- A crime is the act or omission sanctioned by criminal laws.

In crimes of material result, the typical result produced shall also be attributable to the person who omits to prevent it, if such person had the legal duty to prevent it. In these cases, the result shall be considered to be

a consequence of an omissive conduct, when it is determined that the person who omits to prevent it had the duty to act to do so, derived from a law, a contract or his own preceding conduct.
Paragraph added DOF 01-10-1994

A crime is:
I. Instantaneous, when the consummation is exhausted at the same moment in which all the elements of the criminal description have been realized;
Section reformed DOF 06-17-2016

II.- Permanent or continuous, when the consummation is prolonged over time, and
III.- Continuing, when with unity of criminal purpose, plurality of conducts and unity of passive subject, the same legal precept is violated.
Section reformed DOF 05-13-1996 Article reformed DOF 01-13-1984

Article 8.- Criminal actions or omissions can only be committed intentionally or negligently.
Article reformed DOF 01-13-1984, 01-10-1994

Article 9.- A person acts intentionally when, knowing the elements of the criminal type, or foreseeing as possible the typical result, he wants or accepts the realization of the act described by law, and
Errata to the paragraph DOF 02-01-1994

A person acts negligently when he produces the typical result, which he did not foresee being foreseeable or foresaw trusting that it would not occur, by virtue of the violation of a duty of care, which he should and could observe according to the circumstances and personal conditions.
Errata to the article DOF 08-31-1931. Reformed DOF 01-13-1984, 01-10-1994

##### Article 10

Criminal liability does not pass from the person and property of the offenders, except in cases specified by law.

##### Article 11

When any member or representative of a legal person, or of a society, corporation or company of any kind, with the exception of State institutions, commits a crime with the means that such entities provide for that purpose, so that it results committed in the name or under the protection of the social representation or for its benefit, the judge may, in cases exclusively specified by law, decree in the sentence the suspension of the group or its dissolution, when he deems it necessary for public safety.

##### Article 11

Bis.- For the purposes of the provisions of Title X, Chapter II, of the National Code of Criminal Procedure, legal entities may be subject to one or more legal consequences when they have participated in the commission of the following offenses:
A. Those provided for in this Code:

I. Terrorism, provided for in articles 139 to 139 Ter and international terrorism provided for in articles 148 Bis to 148 Quáter;
II. Illicit use of facilities intended for air traffic, provided for in article 172 Bis;

III. Against health, provided for in articles 194 and 195, first paragraph;

IV. Corruption of persons under 18 years of age or of persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who do not have the capacity to resist it, provided for in article 201;
V. Influence peddling provided for in article 221;

VI. Bribery, provided for in articles 222, section II, and 222 bis;
VII. Counterfeiting and alteration of currency, provided for in articles 234, 236 and 237;

VIII. Against national consumption and wealth, provided for in article 254;

IX. Trafficking of minors or of persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act, provided for in article 366 Ter;
X. Habitual commercialization of stolen objects, provided for in article 368 Ter;

XI. Theft of vehicles, provided for in article 376 Bis and possession, commerce, trafficking of stolen vehicles and other conduct provided for in article 377;
XII. Fraud, provided for in article 388;

XIII. Concealment, provided for in article 400;

XIV. Operations with resources of illicit origin, provided for in article 400 Bis;
XV. Against the environment, provided for in articles 414, 415, 416, 418, 419 and 420;

XVI. Regarding copyright, provided for in article 424 Bis;

B. Offenses established in the following legal instruments:
I. Stockpiling and trafficking of weapons, provided for in articles 83 Bis and 84, of the Federal Law on Firearms and Explosives;
II. Trafficking of persons, provided for in article 159, of the Migration Law;
III. Trafficking of organs, provided for in articles 461, 462 and 462 Bis, of the General Health Law;
IV. Trafficking in persons, provided for in articles 10 to 38 of the General Law to Prevent, Punish and Eradicate Crimes in the Matter of Trafficking in Persons and for the Protection and Assistance to Victims of these Crimes;
V. Clandestine introduction of firearms that are not reserved for the exclusive use of the Army, Navy or Air Force, provided for in article 84 Bis, of the Federal Law on Firearms and Explosives;
VI. Of the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in the Matter of Kidnapping, Regulatory of section XXI of article 73 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, those provided for in articles 9, 10, 11 and 15;

VII. Smuggling and its equivalent, provided for in articles 102 and 105 of the Federal Tax Code;
VIII. Tax fraud and its equivalent, provided for in articles 108 and 109, of the Federal Tax Code;
VIII Bis. Of the Federal Tax Code, the offense provided for in article 113 Bis;
Fracción adicionada DOF 08-11-2019

IX. Of the Industrial Property Law, the offenses provided for in article 223;

X. Of the Law on Credit Institutions, those provided for in articles 111; 111 Bis; 112; 112 Bis; 112 Ter; 112 Quáter; 112 Quintus; 113 Bis and 113 Bis 3;
XI. Of the General Law on Negotiable Instruments and Credit Operations, those provided for in articles 432, 433 and 434;
XII. Of the General Law on Organizations and Auxiliary Credit Activities, those provided for in articles 96; 97; 98; 99; 100 and 101;
XIII. Of the Securities Market Law, those provided for in articles 373; 374; 375; 376; 381; 382; 383 and 385;
XIV. Of the Law on Retirement Savings Systems, those provided for in articles 103; 104 when the amount of the disposition of funds, securities or documents that they handle from workers by reason of their purpose exceeds three hundred fifty thousand days of general minimum wage in force in the Federal District; 105; 106 and 107 Bis 1;

XV. Of the Investment Funds Law, those provided for in articles 88 and 90;
XVI. Of the Credit Unions Law, those provided for in articles 121; 122; 125; 126 and 128;

XVII. Of the Law to Regulate the Activities of Savings and Loan Cooperative Societies, those provided for in articles 110; 111; 112; 114 and 116;
XVIII. Of the Popular Savings and Credit Law, those provided for in articles 136 Bis 7; 137; 138; 140 and 142;
XIX. Of the Commercial Bankruptcy Law, those provided for in articles 117 and 271;

XX. Those provided for in article 49 of the Federal Law for the Control of Chemical Substances Susceptible to Diversion for the Manufacture of Chemical Weapons;
XXI. Those provided for in articles 8, 9, 10, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 17, 18 and 19 of the Federal Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in the Matter of Hydrocarbons.
Fracción reformada DOF 01-06-2018

XXII. In other cases expressly provided for in applicable legislation.

For the purposes of article 422 of the National Code of Criminal Procedure, the following limits of punishability shall apply for the legal consequences for legal entities:
a) Suspension of activities, for a period of between six months to six years.

b) Closure of premises and establishments, for a period of between six months to six years.
c) Prohibition from carrying out in the future the activities in the exercise of which the offense was committed or participated in its commission, for a period of between six months to ten years.
d) Temporary disqualification consisting of the suspension of rights to participate directly or through an intermediary in contracting procedures or to enter into contracts regulated by the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector, as well as by the Law on Public Works and Services related thereto, for a period of between six months to six years.

e) Judicial intervention to safeguard the rights of workers or creditors for a period of between six months to six years.
The judicial intervention may affect the entirety of the organization or be limited to any of its facilities, sections or business units. The scope of the intervention and who will be in charge of it shall be determined exactly, as well as the deadlines within which monitoring reports must be made for the judicial body. The judicial intervention may be modified or suspended at any time following a report from the receiver and the Public Prosecutor. The receiver shall have the right to access all facilities and premises of the company or legal entity, as well as to receive any information deemed necessary for the exercise of their functions. Applicable legislation shall determine the aspects related to the functions of the receiver and their respective remuneration.

In all cases provided for in article 422 of the National Code of Criminal Procedure, the sanctions may be reduced by up to one quarter, if prior to the act attributed to them, the legal entities had a permanent control body, responsible for verifying compliance with applicable legal provisions to monitor internal crime prevention policies and have carried out before or after the act attributed to them, the reduction of the damage caused by the typical act.
Artículo adicionado DOF 17-06-2016

#### CHAPTER II

Attempt

##### Article 12

Punishable attempt exists when the resolution to commit a crime is externalized by partially or totally performing the executive acts that should produce the result, or by omitting those that should prevent it, if the result is not consummated due to causes beyond the agent's will.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 14-01-1985, 10-01-1994

To impose the penalty for attempt, the judge shall take into account, in addition to what is provided in article 52, the greater or lesser degree of proximity to the consummative moment of the crime.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 10-01-1994

If the subject spontaneously desists from the execution or prevents the consummation of the crime, no penalty or security measure shall be imposed with regard to it, without prejudice to applying the one corresponding to executed or omitted acts that in themselves constitute crimes.  
Paragraph added DOF 14-01-1985

#### CHAPTER III

Persons responsible for crimes

##### Article 13.- The following are authors or participants in a crime

Paragraph reformed DOF 10-01-1994
I.-	Those who agree upon or prepare its commission.  

II.-	Those who commit it themselves;  

III.-	Those who commit it jointly;  
IV.-	Those who carry it out by using another person;  

V.-	Those who intentionally induce another person to commit it;  
Section reformed DOF 10-01-1994

VI.-	Those who intentionally provide aid or assistance to another person for its commission;  
Section reformed DOF 10-01-1994

VII.-	Those who, after its execution, assist the offender in fulfillment of a promise made prior to the crime; and
VIII.- Those who, without prior agreement, intervene with others in its commission, when the result that each one produced cannot be determined.  
Section reformed DOF 10-01-1994

The authors or participants referred to in this article shall each be liable to the extent of their own culpability.  
Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994

For the subjects referred to in sections VI, VII and VIII, the punishability provided by article 64 bis of this Code shall apply.  
Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994 Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 09-03-1946, 13-01-1984

##### Article 14

If several offenders take part in the commission of a specific crime and one of them commits a different crime, without prior agreement with the others, all shall be liable for the commission of the new crime, unless the following requirements are met:  
I.- That the new crime does not serve as an adequate means to commit the principal one;  

II.- That the former is not a necessary or natural consequence of the latter, or of the agreed means;  

III.- That they had not known beforehand that the new crime was going to be committed; and
IV.- That they had not been present at the execution of the new crime, or that having been present, they had done everything in their power to prevent it.

#### CHAPTER IV

Causes of exclusion of crime
Chapter title reformed DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 15.- A crime is excluded when

I.-	The act is performed without the intervention of the agent's will;  

II.-	The nonexistence of any of the elements that make up the typical description of the crime in question is demonstrated;  
Section reformed DOF 18-05-1999

III.- One acts with the consent of the holder of the affected legal interest, provided that the following requirements are met:
a)	That the legal interest is available;  
b)	That the holder of the interest has the legal capacity to freely dispose of it; and

c)	That the consent is express or tacit and without any defect; or that the act is performed in circumstances such as to permit a well-founded presumption that, had the holder been consulted, he would have granted the same;  
IV.- A real, present or imminent aggression, and without right, is repelled in protection of one's own or another's legal interests, provided that there exists necessity of the defense and rationality of the means employed and there is no sufficient and immediate intentional provocation on the part of the person attacked or the person being defended.  
It shall be presumed as legitimate defense, unless proven otherwise, the act of causing harm to anyone who by any means attempts to enter, without right, the agent's home, that of his family, its dependencies, or those of any person he has the obligation to defend, the place where his own or another's property is located with respect to which the same obligation exists; or finds him in any of those places in circumstances such as to reveal the probability of an aggression;  
V.-  One acts out of the necessity to safeguard one's own or another's legal interest from a real, present or imminent danger, not intentionally caused by the agent, injuring another interest of lesser or equal value than the one safeguarded, provided that the danger is not avoidable by other means and the agent does not have the legal duty to face it;  

VI.- The action or omission is performed in fulfillment of a legal duty or in exercise of a right, provided that there exists rational necessity of the means employed to fulfill the duty or exercise the right, and that the latter is not performed with the sole purpose of harming another;  
VII.- At the moment of performing the typical act, the agent does not have the capacity to understand the unlawful character of the act or to conduct himself in accordance with that understanding, by virtue of suffering from mental disorder or retarded intellectual development, unless the agent had provoked his mental disorder intentionally or negligently, in which case he shall be liable for the typical result provided that he had foreseen it or it was foreseeable to him.  
When the capacity referred to in the preceding paragraph is only considerably diminished, the provisions of article 69 bis of this Code shall apply.  
VIII.- The action or omission is performed under an invincible error;  

A)	Regarding any of the essential elements that make up the criminal type; or
B)	Regarding the unlawfulness of the conduct, whether because the subject is unaware of the existence of the law or its scope, or because he believes that his conduct is justified.  

If the errors referred to in the preceding subsections are vincible, the provisions of article 66 of this Code shall apply;  
IX.- Given the circumstances that concur in the performance of an unlawful conduct, a conduct different from the one performed is not rationally demandable of the agent, by virtue of his not having been able to determine himself to act in accordance with law; or
X.-	The typical result is produced by fortuitous event.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 05-01-1955, 08-03-1968, 13-01-1984, 23-12-1985, 10-01-1994

##### Article 16

In cases of excess of legitimate defense or excess in any other cause of justification, one-fourth of the sanction corresponding to the crime in question shall be imposed, the imputation on an intentional basis remaining in effect.  
Article reformed DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994, 17-06-2016

##### Article 17

The causes of exclusion of crime shall be investigated and resolved ex officio or at the request of a party, at any stage of the proceeding.  
Article reformed DOF 10-01-1994

#### CHAPTER V

Concurrence of Crimes
Chapter title reformed DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 18

Ideal concurrence exists when several crimes are committed with a single conduct. Real concurrence exists when several crimes are committed with a plurality of conducts.  
Article reformed DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 19.- There is no concurrence when the conducts constitute a continuing crime

Article reformed DOF 13-01-1984

#### CHAPTER VI

Recidivism

##### Article 20

There is recidivism: whenever a person convicted by final judgment issued by any court of the Republic or abroad commits a new crime, if a term equal to that of the prescription of the penalty has not elapsed since the completion of the sentence or since the pardon thereof, except for the exceptions established by law.  
A sentence served abroad shall be taken into account if it arose from a crime that has such character in this Code or special laws.

##### Article 21

If the recidivist in the same type of offenses commits a new crime arising from the same passion or vicious inclination, he shall be considered a habitual offender, provided that the three offenses have been committed within a period not exceeding ten years.

##### Article 22

The provisions of the preceding articles include cases in which only one of the crimes, or all of them, remain at any stage of attempt, whatever the capacity in which the responsible party intervenes.

##### Article 23

The preceding articles shall not apply in the case of political crimes and when the agent has been pardoned for being innocent.  
SECOND TITLE

#### CHAPTER I

Penalties and security measures

##### Article 24.- The penalties and security measures are

1.-  Imprisonment.  
2.-  Treatment in freedom, semi-freedom and work in favor of the community.  
Clause repealed DOF 12-05-1938. Added DOF 24-03-1944. Repealed DOF 05-01-1948. Added DOF 13-01-1984

3.- Confinement or treatment in freedom of persons not criminally liable and of those who have the habit or need to consume narcotics or psychotropic substances.  
Clause amended DOF 31-12-1974, 13-01-1984

4.-  Confinement.  
5.-  Prohibition from going to a determined place.  

6.-  Pecuniary sanction.  

7.-  (Repealed).  
Clause repealed DOF 13-01-1984

8.-  Confiscation of instruments, objects and products of the crime
Clause amended DOF 13-01-1984, 23-12-1985
9.-  Admonition.  

10.- Warning.  

11.- Bond not to offend.  
12.- Suspension or deprivation of rights.  

13.- Disqualification, removal or suspension from functions or employment.  
Clause amended DOF 10-02-1945
14.- Special publication of judgment.  

15.- Surveillance by the authority.  
Clause amended DOF 13-01-1984

16.- Suspension or dissolution of companies.  

17.- Protective measures for minors.  
18.- Confiscation of assets corresponding to illicit enrichment.  
Clause added DOF 05-01-1983

19. The placement of location and surveillance devices.  
Clause added DOF 30-11-2010

And others established by the laws.

#### CHAPTER II

Imprisonment

##### Article 25

Imprisonment consists of the penalty of deprivation of personal liberty. Its duration shall be from three days to sixty years, and an additional penalty to the maximum limit may only be imposed when a new crime is committed while in confinement. It shall be served in penitentiary centers, in accordance with the legislation on the matter and in compliance with the respective judicial decision.  
The precautionary measure of preventive detention shall be counted toward the completion of the imposed sentence as well as those that may be imposed in other cases, even if they concerned acts prior to entry into prison. In this case, the sentences shall be served simultaneously.  

The maximum limit of the duration of the penalty of deprivation of liberty up to 60 years contemplated in this article is not applicable to crimes that are sanctioned in accordance with what is stipulated in other laws.  
Paragraph amended DOF 17-11-2017 Article amended DOF 12-05-1938, 05-01-1948, 05-01-1955, 03-01-1989, 17-05-1999, 26-05-2004, 03-06-2014, 17-06-2016

##### Article 26

Defendants subject to preventive detention and political prisoners shall be confined in special establishments or departments.

#### CHAPTER III

Treatment in freedom, semi-release and work in favor of the community
Chapter heading amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 27

Treatment in freedom of persons criminally liable consisting of the application of labor, educational and curative measures, as applicable, authorized by law and conducive to the social reintegration of the sentenced person, under the guidance and care of the executing authority. Its duration may not exceed that corresponding to the substituted prison sentence.  
Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016

Semi-freedom implies alternation of periods of deprivation of liberty and treatment in freedom. It shall be applied, according to the circumstances of the case, in the following manner: release during the work or educational week, with confinement on weekends, weekend release, with confinement during the rest of the week; or daytime release, with nighttime confinement. The duration of semi-freedom may not exceed that corresponding to the substituted prison sentence.  
Work in favor of the community consists of the provision of unremunerated services in public educational or social assistance institutions or in private charitable institutions. This work shall be carried out in shifts during periods other than the working hours that represent the source of income for the subsistence of the subject and his family, without exceeding the overtime shift determined by labor law and under the guidance and supervision of the executing authority.  

Work in favor of the community may be an autonomous penalty or a substitute for imprisonment or fine.  
Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994

Each day of imprisonment shall be substituted by one day of work in favor of the community.  

The length of the work shift shall be set by the judge taking into account the circumstances of the case.  

Under no circumstances shall this work be carried out in a manner that is degrading or humiliating to the sentenced person.  
Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016 Article repealed DOF 12-05-1938. Added DOF 24-03-1944. Repealed DOF 05-01-1948. Added DOF 13-01-1984

#### CHAPTER IV

Confinement

##### Article 28

Confinement consists of the obligation to reside in a determined place and not leave it. The Executive shall designate the place, reconciling the requirements of public tranquility with the health and needs of the convicted person. When political crimes are involved, the designation shall be made by the judge who issues the sentence.

#### CHAPTER V

Pecuniary sanction

##### Article 29.- Pecuniary sanction comprises the fine and reparation of damage

The fine consists of the payment of a sum of money to the State, which shall be fixed by day-fines, which may not exceed one thousand, except in cases specified by the law itself. The day-fine is equivalent to the net daily income of the convicted person at the time of committing the offense, taking into account all their income.  
Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994, 23-08-2005

For the purposes of this Code, the lower limit of the day-fine shall be equivalent to the minimum daily wage in force in the place where the offense was committed. With regard to continuing offenses, the minimum wage in force at the time of completion of the last conduct shall be considered. For permanent offenses, the minimum wage in force at the time the commission ceased shall be considered.  
When it is proven that the convicted person cannot pay the fine or can only cover part of it, the judicial authority may substitute it, totally or partially, with community service.  
Each day of work shall settle one day-fine. When substitution of the fine for community service is not possible or convenient, the judicial authority may place the convicted person on probation, which shall not exceed the number of day-fines substituted.  
If the convicted person refuses without justified cause to cover the amount of the fine, the State shall demand it through the coercive economic procedure.  

At any time the amount of the fine may be covered, deducting from it the proportional part corresponding to the days of community service performed, or to the time of imprisonment that the convicted person has served in the case of a fine substituting a custodial sentence, in which case the equivalence shall be at the rate of one day-fine per day of imprisonment.  
Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016 Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 30

Reparation of damage must be comprehensive, adequate, effective, efficient, proportional to the severity of the damage caused and the harm suffered, and shall include at least:  
I.	Restitution of the thing obtained by the offense and if not possible, payment of its price, at its updated value;  

II.	Compensation for material and moral damage caused, including medical and psychological care, social services and rehabilitation or curative treatments necessary for the recovery of health, that the victim has required or requires, as a consequence of the offense. In cases of offenses against free development of personality, liberty and normal psychosexual development and mental health, as well as family violence, it shall also include payment for psychotherapeutic treatments that are necessary for the victim;  

III.	Compensation for damages occasioned;  
IV.	Payment for loss of economic income and loss of profits, for which the salary that the victim had at the time of suffering the offense shall be taken as a basis and in case of not having that information, it shall be according to the minimum wage in force in the place where the act occurred;  
V.	The cost of loss of opportunities, particularly employment, education and social benefits, according to their circumstances;  
VI.	A declaration restoring the dignity and reputation of the victim, through electronic or written means;  
VII.	Public apology, acceptance of responsibility, as well as guarantee of non-repetition, when the offense is committed by public servants.  

The means for rehabilitation must be as complete as possible, and must allow the victim to participate fully in public, private and social life.  
Article amended DOF 05-01-1983, 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994, 30-12-1997, 19-08-2010, 14-06-2012

##### Article 30

Bis.- The following have the right to reparation of damage in the following order: 1st. The offended party; 2nd. In case of death of the offended party, the surviving spouse or cohabitant, and minor children; in their absence, other descendants and ascendants who were economically dependent on them at the time of death.  
Article added DOF 21-01-1991

##### Article 31

Reparation shall be fixed by judges, according to the damage that must be repaired, based on the evidence obtained in the proceedings and the harm caused to the victim or offended party of the offense.  
Paragraph amended DOF 13-01-1984, 14-06-2012

For cases of reparation of damage caused by offenses due to negligence, the Federal Executive shall regulate, without prejudice to the resolution issued by the judicial authority, the manner in which, administratively, such reparation must be guaranteed through special insurance.

##### Article 31

Bis. In every criminal proceeding, the Public Prosecutor shall be obligated to request, ex officio, the conviction regarding reparation of damage and the judge is obligated to resolve accordingly.  
Failure to comply with this provision shall be sanctioned in accordance with the provisions of section VII and the second paragraph of article 225 of this Code.  
At all times, the victim must be informed about the reparation of damage.  
Article added DOF 10-01-1994. Amended DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 32.- The following are obligated to repair damage under the terms of article 29

I.-	Ascendants, for offenses of their descendants who are under their parental authority:

II.-	Guardians and custodians, for offenses of incapacitated persons who are under their authority;  

III.- Directors of boarding schools or workshops, who receive in their establishment students or apprentices under 16 years of age, for offenses they commit during the time they are under their care;  

IV.- Owners, companies or managers of businesses or commercial establishments of any kind, for offenses committed by their workers, day laborers, employees, domestic servants and artisans, by reason of and in the performance of their service;  
V.-  Societies or associations, for offenses of their partners or managing directors, under the same terms in which, according to the laws, they are liable for other obligations that the latter contract.  
The marital partnership is excepted from this rule, since, in any case, each spouse shall be liable with their own property for reparation of the damage they cause, and
VI.	Any institution, association, organization or group of a religious, cultural, sports, educational, recreational or any other nature, whose employees, members, participants, auxiliaries or assistants who carry out their activities voluntarily or for remuneration, and
Section added DOF 19-08-2010

VII.- The State, jointly and severally, for intentional offenses of its public servants committed by reason of the exercise of their functions, and subsidiarily when those offenses are negligent.  
Section amended DOF 10-01-1994. Renumbered DOF 19-08-2010

##### Article 33

The obligation to pay the pecuniary sanction takes precedence over any others contracted after the offense, with the exception of those relating to alimony and labor relations.  
Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 34

The reparation of damage arising from the crime that must be made by the accused, defendant and convicted person, has the character of a public penalty and shall be demanded ex officio by the Public Prosecutor's Office. The victim, the legal advisor and the offended party or their successors in interest may provide to the Public Prosecutor's Office or to the jurisdictional body as applicable, the evidentiary data they have to demonstrate the origin and amount of said reparation, in the terms provided by the National Code of Criminal Procedures.  
Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994, 17-06-2016

Non-compliance by the authorities with the obligation referred to in the preceding paragraph shall be sanctioned with a fine of thirty to forty days of minimum wage.  
Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994

When said reparation must be demanded from a third party, it shall have the character of civil liability and shall be processed in the form of an incident, in the terms established by the Code of Criminal Procedures itself.  
Whoever considers themselves entitled to reparation of damage, which cannot be obtained before the jurisdictional body, by virtue of the non-exercise of the action or the abstention from investigating by the Public Prosecutor's Office, dismissal or acquittal, may resort to the civil or administrative route in the terms of the corresponding legislation.  
Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016 Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 35

The amount of the pecuniary sanction shall be distributed: between the State and the offended party; to the former shall be applied the amount of the fine, and to the latter that of the reparation.  

If it is not possible to make the entire amount of the pecuniary sanction effective, the reparation of damage shall be covered preferentially, and where applicable, pro rata among the offended parties.  
If the offended party renounces the reparation, the amount thereof shall be applied to the State.  

In the event that a precautionary measure has been imposed to guarantee the reparation of damage, this shall be made effective in favor of the victim or offended party when the sentence condemning to repair the damage becomes final.  
Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994, 17-06-2016 Amendment DOF 17-06-2016: Repealed from the article the then fifth paragraph (previously added DOF 10-01-1994)

##### Article 36

When several persons commit the crime, the judge shall set the fine for each of the offenders, according to their participation in the criminal act and their economic conditions; and as to the reparation of damage, the debt shall be considered as joint and several.

##### Article 37

The reparation of damage shall be ordered to be made effective, in the same manner as the fine. Once the sentence imposing such reparation becomes final, the court that has pronounced it shall immediately send a certified copy thereof to the competent tax authority and the latter, within three days following receipt of said copy, shall initiate the economic-coercive procedure, notifying thereof the person in whose favor it has been decreed, or their legal representative.  
Article amended DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 38

If the pecuniary liability cannot be covered with the assets of the responsible party or with the product of their work in prison, the released convicted person shall remain subject to the obligation to pay the remaining part.  
Article amended DOF 17-06-2016

##### Article 39

The judge, taking into account the amount of the damage and the economic situation of the obligated party, may set deadlines for the payment of the reparation thereof, which as a whole shall not exceed one year, and may for this purpose require a guarantee if deemed convenient.  

The authority responsible for collecting the fine may set deadlines for the payment thereof, taking into account the circumstances of the case.  
Article amended DOF 29-12-1981. Errata DOF 13-01-1982. Amended DOF 13-01-1984

#### CHAPTER VI

Forfeiture of Instruments, Objects and Products of the Crime
Chapter title amended DOF 15-01-1951, 13-01-1984, 23-12-1985

##### Article 40

The jurisdictional body by means of a sentence in the corresponding criminal proceeding, may decree the forfeiture of assets that are instruments, objects or products of the crime, with the exception of those that have been abandoned in the terms of the applicable provisions or with respect to those on which the declaration of extinction of ownership has been resolved.  
In the event that the product, instruments or objects of the criminal act have disappeared or are not located for reasons attributable to the accused or convicted person, the forfeiture of assets owned by the accused or convicted person(s) may be decreed, as well as those with respect to which they act as owners or beneficial owners or controlling beneficiary, whose value is equivalent to said product, without prejudice to the applicable provisions on extinction of ownership.  

If they belong to a third party, they shall only be forfeited when the third party who has them in their possession or has acquired them under any title, is in any of the cases referred to in articles 139 Quáter, 400 or 400 bis of this Code, regardless of the legal nature of said third party owner or possessor and of the relationship that the former has with the accused or convicted person, as applicable. The

competent authorities shall proceed to the immediate seizure of the assets that could be subject to forfeiture, during the proceeding. Action shall be taken in the terms provided by this paragraph whatever the nature of the instruments, objects or products of the crime.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 08-05-1945, 15-01-1951, 13-01-1984, 23-12-1985, 08-02-1999, 17-05-1999,
17-06-2016

##### Article 41

Objects or valuables that are at the disposal of the investigating authorities or the judicial authorities, that have not been forfeited and that are not collected by whoever has the right to do so, within a period of ninety calendar days, counted from the notification to the interested party, shall be sold at public auction and the proceeds of the sale shall be applied to whoever has the right to receive it. If notified, they do not appear within six months following the date of notification, the proceeds of the sale shall be allocated to the improvement of the administration of justice, after deductions of the expenses incurred.  

In the case of assets that are at the disposal of the authority, that should not be destroyed and that cannot be preserved or are costly to maintain, their immediate sale at public auction shall be proceeded with, and the proceeds shall be left at the disposal of whoever has the right thereto for a period of six months from the notification made to them, after which it shall be applied to the improvement of the administration of justice.  
Article amended DOF 15-01-1951, 13-01-1965, 23-12-1974, 31-12-1974, 13-01-1984

#### CHAPTER VII

Admonition

##### Article 42

Admonition consists: in the warning that the judge addresses to the accused, making them see the consequences of the crime they committed, urging them to reform and warning them that a greater sanction will be imposed if they reoffend.  
This admonition shall be made in public or in private, as may seem prudent to the judge.

#### CHAPTER VIII

Warning and Bond Not to Offend

##### Article 43

Warning consists in the admonition that the judge makes to a person, when they have committed a crime and there is founded fear that they are disposed to commit a new crime, either by their attitude or by threats, that in case of committing it, they will be considered a repeat offender.  
Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 44

When the judge deems that the warning is not sufficient, they shall also require the accused to post a bond not to offend, or other adequate guarantee, at the judge's discretion.  
Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

#### CHAPTER IX

Suspension of Rights

##### Article 45.- The suspension of rights is of two classes

I.- That which by operation of law results from a sanction as a necessary consequence thereof, and

II.- That which by formal judgment is imposed as a sanction.

In the first case, the suspension begins and concludes with the sanction of which it is a consequence.

In the second case, if the suspension is imposed with another sanction depriving liberty, it shall begin upon the termination of the latter and its duration shall be that specified in the judgment.

##### Article 46

The penalty of imprisonment produces the suspension of political rights and those of guardianship, curatorship, being an attorney-in-fact, defender, executor, expert witness, depositary or judicial receiver, trustee or receiver in bankruptcy, arbitrator, arbitrator or representative of absentees. The suspension shall begin from the time the respective judgment becomes final and shall last for the entire duration of the sentence.

#### CHAPTER X

Special publication of judgment

##### Article 47

The special publication of judgment consists of the total or partial insertion thereof, in one or two newspapers circulating in the locality. The judge shall choose the newspapers and shall determine the manner in which the publication must be made.

The publication of the judgment shall be made at the expense of the offender, of the victim if the latter so requests, or of the State if the judge deems it necessary.

##### Article 48

The judge may, at the request and expense of the victim, order the publication of the judgment in a different entity or in some other newspaper.

##### Article 49

The publication of judgment shall also be ordered by way of reparation and at the request of the interested party, when the latter is acquitted, the imputed act does not constitute an offense, or he did not commit it.

##### Article 50

If the offense for which the publication of judgment was imposed was committed by means of the press, in addition to the publication referred to in the preceding articles, it shall also be made in the newspaper used to commit the offense, with the same type of letter, same color of ink and in the same place.

#### CHAPTER XI

Surveillance by the authority
Chapter added DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 50

Bis.- When the judgment determines restriction of liberty or rights, or conditional suspension of the execution of the judgment, the judge shall order surveillance by the authority over the sentenced person, which shall have the same duration as that corresponding to the sanction imposed.

The surveillance shall consist of exercising over the sentenced person observation and guidance of his conduct by specialized personnel dependent on the executing authority, for social reintegration.

Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016 Article added DOF 13-01-1984
TITLE THREE
Application of Sanctions

#### CHAPTER I

General rules

##### Article 51

Within the limits established by law, judges and courts shall apply the sanctions established for each offense, taking into account the external circumstances of execution and the

peculiarities of the offender; particularly when dealing with indigenous persons, the uses and customs of the peoples and communities to which they belong shall be considered.

Paragraph amended DOF 30-12-1991, 18-12-2002

In the cases of articles 60, section VI, 61, 63, 64, 64 bis and 65 and in any others in which this Code provides penalties in proportion to those provided for the intentional consummated offense, the applicable punishability is, for all legal purposes, that which results from the increase or decrease, as applicable, of the minimum and maximum terms of the penalty provided for the latter. When it concerns imprisonment, the minimum penalty shall never be less than three days.

Paragraph added DOF 14-01-1985

When an intentional offense is committed against any journalist, person or facility with the intention of affecting, limiting or impairing the right to information or the freedoms of expression or of the press, the penalty established for such offense shall be increased by up to one third.

Paragraph added DOF 03-05-2013

In the preceding case, the penalty shall be increased by up to one half when in addition the offense is committed by a public servant in the exercise of his functions or the victim is a woman and gender-based reasons concur in the commission of the offense, in accordance with what the laws on the matter establish.

Paragraph added DOF 03-05-2013

##### Article 52

The judge shall set the penalties and security measures that he deems just and appropriate within the limits specified for each offense, based on the gravity of the unlawful act, the quality and specific condition of the victim or injured party and the degree of culpability of the agent, taking into account:
Paragraph amended DOF 19-08-2010

I.- The magnitude of the damage caused to the legal interest or of the danger to which it may have been exposed;

II.- The nature of the action or omission and of the means employed to execute it;

III.- The circumstances of time, place, manner or occasion of the act committed;

IV.- The form and degree of intervention of the agent in the commission of the offense;
Section amended DOF 19-08-2010

V.- The age, education, learning, customs, social and economic conditions of the subject, as well as the motives that impelled or determined him to commit the offense. When the accused belongs to any indigenous people or community, their uses and customs shall also be taken into account;
Section amended DOF 18-12-2002

VI.- The subsequent behavior of the accused in relation to the offense committed; and
VII.- The other special and personal conditions in which the agent found himself at the time of the commission of the offense, provided that they are relevant to determine the possibility of having adjusted his conduct to the requirements of the norm.

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 05-01-1983, 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994

##### Article 53

The increase in gravity arising from particular circumstances of the victim is not imputable to the accused, if he was inculpably unaware of them when committing the offense.

##### Article 54

The increase or decrease of the penalty, based on the qualities, personal relationships or subjective circumstances of the author of an offense, are not applicable to the other subjects who intervened therein.

Those based on objective circumstances are applicable, if the other subjects have knowledge of them.

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 55

In the event that the accused is a person over seventy years of age or affected by a serious or terminal illness, the jurisdictional body may order that pretrial detention be executed at the domicile of the accused person or, as the case may be, in a medical or geriatric center, under the applicable precautionary measures, in all cases the assessment by the judge shall be supported by expert opinions. The review of the precautionary measure may be promoted by the parties who shall also offer evidence for such purpose.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

Likewise, the provisions of the preceding paragraph shall apply when dealing with pregnant women, or mothers during lactation.  
Párrafo adicionado DOF 17-06-2016

Those who are accused of crimes that warrant mandatory pretrial detention or at the discretion of the Control Judge may evade the action of justice or manifest conduct that makes their social risk presumable shall not enjoy the prerogative provided in the two preceding paragraphs, nor shall those accused of the conducts provided in the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in the Matter of Kidnapping, Regulatory of fraction XXI of article 73 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 30-11-2010, 17-06-2016

Once the final judgment has been issued, the penalty may be substituted by a security measure, at the discretion of the judge or court that imposes it ex officio or at the request of a party, when the active subject has suffered serious consequences to his person, or due to his senility or his precarious state of health, it would be notoriously unnecessary to serve said penalty, with the exception of those sentenced for the conducts provided in article 9 of the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in the Matter of Kidnapping, Regulatory of fraction XXI of article 73 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, who in all cases must serve the imposed penalty.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 30-11-2010 Reforma DOF 17-06-2016: Derogó del artículo los entonces párrafos cuarto y sexto Artículo reformado DOF 13-01-1984, 30-12-1991, 26-05-2004

##### Article 56

The competent jurisdictional authority shall apply the most favorable law ex officio. When the subject has been sentenced to the minimum term or the maximum term of the penalty provided and the reform decreases said term, the most favorable law shall apply. When the subject has been sentenced to a penalty between the minimum term and the maximum term, the reduction resulting in the arithmetic mean term according to the new norm shall apply.  
Artículo reformado DOF 23-12-1985, 17-06-2016

##### Article 57.- (Repealed)

Artículo derogado DOF 23-12-1985

##### Article 58.- (Repealed)

Artículo derogado DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 59.- (Repealed)

Artículo derogado DOF 14-01-1985

##### Article 59 Bis.- Repealed

Artículo adicionado DOF 13-01-1984. Derogado DOF 10-01-1994

#### CHAPTER II

Application of sanctions to culpable crimes
Denominación del Capítulo reformada DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994

##### Article 60

In cases of culpable crimes, up to one-fourth of the penalties and security measures assigned by law to the basic type of intentional crime shall be imposed, with the exception of those for which the law establishes a specific penalty. In addition, suspension of up to three years of rights to exercise a profession, trade, authorization, license or permit shall be imposed, as applicable.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994. Fe de erratas DOF 01-02-1994. Reformado DOF 26-12-2013

Sanctions for culpable crimes shall only be imposed in relation to the crimes provided in the following articles: 150, 167, fraction VI, 169, 199 Bis, 289, second part, 290, 291, 292, 293, 302, 307, 323, 397, 399, 414, first paragraph and third in its result hypothesis, 415, fractions I and II and last paragraph in its result hypothesis, 416, 420, fractions I, II, III and V, and 420 Bis, fractions I, II and IV of this Code.  
Párrafo adicionado DOF 10-01-1994. Fe de erratas DOF 01-02-1994. Reformado DOF 06-02-2002

When as a consequence of culpable acts or omissions, classified as serious, that are attributable to personnel providing services in a railway, aeronautical, shipping or any other federal or local public service transportation company, homicides of two or more persons are caused, the penalty shall be five to twenty years of imprisonment, removal from employment, position or commission and disqualification from obtaining others of the same nature. The same penalty shall be imposed when it involves school service transportation.  
Párrafo adicionado DOF 10-01-1994. Fe de erratas DOF 01-02-1994

The classification of the seriousness of the culpability is left to the prudent discretion of the judge, who must take into consideration the general circumstances indicated in article 52, and the following special ones:
Párrafo reformado DOF 10-01-1994

I.-	The greater or lesser ease of foreseeing and avoiding the damage that resulted;  

II.-	The duty of care of the accused that is required of him by the circumstances and personal conditions that the trade or activity he performs impose upon him;  
Fracción reformada DOF 10-01-1994

III.-	Whether the accused has previously committed crimes in similar circumstances;  

IV.-	Whether he had time to act with the necessary reflection and care, and
V.- The state of the equipment, tracks and other conditions of mechanical operation, in the case of infractions committed in the services of transportation companies, and in general, by vehicle drivers.  

VI.-	Repealed.  

Fracción adicionada DOF 13-01-1984. Derogada DOF 10-01-1994 Artículo reformado DOF 10-02-1945, 05-01-1955

##### Article 61

In the cases referred to in the first part of the first paragraph of the preceding article, reparation of the damage is excepted. Whenever the intentional crime corresponds to an alternative sanction that includes a non-custodial penalty, that situation shall benefit the person responsible for the culpable crime.  
Artículo reformado DOF 10-02-1945, 15-01-1951, 10-01-1994

##### Article 62

When damage to another's property not exceeding the equivalent of one hundred times the minimum wage is caused by culpability, it shall be sanctioned with a fine up to the value of the damage caused,

plus the reparation thereof. The same sanction shall apply when the culpable crime is caused by reason of vehicle traffic regardless of the value of the damage.  
When injuries of any nature are caused by culpability and by reason of vehicle traffic, proceedings shall only be initiated at the request of the offended party or his legitimate representative, provided that the driver was not in a state of intoxication or under the influence of narcotics, psychotropics or any other substance that produces similar effects and has not abandoned the victim.  
Artículo reformado DOF 24-03-1944, 15-01-1951, 05-01-1955, 19-03-1971, 13-01-1984, 19-11-1986, 10-01-1994

#### CHAPTER III

Application of sanctions in case of attempt

##### Article 63

The person responsible for a punishable attempt shall be subject, at the judge's discretion and taking into consideration the provisions of articles 12 and 52, to up to two-thirds of the sanction that should have been imposed had the crime they intended to commit been consummated, unless otherwise provided.  
Errata to paragraph DOF 01-02-1994

In cases of attempt where it is not possible to determine the harm that was intended to be caused, when such harm is determinative for the correct typical adequacy, up to half of the sanction indicated in the preceding paragraph shall be applied.  
In cases of punishable attempt of a serious crime so classified by law, the judicial authority shall impose a prison sentence that shall not be less than the minimum penalty and may reach up to two-thirds of the maximum sanction provided for the consummated crime.  
Paragraph added DOF 13-05-1996 Article amended DOF 15-01-1951, 10-01-1994

#### CHAPTER IV

Application of sanctions in case of concurrence, continuing crime, complicity, recidivism and vincible error
Chapter title amended DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994

##### Article 64

In case of ideal concurrence, the sanctions corresponding to the crime that merits the greatest penalty shall be imposed, which may be increased without exceeding half of the maximum duration of the penalties corresponding to the remaining crimes, provided that the applicable sanctions are of the same nature; when they are of a different nature, the legal consequences indicated for the remaining crimes may be imposed, with the exception of cases in which one of the crimes for which there is ideal concurrence is among those contemplated in the General Law to Prevent and Punish crimes related to Kidnapping, and the General Law to Prevent, Punish and Eradicate Crimes related to Trafficking in Persons and for the Protection and Assistance to Victims of these Crimes, both regulatory of section XXI of article 73 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, in which cases the rules of real concurrence shall apply.  

In case of real concurrence, the sanction of the most serious crime shall be imposed, which may be increased with the penalties that the law contemplates for each of the remaining crimes, without exceeding the maximums indicated in Title Two of Book One. If the penalties were imposed in the same proceeding or in different ones, but if the facts are connected or similar, or one derived from the other, in any case the penalties must be counted from the moment of deprivation of liberty for the first crime.  
In case of continuing crime, the criminal sanction shall be increased up to one-half of that corresponding to the maximum of the crime committed, without exceeding the maximum indicated in Title Two of Book One.  

Article amended DOF 19-12-1964, 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994, 13-05-1996, 17-05-1999, 26-05-2004, 30-11-2010, 17-06-2016

##### Article 64

Bis.- In the cases provided for by sections VI, VII and VIII of article 13, a penalty of up to three-quarters of that corresponding to the crime in question shall be imposed and, where applicable, according to the respective modality.  
Article added DOF 13-01-1984. Amended DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 65

The recidivism referred to in article 20 shall be taken into account for the judicial individualization of the penalty, as well as for the granting or not of the benefits or criminal substitutes that the law provides.  
In the event that the accused for any intentional crime classified by law as serious or that warrants ex officio preventive detention, as applicable, is a recidivist for crimes of such nature, the applicable sanction for the new crime committed shall be increased by two-thirds and up to one time more than the maximum penalty provided for it, without exceeding the maximum indicated in Title Two of Book One.  
Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016 Amendment DOF 17-05-1999: Repealed from the article the then third paragraph Article amended DOF 12-05-1938, 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994, 13-05-1996

##### Article 66

In the event that the error referred to in subsection a) of section VIII of article 15 is vincible, the punishability of the negligent crime shall be imposed if the act in question admits such form of commission. If the vincible error is that provided for in subsection b) of said section, the penalty shall be up to one-third of the crime in question.  
Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 12-05-1938, 24-03-1944, 05-01-1948, 10-01-1994

#### CHAPTER V

Treatment of persons not criminally responsible and of those who have the habit or need to consume narcotics or psychotropic substances, in confinement or at liberty
Chapter title amended DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1986

##### Article 67

In the case of persons not criminally responsible, the judge shall order the applicable treatment measure in confinement or at liberty, following the corresponding procedure.  
If it involves confinement, the person not criminally responsible shall be confined in the corresponding institution for treatment.  
In the event that the convicted person has the habit or need to consume narcotics or psychotropic substances, the judge shall also order the appropriate treatment by the competent health authority or another medical service under its supervision, independently of the execution of the penalty imposed for the crime committed.  
Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1986 Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 68

Persons not criminally responsible may be delivered by the judicial or executing authority, as applicable, to those who are legally responsible for their care, provided that they undertake to take adequate measures for their treatment and supervision, guaranteeing, by any means and to the satisfaction of the aforementioned authorities, the fulfillment of the obligations undertaken.  
The executing authority may resolve on the modification or conclusion of the measure, provisionally or definitively, considering the needs of the treatment, which shall be accredited through periodic reviews, with the frequency and characteristics of the case.  
Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 69

In no case shall the treatment measure imposed by the criminal judge exceed the duration corresponding to the maximum penalty applicable to the crime. If, upon conclusion of this time, the executing authority considers that the subject continues to need treatment, it shall place them at the disposal of the health authorities to proceed in accordance with applicable laws.  
Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 69

Bis.- If the capacity of the perpetrator to understand the unlawful character of the act or to determine themselves in accordance with that understanding is only diminished by the causes indicated in section VII of article 15 of this Code, in the judge's judgment, as appropriate, up to two-thirds of the penalty that would correspond to the crime committed shall be imposed, or the security measure referred to in article 67, or both, if necessary, taking into account the degree of impairment of the perpetrator's criminal responsibility.  
Article added DOF 10-01-1994

#### CHAPTER VI

Substitution and commutation of sanctions

##### Article 70

Imprisonment may be substituted, at the judge's discretion, considering the provisions of articles 51 and 52 in the following terms:  
I. By community service or semi-liberty, when the imposed sentence does not exceed four years;  
Section reformed DOF 13-05-1996
II. By treatment in liberty, if the imprisonment does not exceed three years, or
Section reformed DOF 13-05-1996

III. By fine, if the imprisonment does not exceed two years.  
Section reformed DOF 13-05-1996

The substitution may not be applied to anyone who has previously been convicted in a final judgment for an intentional crime prosecuted ex officio. Nor shall it apply to anyone convicted of any of the crimes indicated in section I of article 85 of this Code.  
Paragraph added DOF 13-05-1996. Reformed DOF 31-12-1998, 17-05-1999 Reform DOF 10-01-1994: Repealed from the article the then second paragraph
Article repealed DOF 12-05-1938. Added DOF 24-03-1944. Repealed DOF 05-01-1948. Added DOF 13-01-1984. Reformed DOF
30-12-1991

##### Article 71

The judge shall revoke the substitution and order the execution of the imposed prison sentence when the convicted person fails to comply with the conditions that were established for such purpose, unless the judge deems it appropriate to warn him that if a new fault is incurred, the substituted sanction will be enforced or when the convicted person is sentenced for another crime. If the new crime is negligent, the judge shall decide whether the substituted sentence should be applied.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 10-01-1994. Errata DOF 01-02-1994

In case the substituted prison sentence is enforced, the time during which the convicted person had served the substitute sanction shall be taken into account.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 17-06-2016 Article repealed DOF 12-05-1938. Added DOF 24-03-1944. Repealed DOF 05-01-1948. Added DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 72

In case a guarantor has been appointed for the fulfillment of the duties inherent to the substitution of sanctions, the obligation of the former shall conclude upon the extinction of the imposed sentence. When the guarantor has well-founded reasons not to continue in his performance, he shall present them to the judge, so that the latter, if he deems them just, may require the convicted person to present a new guarantor within the period that shall be prudently fixed, with the warning that the sanction will be enforced if he does not do so. In case of

death or insolvency of the guarantor, the convicted person must bring the fact to the judge's attention, for the purpose and under the warning expressed in the preceding paragraph, in the terms of section VI of article 90.  
Article repealed DOF 12-05-1938. Added DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 73

The Executive, in the case of political crimes, may commute sanctions after they have been imposed in an irrevocable sentence, according to the following rules:  
I.- When the imposed sanction is imprisonment, it shall be commuted to confinement for a term equal to two-thirds of the term that the imprisonment should last, and
II.- If it is confinement, it shall be commuted to a fine, at the rate of one day of the former for one day of fine.  
Section reformed DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 74

The convicted person who considers that at the time of sentencing he met the conditions for the benefit of substitution or commutation of the sanction and that due to inadvertence on his part or the judge's it was not granted to him, may request before the latter that it be granted, opening the respective incident in the terms of section X of article 90.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 17-06-2016

In all cases in which the substitution or commutation of the sentence is appropriate, when calculating the substitute sanction, in addition to what is established in the last paragraph of article 29 of this Code, the time during which the convicted person suffered preventive detention shall be deducted.  
Paragraph added DOF 30-12-1991 Article reformed DOF 19-03-1971, 13-01-1984

##### Article 75

When the convicted person fully proves that he cannot comply with any of the modalities of the sanction that was imposed on him because it is incompatible with his age, sex, health or physical constitution, the Execution Judge may modify it, provided that the modification is not essential.  
Article reformed DOF 19-03-1971, 17-06-2016

##### Article 76

For the appropriateness of substitution and commutation, the convicted person shall be required to make reparation for the damage or provide the guarantee indicated by the jurisdictional body to ensure its payment, within the period fixed for him.  
Article reformed DOF 19-03-1971, 17-06-2016
TITLE FOUR CHAPTER I
Execution of sentences

##### Article 77

The jurisdictional authority is responsible for the imposition of sentences, their modification and duration; likewise, the Federal Executive is responsible for penitentiary administration.  
Article reformed DOF 17-06-2016

##### Article 78.- (Repealed)

#### CHAPTER II

Work of prisoners

Article repealed DOF 23-12-1985

##### Article 79.- (Repealed)

Article repealed DOF 23-12-1985

##### Article 80.- (Repealed)

Article repealed DOF 23-12-1985

##### Article 81.- (Repealed)

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 19-03-1971. Repealed DOF 23-12-1985

##### Article 82.- (Repealed)

Article reformed DOF 19-03-1971. Repealed DOF 23-12-1985

##### Article 83.- (Repealed)

Article reformed DOF 19-03-1971. Repealed DOF 23-12-1985

#### CHAPTER III

Preparatory liberty and retention

##### Article 84

Preparatory liberty shall be granted to the convicted person, upon the report referred to in the Code of Criminal Procedures, who has served three-fifths of his sentence, if it concerns intentional crimes, or half of it in the case of negligent crimes, provided that he meets the following requirements:  
I.- That he has observed good conduct during the execution of his sentence;  

II.- That from the examination of his personality it is presumed that he is socially rehabilitated and in a condition not to commit crimes again, and
III.- That he has made reparation or undertakes to make reparation for the damage caused, subject to the form, measures and terms that are fixed for him for said purpose, if he cannot cover it immediately.  

Once the foregoing requirements are met, the competent authority shall have a period of no more than 30 working days to grant preparatory liberty or, as the case may be, inform the interested party of the result of his procedure; said preparatory liberty shall be subject to the following conditions:
Paragraph reformed DOF 30-06-2006

a).- To reside or, as the case may be, not to reside in a determined place, and inform the authority of changes in his domicile. The designation of the place of residence shall be made by reconciling the circumstance that the prisoner can obtain work in the place that is fixed, with the fact that his permanence there is not an obstacle to his reform;  

b).- To perform within the period determined by the resolution, a lawful trade, art, industry or profession, if he does not have his own means of subsistence;  
c).- To abstain from the abuse of intoxicating beverages and from the use of narcotics: psychotropic substances or substances that produce similar effects, except by medical prescription;  
Subsection reformed DOF 31-12-1974

d).- To submit to the guidance and supervision measures that are dictated to him and to the surveillance of an honest and established person, who undertakes to report on his conduct, presenting him whenever required to do so.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 19-03-1971


This excerpt sits under: FEDERAL PENAL CODE > FIRST BOOK PRELIMINARY TITLE > FIRST TITLE > CHAPTER VI

##### Article 85. Preparatory release shall not be granted to

I. Those sentenced for any of the offenses provided for in this Code that are indicated below:
a) Illicit use of facilities intended for air traffic, provided for in article 172 bis, third paragraph;  
b) Against health, provided for in article 194, except in the case of individuals in whom evident cultural backwardness, social isolation and extreme economic necessity concur; and for the transportation modality, if they meet the requirements established in articles 84 and 90, section I, subsection c), for which they must be first-time offenders, despite not being in the three circumstances indicated in the general exception of this subsection;  
Subsection amended DOF 12-06-2003

c) Corruption of persons under eighteen years of age or of persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who do not have the capacity to resist it provided for in article 201; Pornography of persons under eighteen years of age or of persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who do not have the capacity to resist it, provided for in article 202; Sexual tourism against persons under eighteen years of age or of persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who do not have the capacity to resist it, provided for in articles 203 and 203 bis; Procuring of persons under eighteen years of age or of persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who do not have the capacity to resist it, provided for in article 204; Pederasty, provided for in article 209 Bis;  
Subsection amended DOF 27-03-2007, 27-11-2007, 19-08-2010

d) Rape, provided for in articles 265, 266 and 266 bis;  
e) Homicide, provided for in articles 315, 315 Bis and 320; and feminicide provided for in article 325;  
Subsection amended DOF 14-06-2012

f) Trafficking of minors, provided for in article 366 Ter.  
Subsection amended DOF 12-06-2000, 30-11-2010

g) Commercialization of stolen objects, provided for in article 368 ter;  

h) Vehicle theft, provided for in article 376 bis;  
i) Theft, provided for in articles 371, last paragraph; 372; 381, sections VII, VIII, IX, X, XI and XV; and 381 Bis;  
Subsection amended DOF 26-06-2008

j) Operations with resources of illicit origin, provided for in article 400 Bis;  
Subsection amended DOF 26-06-2008

k) Those provided for and sanctioned in articles 112 Bis, 112 Ter, 112 Quáter and 112 Quintus of the Law on Credit Institutions, when the person who commits it is part of an association, band or gang under the terms of article 164, or 164 Bis, or
Subsection added DOF 26-06-2008

l) Those provided for and sanctioned in articles 432, 433, 434 and 435 of the General Law on Negotiable Instruments and Credit Operations, when the person who commits it is part of an association, band or gang under the terms of article 164 or 164 Bis.  
Subsection added DOF 26-06-2008

II. Offenses on Trafficking in Persons contained in Title Two of the General Law to Prevent, Punish and Eradicate Offenses on Trafficking in Persons and for the Protection and Assistance to Victims of these Offenses;  
Section added DOF 27-11-2007. Amended DOF 14-06-2012

III. Those who incur a second recidivism of an intentional offense or are considered habitual offenders.  
Section renumbered DOF 27-11-2007

IV. Those sentenced for the conduct provided for in the General Law to Prevent and Punish Offenses on Kidnapping, Regulatory of section XXI of article 73 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, except those provided for in articles 9, 10, 11, 17 and 18.  
Section added DOF 30-11-2010

V. Those sentenced for the offense of Torture.  
Section added DOF 26-06-2017

In the case of offenses included in Title Ten of this Code, preparatory release shall only be granted when the reparation of damage referred to in section III of article 30 is satisfied or a bond guaranteeing it is provided.  
Article amended DOF 09-03-1946, 14-01-1966, 08-03-1968, 19-03-1971, 08-12-1978, 05-01-1983, 28-12-1992, 10-01-1994. Errata
DOF 01-02-1994. Amended DOF 17-05-1999

##### Article 86.- The competent authority shall revoke preparatory release when

I. The released person unjustifiably fails to comply with the conditions imposed to grant the benefit. The authority may, in case of a first non-compliance, admonish the sentenced person and warn of revoking the benefit in case of a second non-compliance. When the released person violates measures that establish frequent appearances for treatment, revocation shall only proceed upon the third non-compliance, or
II. The released person is convicted of a new intentional offense, by means of a final judgment, in which case revocation shall operate ex officio. If the new offense were negligent, the authority may, with reasoning and according to the seriousness of the act, revoke or maintain preparatory release.  
The convicted person whose preparatory release is revoked must serve the remainder of the sentence in prison, for which the authority shall consider the time served in release. The acts that give rise to the new proceedings referred to in section II of this article interrupt the periods to extinguish the sanction.  
Article amended DOF 19-03-1971, 10-01-1994, 17-05-1999

##### Article 87

Sentenced persons who enjoy preparatory release, granted by the jurisdictional authority, shall remain under the supervision and surveillance of the authority determined for that purpose by the applicable legislation on the matter.  
Article amended DOF 19-03-1971, 23-01-2009, 17-06-2016

##### Article 88.- (Repealed)

Article repealed DOF 23-12-1985

##### Article 89.- (Repealed)

#### CHAPTER IV

Conditional sentence

Article repealed DOF 23-12-1985

##### Article 90

The granting and enjoyment of the benefits of conditional sentencing shall be subject to the following rules:  
I.-  The judge or Court, as applicable, when issuing a conviction sentence or in the hypothesis established by section X of this article, shall suspend the execution of the penalties with reasoning, at the request of a party or ex officio, if these conditions are met:
a).-	That the conviction refers to a prison sentence not exceeding four years;  
Paragraph amended DOF 30-12-1991

b)	That the sentenced person is not a repeat offender for an intentional crime, has shown good conduct before and after the punishable act and that the conviction does not refer to any of the crimes indicated in section I of article 85 of this Code, and
Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994, 17-05-1999

c)	That based on their personal background or honest way of living, as well as the nature, modalities and motives of the crime, it is presumed that the sentenced person will not commit crimes again.  
Paragraph amended DOF 17-05-1999

d)	Repealed.  
Paragraph added as paragraph e) DOF 05-01-1983. Repealed as paragraph e) DOF 10-01-1994. Repealed as paragraph d) DOF 17-05-1999

II.-	To enjoy this benefit the sentenced person must:

a).- Provide the guarantee or submit to the measures imposed upon them, to ensure their appearance before the authority whenever required;  
b).- Undertake to reside in a specific place, from which they may not be absent without permission from the authority exercising care and supervision over them;  
c).-	Perform within the time period set for them, a lawful profession, art, trade or occupation;  
d).- Abstain from the abuse of intoxicating beverages and from the use of narcotics, psychotropic substances or other substances that produce similar effects, except by medical prescription; and
Errata to the paragraph DOF 07-05-1971. Amended DOF 31-12-1974
e).-	Repair the damage caused.
