The narcotics used in the commission of the crimes referred to in this chapter shall be placed at the disposal of the federal health authority, which shall proceed in accordance with the provisions or laws on the matter to their lawful use or destruction.  
In the case of instruments and vehicles used to commit the crimes considered in this chapter, as well as objects and products of those crimes, whatever the nature of said property, the provisions of articles 40 and 41 shall apply. For that purpose, the Public Prosecutor shall order during the preliminary investigation the corresponding seizure and the appropriate destination in support of the prosecution of justice, or shall request it in the proceeding, and shall promote the forfeiture so that the property in question or its product is destined to the administration of justice, or shall promote, as the case may be, the

suspension and deprivation of agrarian or other rights, before the authorities that are competent in accordance with the applicable rules.  
Article amended DOF 14-11-1947, 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 14-01-1985, 10-01-1994

##### Article 194

Imprisonment of ten to twenty-five years and one hundred to five hundred days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who:  
I.- Produces, transports, traffics, trades, supplies even gratuitously or prescribes any of the narcotics indicated in the preceding article, without the corresponding authorization referred to in the General Health Law;  

For the purposes of this section, by produce is understood: to manufacture, make, elaborate, prepare or condition any narcotic, and by trade: to sell, buy, acquire or alienate any narcotic.  
By supply is understood the material transmission in a direct or indirect manner, for any reason, of the possession of narcotics.  
Paragraph added DOF 20-08-2009

The trade and supply of narcotics may be investigated, prosecuted and, where appropriate, sanctioned by the authorities of the common jurisdiction in the terms of the General Health Law, when the assumptions of article 474 of said ordinance are met.  
Paragraph added DOF 20-08-2009

II.- Introduces into or removes from the country any of the narcotics included in the preceding article, even if momentarily or in transit.  
If the introduction or removal referred to in this section is not consummated, but from the acts performed it is clearly evident that this was the agent's purpose, the applicable penalty shall be up to two-thirds of that provided for in this article.  
III.- Contributes economic or any kind of resources, or collaborates in any way in the financing, supervision or promotion to make possible the execution of any of the crimes referred to in this chapter; and
IV.- Performs acts of advertising or propaganda, so that any of the substances included in the preceding article is consumed.  
The same penalties provided for in this article and, in addition, deprivation of office or commission and disqualification to hold another for up to five years, shall be imposed on the public servant who, in the exercise of their functions or taking advantage of their position, permits, authorizes or tolerates any of the conduct indicated in this article.  
Article amended DOF 14-11-1947, 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 08-12-1978, 10-01-1986, 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994


This excerpt sits under: FEDERAL PENAL CODE > FIRST BOOK PRELIMINARY TITLE > FIRST TITLE > CHAPTER I

##### Article 195

A penalty of five to fifteen years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred fifty days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who possesses any of the narcotics specified in Article 193, without the corresponding authorization referred to in the General Health Law, provided that such possession is for the purpose of carrying out any of the conducts provided for in Article 194, both of this code.

The possession of narcotics may be investigated, prosecuted and, where appropriate, sanctioned by the authorities of common jurisdiction in the terms of the General Health Law, when the assumptions of Article 474 of said ordinance are met.

When the accused possesses any of the narcotics specified in the table provided for in Article 479 of the General Health Law, in an amount equal to or greater than that resulting from multiplying by one thousand those referred to therein, it is presumed that the possession is intended to commit any of the conducts provided for in Article 194 of this code.
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 08-12-1978, 03-01-1989, 10-01-1994, 20-08-2009

##### Article 195

bis.- When by the circumstances of the act the possession of any of the narcotics specified in Article 193, without the authorization referred to in the General Health Law, cannot be considered intended to carry out any of the conducts referred to in Article 194, a penalty of four to seven years six months of imprisonment and fifty to one hundred fifty days' fine shall be applied.

The Federal Public Prosecutor's Office shall not criminally prosecute for this offense against the person who possesses:
I. Medications containing narcotics, whose sale to the public is subject to special acquisition requirements, when by their nature and quantity such medications are those necessary for the treatment of the person who possesses them or of other persons subject to the custody or care of the person who has them in their possession.
II. Peyote or hallucinogenic mushrooms, when by the quantity and circumstances of the case it may be presumed that they will be used in the ceremonies, uses and customs of indigenous peoples and communities, as recognized by their own authorities.
For purposes of this chapter, possession is understood as: the material holding of narcotics or when these are within the radius of action and availability of the person.
The possession of narcotics may be investigated, prosecuted and, where appropriate, sanctioned by the authorities of common jurisdiction in the terms of the General Health Law, when the assumptions of Article 474 of said ordinance are met.
Article added DOF 10-01-1994. Amended DOF 20-08-2009

##### Article 196

The penalties that may be applicable for the offenses provided for in Article 194 shall be increased by one half, when:
I.- It is committed by public servants in charge of preventing, reporting, investigating or judging the commission of offenses against health or by a member of the Mexican Armed Forces in retirement, reserve or active status. In this case, such public servants shall also be imposed suspension from holding office or commission in the public service, for up to five years, or dismissal, and disqualification for up to a time equal to that of the imprisonment penalty imposed. If it is a member of the Mexican Armed Forces in any of the situations mentioned, they shall also be imposed definitive discharge from the Armed Force to which they belong, and shall be disqualified for up to a time equal to that of the imprisonment penalty imposed, from holding public office or commission where applicable;

II.- The victim is a minor or incapacitated to understand the relevance of the conduct or to resist the agent;
III. Minors or incapacitated persons are used to commit any of those offenses;

IV.- It is committed in educational, welfare, police or detention centers, or in their vicinity with those who attend them;

V.- The conduct is carried out by professionals, technicians, assistants or personnel related to the health disciplines in any of their branches and they take advantage of that situation to commit them. In this case, suspension of rights or functions for the professional practice or trade for up to five years and disqualification for up to a time equivalent to that of the imprisonment imposed shall also be imposed;

VI.- The agent induces another person to commit any offense provided for in Article 194, taking advantage of the family or moral ascendancy or the authority or hierarchy they have over them; and
VII.- It involves the owner, possessor, lessee or usufructuary of an establishment of any nature and they use it to carry out any of the offenses provided for in this chapter or permit their commission by third parties. In this case, in addition, the establishment shall be permanently closed.
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 08-12-1978, 10-01-1994

##### Article 196 Bis.- Repealed

Article added DOF 10-01-1994. Repealed DOF 07-11-1996

##### Article 196

Ter.- A penalty of five to fifteen years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days' fine, as well as confiscation of the instruments, objects and products of the offense, shall be imposed on anyone who diverts or by any means contributes to diverting chemical precursors, essential chemical products or machines, to the cultivation, extraction, production, preparation or conditioning of narcotics in any form prohibited by law.
Paragraph amended DOF 08-02-1999

The same penalty of imprisonment and fine, as well as disqualification from holding any public employment, office or commission for up to five years, shall be imposed on the public servant who, in the exercise of their functions, permits or authorizes any of the conducts included in this article.

Chemical precursors, essential chemical products and machines are those defined in the law on the matter.
Paragraph amended DOF 08-02-1999 Article added DOF 13-05-1996

##### Article 197

Anyone who, without a prescription from a legally authorized physician, administers to another person, whether by injection, inhalation, ingestion or by any other means, any narcotic referred to in Article 193, shall be imposed a penalty of three to nine years of imprisonment and sixty to one hundred eighty days' fine, whatever the quantity administered. The penalties shall be increased by up to one half more if the victim is a minor or incapable of understanding the relevance of the conduct or of resisting the agent.

Anyone who improperly supplies free of charge or prescribes to a third party, of legal age, any narcotic mentioned in Article 193, for their personal and immediate use, shall be imposed a penalty of two to six years of imprisonment and forty to one hundred twenty days' fine. If the person who acquires it is a minor or incapacitated, the penalties shall be increased by up to one half.

The same penalties of the preceding paragraph shall be imposed on anyone who induces or assists another to consume any of the narcotics specified in Article 193.
Article amended DOF 14-11-1947, 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 08-12-1978, 03-01-1989, 10-01-1994

##### Article 198

A person who, engaging as a principal activity in work typical of the countryside, sows, cultivates or harvests marijuana plants, poppy, hallucinogenic mushrooms, peyote or any other plant that produces similar effects, on their own account, or with financing from third parties, when such person has limited education and extreme economic need, shall be subject to imprisonment of one to six years.

The same penalty shall be imposed on a person who, on property owned, held or possessed by them, consents to the sowing, cultivation or harvest of said plants in circumstances similar to the preceding hypothesis.  
If in the conduct described in the two preceding paragraphs the circumstances specified therein do not concur, the penalty shall be up to two-thirds of that provided in article 194, provided that the sowing, cultivation or harvest is done with the purpose of carrying out any of the conduct provided in sections I and II of said article. If that purpose is lacking, the penalty shall be two to eight years of imprisonment.

If the offense were committed by a public servant of a police corporation, dismissal from public employment, position or commission shall also be imposed and they shall be disqualified for one to five years from holding another, and if the offense is committed by a member of the Mexican Armed Forces in retirement, reserve or active status, in addition to the indicated prison sentence, definitive discharge from the Armed Force to which they belong shall be imposed and they shall be disqualified for one to five years from holding public position or commission.

The sowing, cultivation or harvest of marijuana plants shall not be punishable when these activities are carried out for medical and scientific purposes under the terms and conditions of the authorization issued for such purpose by the Federal Executive.  
Paragraph added DOF 19-06-2017 Article amended DOF 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 08-12-1978, 14-01-1985, 10-01-1986, 03-01-1989, 10-01-1994

##### Article 199

The Public Prosecutor or the judicial authority with jurisdiction, as soon as it learns that a person related to any proceeding for the offenses provided in articles 195 or 195 bis is drug-dependent, must immediately inform and, as applicable, involve the competent health authorities, for purposes of the corresponding treatment.

In every detention center, rehabilitation services shall be provided to the drug-dependent person.  
For the granting of conditional sentence or the benefit of preparatory release, when they apply, drug dependence shall not be considered as an antecedent of bad conduct, but in all cases it shall be required that the sentenced person submit to the corresponding medical treatment for their rehabilitation, under supervision of the executing authority.  
Article amended DOF 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 10-01-1986, 10-01-1994, 20-08-2009

#### CHAPTER II

Danger of Contagion
Chapter added DOF 14-02-1940

##### Article 199

Bis.- A person who, knowing that they are ill with a venereal disease or other serious disease in an infectious period, endangers the health of another through contagion, by sexual relations or other transmissible means, shall be sanctioned with three days to three years of imprisonment and up to forty days' fine.

If the disease suffered were incurable, a penalty of six months to five years of imprisonment shall be imposed.

When it involves spouses, cohabitants male or cohabitants female, proceedings may only be brought upon complaint of the offended party.  
Article added DOF 14-02-1940. Amended DOF 21-01-1991

#### Chapter III

Offenses against Reproductive Rights
Chapter added DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 199

Ter. A person who commits the offense provided in article 466 of the General Health Law with violence shall be subject to five to fourteen years of imprisonment and up to one hundred twenty days' fine.  
Article added DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 199

Quáter. A person who implants in a woman a fertilized ovum, when they have used for that purpose another's ovum or sperm from an unauthorized donor, without the express consent of the patient, of the donor or with the consent of a minor or of a person incapable of understanding the meaning of the act or of resisting it, shall be sanctioned with four to seven years of imprisonment and up to seventy days' fine.

If the offense is committed with violence or a pregnancy results from it, the applicable penalty shall be five to fourteen years and up to one hundred twenty days' fine.  
In addition to the penalties provided, suspension from exercising the profession or, in case of public servants, disqualification from holding public employment, position or commission, for a time equal to that of the prison sentence imposed, as well as dismissal, shall be imposed.

When a relationship of marriage, cohabitation or partnership exists between the perpetrator and the victim, the offenses provided in the preceding articles shall be prosecuted upon complaint.  
If children result as a consequence of the commission of any of the offenses provided in the preceding articles, reparation of damages shall also include payment of support for them and for the mother, under the terms established by civil legislation.  
Article added DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 199

Quintus. A person who, without the consent of a person, performs on them surgical, chemical or any other type of procedures to render them sterile, commits the offense of provoked sterility.  
The person responsible for provoked sterility shall be subject to four to seven years of imprisonment and up to seventy days' fine, as well as full payment of reparation for damages and losses caused, which may include the corresponding surgical procedure to reverse the sterility.

In addition to the penalties indicated in the preceding paragraph, the responsible person shall be subject to suspension from employment or profession for a period equal to that of the prison sentence imposed up to definitive disqualification, provided that by virtue of its exercise damage has resulted for the victim; or, in case the responsible person is a public servant, they shall be deprived of the public employment, position or commission they have been holding, provided that by virtue of its exercise they have committed said typical conduct.  
Article added DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 199

Sextus. The offenses provided in this chapter shall be prosecutable ex officio, with the exception of those indicated as requiring complaint by the offended party.  
Article added DOF 14-06-2012

TITLE SEVENTH BIS
OFFENSES AGAINST THE INDEMNITY OF PRIVACY OF SEXUAL INFORMATION
Title added DOF 15-06-2018

#### CHAPTER I

Communication of Sexual Content with Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or Persons who do not have Capacity to Understand the Meaning of the Act or Persons who do not have Capacity to Resist It
Chapter added DOF 15-06-2018

##### Article 199

Septies.- A person who, making use of means of broadcasting, telecommunications, computer or any other means of data transmission, contacts a person under eighteen years of age, a person who does not have capacity to understand the meaning of the act or a person who does not have capacity to resist it and requests from them images, audio or video of explicit sexual activities, acts of sexual connotation, or solicits a sexual encounter from them, shall be subject to four to eight years of imprisonment and a fine of four hundred to one thousand days' fine.

#### CHAPTER II

Violation of Sexual Intimacy

Article added DOF 15-06-2018

Chapter added DOF 01-06-2021

##### Article 199

Octies.- The crime of violation of sexual intimacy is committed by any person who discloses, shares, distributes or publishes images, videos or audio of intimate sexual content of a person who has reached the age of majority, without their consent, their approval or their authorization.  
As well as whoever videotapes, audiotapes, photographs, prints or produces images, audio or videos with intimate sexual content of a person without their consent, without their approval, or without their authorization.  
These conducts shall be punished with a penalty of three to six years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to one thousand Units of Measure and Update.  
Article added DOF 01-06-2021

##### Article 199

Nonies.- The same penalties provided in the preceding article shall be imposed when the images, videos or audio of intimate sexual content that are disclosed, shared, distributed or published do not correspond with the person who is indicated or identified in them.  
Article added DOF 01-06-2021

##### Article 199

Decies.- The minimum and maximum of the penalty shall be increased by up to one half:

I.-  When the crime is committed by the spouse, cohabitant male or female, or by any person with whom the victim has or has had a sentimental, affective or trust relationship;  
II.- When the crime is committed by a public servant in the exercise of their functions;  

III.- When it is committed against a person who cannot understand the meaning of the act or does not have the capacity to resist it;  
IV.- When any type of non-lucrative benefit is obtained;  

V.- When it is done for lucrative purposes, or

VI.- When as a consequence of the effects or impacts of the crime, the victim attempts against their integrity or against their own life.  
Article added DOF 01-06-2021

TITLE EIGHT
CRIMES AGAINST THE FREE DEVELOPMENT OF PERSONALITY.  
Denomination reformed DOF 14-01-1966. Title moved DOF 20-01-1967. Moved (formerly Title Ninth) DOF 29-07-1970.  
Denomination reformed DOF 27-03-2007

#### CHAPTER I

Corruption of Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or of Persons who do not have the Capacity to understand the Meaning of the Act or of Persons who do not have the Capacity to Resist it.  
Denomination of the Chapter reformed DOF 14-02-1940, 14-01-1966, 27-03-2007

##### Article 200

Whoever trades, distributes, exhibits, circulates or offers to persons under eighteen years of age, books, writings, recordings, films, photographs, printed advertisements, images or objects of a pornographic nature, real or simulated, whether physically or through any means, shall be punished with six months to five years of imprisonment and three hundred to five hundred days fine.  

Material that signifies or has as its purpose scientific, artistic or technical dissemination, or as the case may be, sex education, education about reproductive function, prevention of sexually transmitted diseases and adolescent pregnancy, shall not be understood as pornographic or harmful material, provided that they are approved by the competent authority.  
Article reformed DOF 14-02-1940, 14-01-1966, 21-01-1991, 27-03-2007

##### Article 201

The crime of corruption of minors is committed by whoever compels, induces, facilitates or procures one or more persons under 18 years of age or one or more persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or one or more persons who do not have the capacity to resist it to perform any of the following acts:
Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016
a) Habitual consumption of alcoholic beverages;  

b) Consumption of toxic substances or consumption of any of the narcotics referred to in the first paragraph of article 193 of this Code or pharmaceutical dependence;  
c) Begging for purposes of exploitation;  

d) Commission of any crime;  

e) Forming part of a criminal association; or
Subsection reformed DOF 18-07-2016

f) Performing acts of bodily or sexual exhibitionism, simulated or not, with lascivious or sexual purpose.  

Whoever commits this crime shall be punished: in the case of subsection a) or b) with imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of five hundred to one thousand days; in the case of subsection c) with imprisonment of four to nine years and four hundred to nine hundred days fine; in the case of subsection d) the provisions of article 52, of Chapter I, of Title Three, of this Code shall apply; in the case of subsection e) or f) with imprisonment of seven to twelve years and a fine of eight hundred to two thousand five hundred days.  

When it concerns begging due to poverty or abandonment, it must be attended to by social assistance.  
Preventive, educational or any other programs designed and provided by public, private or social institutions that have as their object sex education, education about reproductive function, prevention of sexually transmitted diseases and adolescent pregnancy, shall not be understood as corruption, provided that they are approved by the competent authority; photographs, video recordings, audio recordings or fixed or moving images, printed, embodied or that are contained or reproduced in magnetic, electronic or other media and that constitute family memories.  

In case of doubt, the judge shall request expert opinions to evaluate the conduct in question.  

When it is not possible to determine with precision the age of the victim or victims, the judge shall request the corresponding expert opinions.  
Article reformed DOF 14-01-1966, 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 03-01-1989, 10-01-1994, 04-01-2000, 27-03-2007

##### Article 201

BIS.- It is prohibited to employ persons under eighteen years of age or persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act, in canteens, taverns, bars, nightclubs, vice centers or any other place where their healthy physical, mental or emotional development is negatively affected.  

Contravention of this provision shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three years and three hundred to seven hundred days fine; in case of recidivism, the definitive closure of the establishment shall be ordered.  
The same penalty shall be imposed on mothers, fathers, tutors or guardians who accept or promote that their daughters or sons under eighteen years of age or persons under eighteen years of age or persons who are under their care, custody or guardianship, be employed in said establishments.  

For the purposes of this provision, a person under eighteen years of age who for a salary, for food alone, for commission of any kind or for any other stipend or emolument, or gratuitously, provides their services in such place shall be considered as employed in the canteen, tavern, bar or vice center.  
Article added DOF 04-01-2000. Reformed DOF 27-03-2007

##### Article 201

bis 1. Repealed
Article added DOF 04-01-2000. Repealed DOF 27-03-2007

##### Article 201

bis 2. Repealed
Article added DOF 04-01-2000. Repealed DOF 27-03-2007

##### Article 201

bis 3. Repealed

Article added DOF 04-01-2000. Repealed DOF 27-03-2007

#### CHAPTER II

Pornography of Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or of Persons who do not have the Capacity to understand the Meaning of the Act or of Persons who do not have the Capacity to Resist it.  
Denomination reformed DOF 03-01-1989, 04-01-2000. Chapter relocated with denomination reformed DOF 27-03-2007

##### Artículo 202

Commits the crime of pornography of persons under eighteen years of age or of persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who do not have the capacity to resist it, whoever procures, compels, facilitates or induces, by any means, one or more of these persons to perform sexual acts or acts of bodily exhibitionism with lascivious or sexual purposes, real or simulated, with the object of video recording them, photographing them, filming them, exhibiting them or describing them through printed advertisements, transmission of data files on public or private telecommunications networks, computer systems, electronic or substitute systems. The author of this crime shall be imposed a penalty of seven to twelve years of imprisonment and eight hundred to two thousand days' fine.

Whoever fixes, prints, video records, photographs, films or describes acts of bodily or lascivious or sexual exhibitionism, real or simulated, in which one or more persons under eighteen years of age or one or more persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or one or more persons who do not have the capacity to resist it participate, shall be imposed the penalty of seven to twelve

years of imprisonment and eight hundred to two thousand days' fine, as well as the confiscation of the objects, instruments and products of the crime.  
The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever reproduces, stores, distributes, sells, purchases, leases, exhibits, advertises, transmits, imports or exports the material referred to in the preceding paragraphs.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 14-01-1966, 27-03-2007

##### Artículo 202

BIS.- Whoever stores, purchases, leases, the material referred to in the preceding paragraphs, without purposes of commercialization or distribution shall be imposed from one to five years of imprisonment and one hundred to five hundred days' fine. Likewise, he shall be subject to specialized psychiatric treatment.  
Article added DOF 27-03-2007

#### CAPÍTULO III

Sexual Tourism against Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or Persons who do not have the Capacity to understand the Meaning of the Act or Persons who do not have the Capacity to Resist it.  
Denomination reformed DOF 13-01-1984. Chapter relocated with denomination reformed DOF 27-03-2007

##### Artículo 203

Commits the crime of sexual tourism whoever promotes, advertises, invites, facilitates or arranges by any means for one or more persons to travel inside or outside the national territory with the purpose of performing any type of real or simulated sexual acts with one or more persons under eighteen years of age, or with one or more persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or with one or more persons who do not have the capacity to resist it.

The author of this crime shall be imposed a penalty of seven to twelve years of imprisonment and eight hundred to two thousand days' fine.  
Article reformed DOF 15-01-1951, 30-12-1997, 04-01-2000, 27-03-2007

##### Artículo 203

BIS.- Whoever performs any type of real or simulated sexual acts with one or more persons under eighteen years of age, or with one or more persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or with one or more persons who do not have the capacity to resist it, by virtue of sexual tourism, shall be imposed a penalty of twelve to sixteen years of imprisonment and two thousand to three thousand days' fine, likewise, he shall be subject to specialized psychiatric treatment.  
Article added DOF 27-03-2007

#### CAPÍTULO IV

Procuring of Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or Persons who do not have the Capacity to understand the Meaning of the Act or Persons who do not have the Capacity to Resist it.  
Chapter relocated with denomination reformed DOF 27-03-2007

##### Artículo 204

Commits the crime of procuring of persons under eighteen years of age or of persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who do not have the capacity to resist it:  
I.- Any person who exploits the body of the aforementioned persons, through carnal commerce or obtains any profit from it;  
II.- Whoever induces or solicits any of the aforementioned persons, to sexually commerce with his body or facilitates the means for him to engage in prostitution, and

III.- Whoever manages, administers or sustains directly or indirectly, brothels, houses of assignation or places of concurrence dedicated to exploiting the prostitution of persons under eighteen years of age or of persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who do not have the capacity to resist it, or obtains any benefit from their proceeds.

The person responsible for this crime shall be imposed imprisonment of eight to fifteen years and one thousand to two thousand five hundred days of fine, as well as definitive closure of the establishments described in section III.  
Article reformed DOF 27-03-2007

#### CAPÍTULO V

Trafficking of Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or Persons who do not have the Capacity to understand the Meaning of the Act or Persons who do not have the Capacity to Resist it.  
Chapter added DOF 27-03-2007

##### Artículo 205. Derogado

Article repealed DOF 14-01-1966. Added DOF 13-01-1984. Reformed DOF 03-01-1989, 04-01-2000, 27-03-2007. Repealed DOF
27-11-2007

##### Artículo 205

Bis. The sanctions indicated in articles 200, 201, 204 and 209 Quáter shall be imprescriptible. Likewise, the sanctions indicated in said articles shall be increased to double that which corresponds when the author has, with respect to the victim, any of the following relationships:
Paragraph reformed DOF 27-11-2007, 14-06-2012, 25-04-2023

a)	Those who exercise parental authority, guardianship or custody;  

b)	Ascendants or descendants without limit of degree;  

c)	Relatives in collateral line up to the fourth degree;  
d)	Tutors or curators;  

e)	Whoever exercises over the victim by virtue of an employment, teaching, domestic, medical or any other relationship that implies a subordination of the victim;  
f)	Whoever avails himself of public function to commit the crime;  

g)	Whoever resides in the same domicile as the victim;  

h)	A minister of a religious cult;  
i)	When the author employs physical, psychological or moral violence against the victim; and

j)	Whoever is linked with the victim by an affective or friendship bond, of gratitude, or any other that may influence obtaining the trust of the latter.  
In the cases of subsections a), b), c) and d) in addition to the sanctions indicated, the authors of the crime shall lose parental authority, tutorship or curatorship, as the case may be, with respect to all their descendants, the right to alimony that could correspond to him by his relationship with the victim and the right that he could have with respect to the property of the latter.

In the cases of subsections e), f) and h) in addition to the sanctions indicated, he shall be punished with dismissal and disqualification to perform the position or commission or any other of a public or similar character, up to a time equal to the penalty imposed.

In all cases the judge shall agree to the pertinent measures so that the offender is permanently prohibited from having any type of contact or relationship with the victim.

#### CAPITULO VI

Procuring and Trafficking of Persons.  

Article added DOF 27-03-2007

Chapter added DOF 27-03-2007

##### Artículo 206

Procuring shall be sanctioned with imprisonment of two to nine years and fifty to five hundred days' fine.  
Article reformed DOF 03-01-1989, 27-03-2007

##### Article 206 BIS.- Commits the crime of pandering

I.- Any person who exploits the body of another through carnal commerce, is maintained by this commerce or obtains any profit from it;  
II.- Whoever induces or solicits a person to sexually commerce their body with another or facilitates the means for them to engage in prostitution, and
III.- Whoever manages, administers or maintains directly or indirectly, brothels, houses of assignation or places of gathering expressly dedicated to exploiting prostitution, or obtains any benefit from their proceeds.  
Article added DOF 27-03-2007

##### Article 207. Repealed

Article amended DOF 14-02-1940, 27-03-2007. Repealed DOF 27-11-2007

#### CHAPTER VII

Provocation of a Crime and Apology for it or for any Vice and the Omission to prevent a Crime that threatens the Free Development of Personality, Human Dignity or Physical or Mental Integrity.  
Chapter added DOF 27-03-2007

##### Article 208

Whoever publicly provokes the commission of a crime, or makes an apology for it or for any vice, shall be subject to ten to one hundred eighty days of community service, if the crime is not executed; otherwise the provoker shall be subject to the sanction corresponding to their participation in the crime committed.  
Article amended DOF 14-01-1966, 23-12-1985, 04-01-2000, 27-03-2007

##### Article 209

Whoever, being able to do so with their immediate intervention and without risk to themselves or others, does not prevent the commission of one of the crimes contemplated in Title VIII, Book Two, of this Code or in the Federal Law to Prevent and Punish Trafficking in Persons shall be imposed a penalty of six months to three years in prison and fifty to two hundred days fine.  
Paragraph amended DOF 19-08-2010

The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever, being able to do so, does not go to the authority or its agents so that they prevent a crime of those contemplated in the preceding paragraph and of whose imminent commission they have notice.  

Said penalties shall be imposed on persons related or assigned to any institution, association, organization or group of a religious, cultural, sports, educational, recreational or any other nature and have knowledge of the commission of the crimes referred to in the first paragraph of this article, when they do not inform the competent authority or protect the person who commits it, whether by hiding them, changing their location or in any other way providing them protection.  
Paragraph added DOF 19-08-2010 Article amended DOF 10-01-1994, 27-03-2007

#### CHAPTER VIII

Pederasty
Chapter added DOF 19-08-2010

##### Article 209

Bis.- Nine to eighteen years in prison and seven hundred fifty to two thousand two hundred fifty days fine shall be applied to whoever takes advantage of the trust, subordination or superiority they have over a minor under eighteen years of age, derived from their kinship of any degree, guardianship, curatorship, custody, teaching, religious, labor, medical, cultural, domestic relationship or of any nature and executes, compels, induces or convinces to execute any sexual act, with or without their consent.  

The same penalty shall apply to whoever commits the conduct described in the preceding paragraph, against a person who does not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or to resist it.  

If the agent uses physical violence, the penalties shall be increased by one half.  
The perpetrator of the crime may be subject to comprehensive medical treatment for the time required, which may not exceed the time of the prison sentence imposed.  

In addition to the foregoing penalties, the perpetrator of the crime shall lose, as applicable, parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, adoption, the right to support and the right they might have with respect to the victim's property, in terms of civil legislation.  

When the crime is committed by a public servant or a professional in the exercise of their functions or by reason thereof, in addition to the prison sentence indicated above, they shall be disqualified, dismissed or suspended from their public employment or profession for a term equal to the sentence imposed.  
Article added DOF 19-08-2010

##### Article 209

Ter.- For the purpose of determining the damage caused to the free development of the victim's personality, the necessary expert opinions shall be requested to ascertain their harm. In case of non-compliance with this provision by the Public Prosecutor, they shall be sanctioned in the terms of this Code and applicable legislation.  
In cases where the convicted person refuses or cannot guarantee medical, psychological or specialized care as required, the State shall provide those services to the victim.  
Article added DOF 19-08-2010

#### CHAPTER IX

Forced Cohabitation of Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or of Persons who do not have the Capacity to understand the Meaning of the Act or of Persons who do not have the Capacity to Resist it.  
Chapter added DOF 25-04-2023

##### Article 209

Quáter. Commits the crime of forced cohabitation of persons under eighteen years of age or of persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of

persons who do not have the capacity to resist it, whoever compels, coerces, induces, solicits, arranges or offers one or more of these persons to join informally or customarily, with or without their consent, with someone of their same condition or with a person over eighteen years of age, for the purpose of living together constantly and in a manner equivalent to marriage.  

The person responsible for this crime shall be imposed a penalty of eight to fifteen years in prison and one thousand to two thousand five hundred days fine.  
The penalty provided in the preceding paragraph shall be increased up to one half, in its minimum and maximum, if the victim belongs to any indigenous or Afro-Mexican people or community.  
Article added DOF 25-04-2023
TITLE NINE
Revelation of secrets and illicit access to computer systems and equipment
Title moved DOF 20-01-1967. Moved (formerly Title Ten) DOF 29-07-1970. Denomination amended DOF 17-05-1999

#### CHAPTER I

Revelation of secrets
Chapter added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Article 210

Thirty to two hundred days of community service shall be imposed on whoever without just cause, with prejudice to someone and without consent of the person who may be harmed, reveals any secret or reserved communication that they know or have received by reason of their employment, position or post.  
Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 211

The sanction shall be one to five years, a fine of fifty to five hundred pesos and suspension of profession as applicable, from two months to one year, when the punishable revelation is made by a person who provides professional or technical services or by a public official or employee or when the secret revealed or published is of an industrial nature.

##### Article 211

Bis.- Whoever reveals, discloses or improperly uses or to the detriment of another, information or images obtained in an interception of private communication, shall be subject to sanctions of six to twelve years in prison and three hundred to six hundred days fine.  
Article added DOF 07-11-1996

#### CHAPTER II

Illicit access to computer systems and equipment
Chapter added DOF 17-05-1999


This excerpt sits under: FEDERAL PENAL CODE > FIRST BOOK PRELIMINARY TITLE > FIRST TITLE > CHAPTER VI

##### Artículo 211

bis 1.- Whoever without authorization modifies, destroys or causes loss of information contained in computer systems or equipment protected by any security mechanism, shall be punished with six months to two years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days' fine.  
Whoever without authorization accesses or copies information contained in computer systems or equipment protected by any security mechanism, shall be punished with three months to one year of imprisonment and fifty to one hundred fifty days' fine.  
Article added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Artículo 211

bis 2.- Whoever without authorization modifies, destroys or causes loss of information contained in computer systems or equipment of the State, protected by any security mechanism, shall be punished with one to four years of imprisonment and two hundred to six hundred days' fine.  

Whoever without authorization accesses or copies information contained in computer systems or equipment of the State, protected by any security mechanism, shall be punished with six months to two years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days' fine.  

Whoever without authorization accesses, obtains, copies or uses information contained in any system, equipment or computer storage medium of public security, protected by any security means, shall be punished with a penalty of four to ten years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to one thousand days of the general minimum wage in force in the Federal District. If the person responsible is or has been a public servant in a public security institution, dismissal and disqualification for four to ten years to serve in another employment, position, office or public commission shall also be imposed.  
Paragraph added DOF 24-06-2009

The foregoing penalties shall be doubled when the conduct obstructs, hinders, impedes, limits or makes impossible the prosecution or administration of justice, or affects records related to a criminal proceeding safeguarded by the competent authorities.  
Paragraph added DOF 17-06-2016 Article added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Artículo 211

bis 3.- Whoever being authorized to access computer systems and equipment of the State, improperly modifies, destroys or causes loss of information they contain, shall be punished with two to eight years of imprisonment and three hundred to nine hundred days' fine.  
Whoever being authorized to access computer systems and equipment of the State, improperly copies information they contain, shall be punished with one to four years of imprisonment and one hundred fifty to four hundred fifty days' fine.  

Whoever being authorized to access systems, equipment or computer storage media in matters of public security, improperly obtains, copies or uses information they contain, shall be punished with a penalty of four to ten years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to one thousand days of the general minimum wage in force in the Federal District. If the person responsible is or has been a public servant in a public security institution, up to one-half more of the imposed penalty, dismissal and disqualification for a period equal to that of the resulting penalty to serve in another employment, position, office or public commission shall also be imposed.  
Paragraph added DOF 24-06-2009 Article added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Artículo 211

bis 4.- Whoever without authorization modifies, destroys or causes loss of information contained in computer systems or equipment of the institutions that comprise the financial system, protected by any security mechanism, shall be punished with six months to four years of imprisonment and one hundred to six hundred days' fine.  

Whoever without authorization accesses or copies information contained in computer systems or equipment of the institutions that comprise the financial system, protected by any security mechanism, shall be punished with three months to two years of imprisonment and fifty to three hundred days' fine.  
Article added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Artículo 211

bis 5.- Whoever being authorized to access computer systems and equipment of the institutions that comprise the financial system, improperly modifies, destroys or causes loss of information they contain, shall be punished with six months to four years of imprisonment and one hundred to six hundred days' fine.  

Whoever being authorized to access computer systems and equipment of the institutions that comprise the financial system, improperly copies information they contain, shall be punished with three months to two years of imprisonment and fifty to three hundred days' fine.  

The penalties provided in this article shall be increased by one-half when the conduct is committed by officials or employees of the institutions that comprise the financial system.  
Article added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Artículo 211

bis 6.- For the purposes of articles 211 Bis 4 and 211 Bis 5 above, institutions that comprise the financial system are understood to be those indicated in article 400 Bis of this Code.  
Article added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Artículo 211

bis 7.- The penalties provided in this chapter shall be increased by up to one-half when the information obtained is used for one's own benefit or that of another.  
Article added DOF 17-05-1999

TITULO DECIMO
Crimes for acts of corruption
Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Eleventh) DOF 29-07-1970. Denomination amended DOF 05-01-1983,
18-07-2016, 05-11-2018, 12-04-2019

#### CAPITULO I

Denomination of Chapter deleted DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 212

For the purposes of this Title and the subsequent one, a public servant is any person who performs an employment, position or commission of any nature in the centralized Federal Public Administration or in that of the Federal District, decentralized agencies, majority state-owned enterprises, organizations and companies assimilated to these, public trusts, state productive enterprises, in autonomous constitutional bodies, in the Congress of the Union, or in the Federal Judicial Power, or who manages federal economic resources. The provisions contained in this Title are applicable to the Governors of the States, to the Deputies, to the Local Legislatures and to the Magistrates of the Local Courts of Justice, for the commission of the crimes provided for in this Title, in federal matters.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 14-07-2014, 18-07-2016

The same sanctions provided for the crime in question shall be imposed on any person who participates in the perpetration of any of the crimes provided for in this Title or the subsequent one.  

In addition to said sanctions, the penalty of removal from office and disqualification from holding public employment, position or commission, as well as from participating in acquisitions, leases, services or public works, concessions for the provision of public services or for exploitation, use and enjoyment of property belonging to the Federation shall be imposed on those responsible for their commission for a period of one to twenty years, according to the following criteria:  
I.-   It shall be for a period of one to ten years when there is no damage or harm or when the amount of the damage or benefit obtained from the commission of the crime does not exceed two hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update, and
II.-  It shall be for a period of ten to twenty years if said amount exceeds the limit indicated in the preceding section.  
Paragraph with sections added DOF 18-07-2016

For the purposes of the foregoing, the judge must consider, in the event that the person responsible has the status of public servant, in addition to the provisions of article 213 of this Code, the elements of the employment, position or commission that he or she held when he or she committed the crime.  
Paragraph added DOF 18-07-2016

When the person responsible has the status of a private individual, the judge must impose the sanction of disqualification from holding public office, as well as from participating in acquisitions, leases, concessions, services or public works, considering, as applicable, the following:  
I.-	The patrimonial damages and harm caused by the acts or omissions;  

II.-	The socioeconomic circumstances of the person responsible;  
III.-	The external conditions and the means of execution, and

IV.-	The amount of the benefit that the person responsible has obtained.  
Paragraph with sections added DOF 18-07-2016

Without prejudice to the foregoing, the category of official or trusted employee shall be a circumstance that may give rise to an aggravation of the penalty.  
Paragraph added DOF 18-07-2016

When the crimes referred to in articles 214, 217, 221, 222, 223 and 224 of this Code are committed by public servants elected by popular vote or whose appointment is subject to ratification by one of the Chambers of the Congress of the Union, the penalties provided shall be increased by up to one third.  
Paragraph added DOF 18-07-2016 Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 213

For the individualization of the sanctions provided for in this Title, the judge shall take into account, as applicable, the hierarchical level of the public servant and the degree of responsibility of the position, his or her seniority in the employment, his or her service record, his or her compensation, his or her level of education, the need to repair the damages and harm caused by the unlawful conduct and the special circumstances of the facts constituting the crime. Without prejudice to the foregoing, the category of official or trusted employee shall be a circumstance that may give rise to an aggravation of the penalty.  
Article reformed DOF 05-01-1983, 18-07-2016

##### Article 213

Bis.- When the crimes referred to in articles 215, 219 and 222 of this Code are committed by public servants who are members of any police, customs or immigration agency, the penalties provided shall be increased by up to one half.  
Article added DOF 03-01-1989. Reformed DOF 18-07-2016

#### CHAPTER II

Unlawful exercise of public service
Chapter relocated with denomination reformed DOF 05-01-1983. Denomination reformed DOF 18-07-2016

##### Article 214

The crime of unlawful exercise of public service is committed by the public servant who:
Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016

I.-  Exercises the functions of an employment, position or commission without having taken legitimate possession, or without satisfying all legal requirements.  
II.-  Continues to exercise the functions of an employment, position or commission after learning that his or her appointment has been revoked or that he or she has been suspended or removed from office.  
III.- Having knowledge by reason of his or her employment, position or commission that the patrimony or interests of any dependency or entity of the centralized federal public administration, decentralized agencies, majority state-owned enterprise, associations and companies assimilated to these and public trusts

of state productive enterprises, of autonomous constitutional bodies, of the Congress of the Union or of the Judicial Power may be seriously affected by any act or omission and does not report it in writing to his or her hierarchical superior or does not prevent it if it is within his or her powers.  
Section reformed DOF 14-07-2014, 18-07-2016

IV.- By himself or herself or through an intermediary, unlawfully removes, destroys, conceals, uses, or renders useless information or documentation that is under his or her custody or to which he or she has access, or of which he or she has knowledge by virtue of his or her employment, position or commission.  
V.-  By himself or herself or through an intermediary, when legally required, renders reports in which he or she states false facts or circumstances or denies the truth in whole or in part regarding them, and
Section added DOF 30-06-2006

VI.- Having an obligation by reason of employment, position or commission to guard, watch over, protect or provide security to persons, places, installations or objects, failing in his or her duty, in any way causes damage to persons, or to places, installations or objects, or loss or removal of objects that are under his or her care.  
Section added DOF 17-11-1986. Moved DOF 30-06-2006

Whoever commits any of the crimes referred to in sections I and II of this article shall be punished with one to three years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days' fine.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016

The violator of sections III, IV, V and VI shall be punished with two to seven years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred fifty days' fine.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 17-11-1986, 30-06-2006, 18-07-2016
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 05-01-1983

#### CHAPTER III

Abuse of authority
Denomination of the Chapter reformed DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 215

Public servants who engage in any of the following conducts commit the crime of abuse of authority:
Paragraph amended DOF 03-01-1989

I.-  When, in order to prevent the execution of a law, decree or regulation, the collection of a tax or the compliance with a judicial decision, they request assistance from the public force or employ it for that purpose;  
II.	Repealed.  
Section repealed DOF 26-06-2017

III.- When they unduly delay or deny to individuals the protection or service they are obligated to provide or prevent the submission or processing of a request;  
IV.- When, being in charge of administering justice, under any pretext, even that of obscurity or silence of the law, they unjustifiably refuse to dispatch a matter pending before them, within the terms established by law;  
V.  When the person in charge or member of a public force, legally required by a competent authority to provide assistance, refuses to provide it or unjustifiably delays it. The same provision shall apply in the case of expert witnesses.  
Section amended DOF 23-01-2009

VI.- When, being in charge of any establishment intended for the execution of custodial sanctions, social reintegration institutions or custody and rehabilitation of minors and preventive or administrative detention centers, or arraigo centers, without the legal requirements, receives as prisoner, detained, arrested, subject to arraigo or inmate a person or keeps them deprived of their liberty, without notifying the corresponding authority of the fact; denies that they are detained, if they are; or does not comply with the release order issued by the competent authority;  
Section amended DOF 18-07-2016

VII.- When, having knowledge of an illegal deprivation of liberty, does not immediately report it to the competent authority or does not immediately put an end to it, if this is within their powers;  
VIII.- When they cause funds, securities or other things that have not been entrusted to them to be delivered to them and they appropriate them or dispose of them improperly.  
IX.- Obtains, demands or solicits without any right or legitimate cause, for themselves or for any other person, part of the salary or remuneration of one or more of their subordinates, gifts or other goods or services;  
Section amended DOF 18-07-2016

X.- When in the exercise of their functions or by reason thereof, they grant public employment, position or commission, or contracts for the provision of professional or commercial services or of any other nature, that are remunerated, knowing that the service for which they were appointed will not be provided, or the contract granted will not be fulfilled;  

XI.- When they authorize or contract someone who is disqualified by final decision of competent authority to hold an employment, position or commission in the public service, or to participate in acquisitions, leases, services or public works, provided they do so with knowledge of such situation;  
Section amended DOF 06-06-2006, 18-07-2016

XII.- When they issue any identification that accredits as a public servant any person who does not actually perform the employment, position or commission referred to in said identification;  
Section amended DOF 06-06-2006

XIII.  Repealed.  
Section added DOF 06-06-2006. Amended DOF 30-11-2010, 18-07-2016. Repealed DOF 26-06-2017

XIV.- Compel to testify the persons mentioned in article 243 Bis of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures, regarding information obtained by reason of the performance of their activity.  
Section added DOF 06-06-2006

XV.	Fail to make the immediate registration of the corresponding detention, falsify the corresponding Administrative Detention Report, fail to update it properly or unjustifiably delay placing the detainee under the custody of the corresponding authority; and
Section added DOF 23-01-2009. Amended DOF 30-11-2010, 26-06-2017

XVI.	Fail to comply with the obligation to prevent the execution of conducts of deprivation of liberty.  
Section added DOF 30-11-2010

Whoever commits the crime of abuse of authority under the terms provided by sections I to V and X to XII, shall be imposed from one to eight years of imprisonment and from fifty to one hundred days fine. The same sanction shall be imposed on persons who accept the appointments, contracts or identifications referred to in sections X to XII.  
Paragraph amended DOF 03-01-1989, 18-07-2016

Whoever commits the crime of abuse of authority under the terms provided by sections VI to IX, XIV, XV and XVI, shall be imposed from two to nine years of imprisonment, from seventy to four hundred days fine and removal and disqualification from two to nine years to hold another public employment, position or commission.  
Paragraph amended DOF 03-01-1989, 06-06-2006, 23-01-2009, 30-11-2010, 18-07-2016, 26-06-2017
Article amended DOF 05-01-1983

#### CHAPTER III BIS

Forced disappearance of persons
(Repealed)
Chapter added DOF 01-06-2001. Repealed DOF 17-11-2017

##### Article 215-A.- Repealed

Article added DOF 01-06-2001. Repealed DOF 17-11-2017

##### Article 215-B.- Repealed

Article added DOF 01-06-2001. Repealed DOF 17-11-2017

##### Article 215-C.- Repealed

Article added DOF 01-06-2001. Repealed DOF 17-11-2017

##### Article 215-D.- Repealed

Article added DOF 01-06-2001. Repealed DOF 17-11-2017

#### CHAPTER IV

Coalition of public servants
Chapter relocated with amended denomination DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 216

Those who, having such character, form a coalition to take measures contrary to a law, regulation or other provisions of a general nature, prevent their execution or to resign from their positions in order to prevent or suspend the public administration in any of its branches, commit the crime of coalition of public servants. Workers who form a coalition in exercise of their constitutional rights or who exercise the right to strike do not commit this crime.  

Whoever commits the crime of coalition of public servants shall be imposed from two years to seven years of imprisonment and a fine of thirty to three hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time of the commission of the crime.  
Article amended DOF 05-01-1983, 18-07-2016

#### CHAPTER V

Illicit use of powers and faculties
Chapter relocated with amended denomination DOF 05-01-1983. Denomination amended DOF 18-07-2016

##### Article 217.- Commits the crime of illicit use of attributions and powers

Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016
I.-	The public servant who illicitly:
Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016

A)	Grants concessions for the provision of public service or for the exploitation, use and enjoyment of property belonging to the domain of the Federation;  
B)	Grants permits, licenses, awards or authorizations of economic content;  
Subsection reformed DOF 18-07-2016

C)	Grants franchises, exemptions, deductions or subsidies on taxes, duties, products, uses or social security contributions and quotas, in general on fiscal revenues, and on prices and tariffs of goods and services produced or provided in the Federal Public Administration;  
Subsection reformed DOF 18-07-2016

D)	Grants, carries out or contracts public works, acquisitions, leases, disposals of goods or services, with public resources;  
Subsection reformed DOF 18-07-2016

E)	Contracts debt or carries out placements of funds and securities with public resources.  
Subsection added DOF 18-07-2016

I. bis.- The public servant who knowingly of the illicitness of the act, and to the detriment of the patrimony or public service or of another person:
A)	Denies the granting or contracting of the operations referred to in this section, when all the requirements established in the applicable regulations for their granting exist, or
B)	Being responsible for administering and directly verifying compliance with the terms of a concession, permit, assignment or contract, has failed to comply with said obligation.  
Section with subsections added DOF 18-07-2016

II.- Any person who requests or promotes the improper performance, granting or contracting of the operations referred to in the preceding section or is a party thereto, and
III.- The public servant who, having public funds in his charge, gives them an application different from that for which they were intended or makes an illegal payment.  
Section reformed DOF 18-07-2016

The same sanctions provided shall be imposed on any person who knowingly of the illicitness of the act, and to the detriment of the patrimony or public service or of another person participates, requests or promotes the perpetration of any of the crimes provided for in this article.  
Paragraph added DOF 18-07-2016

Whoever commits the crime referred to in this article shall be punished with six months to twelve years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred fifty days' fine.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 28-05-2009, 18-07-2016 Reform DOF 28-05-2009: Repealed from the article the then third and fourth paragraphs
Article reformed DOF 05-01-1955, 05-01-1983

##### Article 217

Bis.- To the private individual who, in his capacity as contractor, permit holder, assignee, holder of a concession for the provision of a public service of exploitation, use or enjoyment of property of the domain of the Federation, with the purpose of obtaining a benefit for himself or for a third party:  
I.-	Generates and uses false or altered information, regarding the returns or benefits he obtains, and

II.- When being legally obligated to deliver to an authority information about the returns or benefits he obtains, conceals it.  
Whoever commits the crime referred to in this article shall be punished with three months to nine years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days' fine.  
Article added DOF 18-07-2016

#### CHAPTER V BIS

On the improper payment and receipt of remuneration of public servants
Chapter added DOF 05-11-2018

##### Article 217

Ter. In addition to administrative and political responsibilities, incurs the crime of illicit remuneration:  
I.	The public servant who approves or countersigns the payment, or who signs the voucher, check, payroll or payment order, of a remuneration, retribution, retirement, pension, retirement pay, severance payment for services rendered, loan or credit, not authorized in accordance with the provisions of the Federal Law on Remuneration of Public Servants;  
II.	Whoever receives an improper payment under the terms of the preceding section without making the report within the period indicated in article 5 of the Federal Law on Remuneration of Public Servants, having knowledge of the illicitness of the act, except when he is part of the permanent and supernumerary personnel of public entities who does not hold a middle or senior management position, as well as troop personnel and ranks of the armed forces, or in cases considered by the same article 5 of the aforementioned Law as non-serious administrative fault.  
Article added DOF 05-11-2018. Article and number reformed (formerly article 217 Bis) DOF 12-04-2019

##### Article 217

Quáter. For the commission of the crime indicated in the preceding article, the following penalties shall be imposed:  
I.	If the benefit improperly granted does not exceed the equivalent of five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, three months to two years of imprisonment and a fine of thirty to three hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed shall be imposed;  
II.	If the benefit improperly granted exceeds the equivalent of five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, but is not greater than the equivalent of two thousand times said unit, six months to three years of imprisonment and a fine of thirty to three hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed shall be imposed;  
III.	If the benefit improperly granted exceeds the equivalent of two thousand times, but is not greater than the equivalent of five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, three to five years of imprisonment and a fine of three hundred to one thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed shall be imposed, and

IV.	If the benefit improperly granted exceeds the equivalent of five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, five to twelve years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to three thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed shall be imposed.  

Dismissal and disqualification from holding another public office, employment or commission for six months to fourteen years shall also be imposed.  
Article added DOF 05-11-2018. Article and number reformed (formerly article 217 Ter) DOF 12-04-2019

#### CHAPTER VI

Extortion
Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 218

Commits the crime of extortion the public servant who in such capacity and under the title of tax or contribution, surcharge, rent, interest, salary or emolument, demands, by himself or through another, money, securities, services or any other thing that he knows is not due, or in a greater amount than that indicated by law.  
Whoever commits the crime of extortion shall be imposed the following sanctions:

When the amount or value of what is improperly demanded does not exceed the equivalent of five hundred days of Units of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, or is not valuable, three months to two years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days' fine shall be imposed.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016

When the amount or value of what is improperly demanded exceeds five hundred days of Units of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, two years to twelve years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred to one hundred fifty days' fine shall be imposed.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016 Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 05-01-1983

#### CHAPTER VII

Intimidation
Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 219.- Commits the crime of intimidation

I.- The public servant who, by himself or through an intermediary, using physical or moral violence, inhibits or intimidates any person to prevent that person or a third party from reporting, filing a complaint or providing information relating to the alleged commission of conduct sanctioned by Criminal Legislation or by the General Law of Administrative Responsibilities, and
Section reformed DOF 18-07-2016

II.- The public servant who, by reason of the complaint, report or information referred to in the preceding section, performs an unlawful conduct or omits a lawful duty that harms the interests of the persons who file or provide them, or of any third party with whom said persons have any family, business or emotional ties.  
Whoever commits the crime of intimidation shall be punished with two years to nine years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days' fine.

#### CHAPTER VIII

Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016 Article reformed DOF 03-01-1980, 05-01-1983

Abusive Exercise of Functions
Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 220.- Commits the crime of abusive exercise of functions

I.-  The public servant who, in the performance of his employment, position or commission, unlawfully grants by himself or through an intermediary, contracts, concessions, permits, licenses, authorizations, franchises, exemptions or effects purchases or sales or performs any legal act that produces economic benefits to the public servant himself, his spouse, descendant or ascendant, relatives by consanguinity or affinity up to the fourth degree, any third party with whom he has emotional, economic or direct administrative dependency ties, partners or companies of which the public servant or the aforementioned persons are part;  
Section reformed DOF 18-07-2016

II.- The public servant who, availing himself of information he possesses by reason of his employment, position or commission, whether or not it is the subject matter of his functions, and which is not public knowledge, makes by himself, or through an intermediary, investments, alienations or acquisitions, or any other act that produces an undue economic benefit to the public servant or to any of the persons mentioned in the first section.  

Whoever commits the crime of abusive exercise of functions shall be punished with the following sanctions:

When the amount to which the operations referred to in this article ascend does not exceed the equivalent of five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, three months to two years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days' fine shall be imposed.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016

When the amount to which the operations referred to in this article ascend exceeds five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, two years to twelve years of imprisonment and one hundred to one hundred fifty days' fine shall be imposed.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016 Article reformed DOF 24-03-1944, 09-03-1946. Errata DOF 16-07-1946. Reformed DOF 03-01-1980, 05-01-1983

#### CHAPTER IX

Influence Peddling
Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 221.- Commits the crime of influence peddling

I.- The public servant who, by himself or through an intermediary, promotes or manages the unlawful processing or resolution of public business unrelated to the responsibilities inherent to his employment, position or commission, and
II.- Any person who promotes the unlawful conduct of the public servant or lends himself to the promotion or management referred to in the preceding section.  
III.- The public servant who, by himself or through an intermediary, unduly solicits or promotes any resolution or the performance of any act that is the subject matter of the employment, position or commission of another public servant, which produces economic benefits for himself or for any of the persons referred to in the first section of Article 220 of this Code.  
IV.- The private individual who, without being legally authorized to intervene in a public business, affirms having influence with the public servants empowered to make decisions within said business, and intervenes with them to promote the unlawful resolution thereof, in exchange for obtaining a benefit for himself or for another.  
Section added DOF 18-07-2016

Whoever commits the crime of influence peddling shall be punished with two years to six years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days' fine.  

Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016 Article repealed DOF 03-01-1980. Added DOF 05-01-1983

#### CHAPTER X

Bribery
Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 222. Commit the crime of bribery

I.-  The public servant who, by himself or through an intermediary, unlawfully solicits or receives for himself or for another, money or any benefit, or accepts a promise, to do or refrain from performing an act proper to his functions inherent to his employment, position or commission;  
Section reformed DOF 12-03-2015, 18-07-2016

II.- Whoever gives, promises or delivers any benefit to any of the persons mentioned in article 212 of this Code, so that he may do or omit an act related to his functions, his employment, position or commission, and
Section reformed DOF 12-03-2015, 18-07-2016

III.- The federal legislator who, in the exercise of his functions or powers, and within the framework of the approval process of the respective expenditure budget, manages or requests:
a)	The allocation of resources in favor of a public entity, demanding or obtaining, for himself or for a third party, a commission, gift or consideration, in money or in kind, other than that which corresponds to him for the exercise of his office;  
b)	The granting of public works or services contracts in favor of certain natural or legal persons.  
Section with subsections added DOF 18-07-2016

The same penalty shall apply to any person who manages, requests on behalf of or representing the federal legislator the allocations of resources or granting of contracts referred to in subsections a) and b) of this article.  
Paragraph added DOF 18-07-2016

Whoever commits the crime of bribery shall be punished with the following sanctions:
When the amount or value of the gift, goods or promise does not exceed the equivalent of five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, or is not valuable, three months to two years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days' fine shall be imposed.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 23-08-2005, 18-07-2016

When the amount or value of the gift, goods, promise or consideration exceeds five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, two to fourteen years of imprisonment and one hundred to one hundred fifty days' fine shall be imposed.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 23-08-2005, 18-07-2016

In no case shall the money or gifts delivered be returned to those responsible for the crime of bribery; the same shall be applied for the benefit of the State.  
Article reformed DOF 05-01-1983

#### CHAPTER XI

Bribery of Foreign Public Servants
Chapter added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Article 222

bis.- The penalties provided in the preceding article shall be imposed on anyone who, with the purpose of obtaining or retaining for himself or for another person undue advantages in the development or conduct of international commercial transactions, offers, promises or gives, by himself or through an intermediary, money or any other gift, whether in goods or services:

I. To a foreign public servant, for his benefit or that of a third party, so that said public servant may manage or refrain from managing the processing or resolution of matters related to the functions inherent to his employment, position or commission;  
Fracción reformada DOF 23-08-2005, 12-03-2015

II. To a foreign public servant, for his benefit or that of a third party, so that said public servant may manage the processing or resolution of any matter that is outside the scope of the functions inherent to his employment, position or commission, or
Fracción reformada DOF 23-08-2005, 12-03-2015

III. To any person so that he may go before a foreign public servant and request or propose that he carry out the processing or resolution of any matter related to the functions inherent to the employment, position or commission of the latter.  

For the purposes of this article, foreign public servant means any person who holds an employment, position or commission in the legislative, executive or judicial branch or in an autonomous public body at any order or level of government of a foreign State, whether designated or elected; any person exercising a function for an authority, agency or public enterprise or state-owned enterprise of a foreign country; and any official or agent of a public international organization or body.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 23-08-2005

When any of the offenses included in this article is committed in the circumstances referred to in Article 11 of this Code, the judge shall impose on the legal entity up to one thousand days' fine and may decree its suspension or dissolution, taking into consideration the degree of knowledge of the management bodies regarding the bribery in the international transaction and the damage caused or the benefit obtained by the legal entity.

#### CHAPTER XII

Embezzlement

Párrafo reformado DOF 23-08-2005 Artículo adicionado DOF 17-05-1999

Capítulo adicionado DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 223.- Commits the crime of embezzlement

I.- Any public servant who, for his benefit or that of a third natural or legal person, diverts from its purpose money, securities, properties or any other thing belonging to the State or to a private individual, if by reason of his position he had received them in administration, in deposit, in possession or for any other cause;  
Fracción reformada DOF 18-07-2016

II.- The public servant who unlawfully uses public funds or grants any of the acts referred to in the article on unlawful use of attributions and powers with the purpose of promoting the political or social image of his person, that of his hierarchical superior or that of a third party, or in order to denigrate any person;  
Fracción reformada DOF 18-07-2016

III.- Any person who requests or accepts to carry out the promotions or denigrations referred to in the preceding section, in exchange for public funds or the enjoyment of the benefits derived from the acts referred to in the article on unlawful use of attributions and powers, and
Fracción reformada DOF 18-07-2016

IV.- Any person who, without having the character of federal public servant and being legally obligated to the custody, administration or application of federal public resources, diverts them from their purpose for his own or others' uses or gives them an application different from that for which they were intended.  

Anyone who commits the crime of embezzlement shall be imposed the following sanctions:

When the amount of what was diverted or of the funds unlawfully used does not exceed the equivalent of five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, or is not valuable, imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of thirty to one hundred days shall be imposed.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016
