When the amount of what was diverted or of the funds unlawfully used exceeds five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, imprisonment of two years to fourteen years and a fine of one hundred to one hundred fifty days shall be imposed.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

When the resources subject to embezzlement are federal contributions for public security purposes, up to one third more of the penalties indicated in the preceding paragraphs shall be applied.  
Párrafo adicionado DOF 24-06-2009 Artículo reformado DOF 05-01-1983

#### CHAPTER XIII

Illicit Enrichment
Capítulo adicionado DOF 05-01-1983

##### Article 224

Anyone who, by reason of his employment, position or commission in the public service, has incurred illicit enrichment shall be sanctioned. Illicit enrichment exists when the public servant could not prove the legitimate increase of his patrimony or the legitimate origin of the assets in his name or of those with respect to which he conducts himself as owner.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

For purposes of the preceding paragraph, there shall be computed among the assets acquired by public servants or with respect to which they conduct themselves as owners, those received by or available to their spouse and their direct economic dependents, unless the public servant proves that they obtained them by themselves.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

It shall not be illicit enrichment in case the increase of patrimony is the product of conduct that fits another hypothesis of this Title. In this case the corresponding hypothesis and sanction shall be applied, without giving rise to a concurrence of crimes.  
Párrafo adicionado DOF 18-07-2016
Anyone who commits the crime of illicit enrichment shall be imposed the following sanctions: Confiscation for the benefit of the State of those assets whose origin cannot be proven.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

When the amount to which the illicit enrichment amounts does not exceed the equivalent of five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update, imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of thirty to one hundred days shall be imposed.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

When the amount to which the illicit enrichment amounts exceeds the equivalent of five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update, imprisonment of two years to fourteen years and a fine of one hundred to one hundred fifty days shall be imposed.  

TITLE ELEVENTH

Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016 Artículo reformado DOF 05-01-1983

Crimes committed against the administration of justice
Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Decimosegundo) DOF 29-07-1970. Denominación reformada DOF 13-01-1984

#### CHAPTER I

Crimes committed by public servants
Capítulo adicionado DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 225

The following are crimes against the administration of justice, committed by public servants:  
I.-	To hear cases for which they have a legal impediment or to refrain from hearing those that correspond to them, without having a legal impediment to do so;  
II.-	To perform any other official employment or a private position or post that the law prohibits them;  III.-	To litigate on their own behalf or through an intermediary, when the law prohibits them from exercising their profession;  IV.-	To direct or advise persons who litigate before them;  
V.-	To fail to comply with a provision legally communicated to them by their competent superior, without founded cause for doing so;  
VI.-   To issue, knowingly, a substantive resolution or a final judgment that is unlawful by violating a conclusive precept of the law, or being contrary to the proceedings followed in trial or to omit issuing a procedural resolution, a substantive resolution or a lawful final judgment, within the terms provided by law;  
Section amended DOF 18-07-2016

VII.-	To execute acts or incur omissions that produce damage or grant someone undue advantages;  
VIII.-	To delay or hinder maliciously or through negligence the administration of justice;  

IX.	To unjustifiably refrain from exercising the criminal action that corresponds to a person who is detained at their disposal as accused of a crime, when it is appropriate in accordance with the Constitution and the laws of the matter, in cases where the law imposes that obligation on them; or to exercise criminal action when no complaint, accusation or charge is appropriate;  
Section amended DOF 10-01-1994, 17-06-2016

X.-	To detain an individual outside the cases indicated by law, or to retain them for longer than the time indicated in the Constitution;  

Section amended DOF 10-01-1994, 23-01-2009, 17-06-2016, 18-07-2016
XI.	Repealed.  
Section repealed DOF 17-06-2016

XII.	Repealed.  
Section amended DOF 10-01-1994, 17-06-2016. Repealed DOF 26-06-2017

XIII.-  To conceal from the accused the name of who accuses them, except in cases provided by law, not to inform them of the crime attributed to them or not to perform evidentiary disclosure in accordance with what the National Code of Criminal Procedures establishes;  
Section repealed DOF 17-06-2016. Added DOF 18-07-2016

XIV.	To prolong pretrial detention for longer than the maximum time the law sets for the crime that motivates the proceeding;  
Section amended DOF 17-06-2016

XV.-	To impose taxes or contributions in any places of detention or confinement;  

XVI.	To unjustifiably delay compliance with judicial resolutions, in which it is ordered to release a detainee;  
Section amended DOF 17-06-2016

XVII.-  To fail to issue an order for binding over for trial or for release of a detainee, within seventy-two hours following their being placed at their disposal, unless the accused has requested an extension of the term, in which case the new term shall apply;  
Section amended DOF 10-01-1994, 17-06-2016, 18-07-2016

XVIII.-	To order or conduct searches or home visits outside the cases authorized by law;  
XIX.	To open criminal proceedings against a public servant with immunity, without having previously removed it, in accordance with the provisions of the law;  
Section amended DOF 17-06-2016

XX.-   To order the apprehension of an individual for a crime that does not merit a custodial sentence, or in cases where no complaint, accusation or charge precedes; or to carry out the apprehension without placing the detainee at the disposal of the judge within the term indicated by article 16 of the Constitution;  
Section amended DOF 10-01-1994, 18-07-2016

XXI.   To those in charge of or employees of penitentiary centers who charge any amount to the accused, sentenced persons or their families, in exchange for providing them with goods or services that the State provides free of charge to grant them privileged conditions in accommodation, food or regime;  
Section amended DOF 17-06-2016

XXII.-	To auction, in their own favor, directly or through an intermediary, the goods subject to an auction in whose trial they have intervened;  
XXIII.-	To admit or appoint a depositary or deliver to them the seized goods, without compliance with the corresponding legal requirements;  
XXIV.-	To unlawfully warn the defendant regarding the attachment order decreed against them;  
Section amended DOF 18-07-2016

XXV.- To appoint as trustee or receiver in a bankruptcy or insolvency, a person who is a debtor, relative or who has been a lawyer of the bankrupt, or a person who has a kinship relationship with the official, close friendship or is linked with them by business of common interest; and

XXVI.-  To permit, outside the cases provided by law, the temporary departure of persons who are confined.  
Section amended DOF 17-11-1986

XXVII. To fail to order the release of an accused, decreeing their binding over for trial, when they are accused of a crime or modality that has a non-custodial or alternative sentence;  
Section added DOF 10-01-1994. Amended DOF 17-05-1999, 06-06-2006, 17-06-2016

XXVIII.- To disclose to those who do not have the right, documents, records or information contained in an investigation file or in a criminal proceeding and that by provision of law or resolution of the judicial authority, are reserved or confidential;  
Section added DOF 17-05-1999. Amended DOF 06-06-2006, 17-06-2016, 18-07-2016

XXIX.	Repealed.  
Section added DOF 06-06-2006. Repealed DOF 23-01-2009
XXX.	To retain the accused without complying with the requirements established by the Constitution and the respective laws;  
Section added DOF 23-01-2009. Amended DOF 17-06-2016

XXXI.	To alter, modify, conceal, destroy, lose or disturb the scene of the events or of the discovery, indications, evidence, objects, instruments or products related to a criminal act or the chain of custody procedure;  
Section added DOF 23-01-2009. Amended DOF 14-06-2012, 17-06-2016

XXXII.-	To divert or obstruct the investigation of the criminal act in question or to favor the accused evading the action of justice;  
Section added DOF 23-01-2009. Amended DOF 14-06-2012, 17-06-2016, 18-07-2016

XXXIII.	To compel a person or their representative to grant pardon in crimes that are prosecuted by complaint; and
Section added DOF 14-06-2012

XXXIV.	To compel a person to resign from their position or employment to avoid responding to accusations of harassment, or to conceal violations of the Federal Labor Law.  
Section added DOF 14-06-2012

XXXV.	To anyone exercising functions of probation supervisor or by reason of them makes threats, harasses or exercises violence against the prosecuted person, sentenced person, their family and possessions;  
Section added DOF 16-06-2016

XXXVI.	To anyone exercising functions of probation supervisor who unduly requires favors, actions or any transfer of goods from the prosecuted person, sentenced person or their family;  
Section added DOF 16-06-2016

XXXVII.	To anyone exercising functions of probation supervisor who falsifies reports to the Execution Judge.  
Section added DOF 16-06-2016

Whoever commits the crimes provided in sections I, II, III, VII, VIII, IX, XX, XXIV, XXV, XXVI, XXXIII and XXXIV, shall be imposed a prison sentence of three to eight years and thirty to one thousand one hundred days' fine.  
Paragraph amended DOF 17-05-1999, 14-06-2012, 18-07-2016

Whoever commits the crimes provided in sections IV, V, VI, X, XI, XIII, XIV, XV, XVI, XVII, XVIII, XIX, XXI, XXII, XXIII, XXVII, XXVIII, XXX, XXXI, XXXII, XXXV, XXXVI and XXXVII shall be imposed a prison sentence of
four to ten years and one hundred to one hundred fifty days' fine.  
Paragraph amended DOF 17-05-1999, 06-06-2006, 23-01-2009, 16-06-2016, 18-07-2016

In the case of private individuals performing functions proper to the probation supervisor, and independently of the individual criminal responsibility of workers or administrators, the organization may be subject to the penalties and measures regarding criminal liability of legal persons stipulated in this Code.  
Paragraph added DOF 16-06-2016 Amendment DOF 18-07-2016: Repealed from the article the then fourth paragraph (previously amended DOF 17-05-1999)
Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

#### CHAPTER II

Improper exercise of one's own right
Chapter added DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 226

Whoever employs violence to enforce a right or purported right that he must exercise, shall be subject to imprisonment of three months to one year or a fine of 30 to 90 days. In these cases, proceedings shall only be initiated upon complaint by the offended party.  
Article reformed DOF 13-01-1984, 30-12-1991

##### Article 227

The foregoing provisions shall apply to all officials or employees of the public administration, when in the exercise of their duties they commit the acts or incur the omissions expressed in the said articles.  
TITLE TWELFTH
Professional Liability
Title moved DOF 20-01-1967. Moved (formerly Title Thirteenth) DOF 29-07-1970

#### CHAPTER I

General Provisions
Name of Chapter reformed DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 228

Professionals, artists or technicians and their assistants shall be liable for crimes they commit in the exercise of their profession, under the following terms and without prejudice to the provisions contained in the General Health Law or other rules on professional practice, as applicable:
Paragraph reformed DOF 13-01-1984, 14-01-1985

I.-  In addition to the sanctions established for crimes that are consummated, whether intentional or negligent, they shall be subject to suspension from one month to two years in the exercise of the profession or permanently in case of recidivism; and
Section reformed DOF 10-01-1994

II.-	They shall be obligated to repair the damage for their own acts and for those of their assistants, when the latter act in accordance with the instructions of the former.  
Section reformed DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 229

The preceding article shall apply to physicians who, having granted responsibility to take charge of the care of an injured or sick person, abandon them in their treatment without justified cause, and without giving immediate notice to the corresponding authority.

##### Article 230

Imprisonment of three months to two years, up to one hundred days fine and suspension of three months to one year at the discretion of the judge shall be imposed on directors, managers or administrators of any health center, when they incur in any of the following cases:  
I.-	Prevent the departure of a patient, when the patient or their relatives request it, alleging debts of any kind;  
II.-	Retain a newborn without necessity, for the reasons referred to in the final part of the preceding section;  
III.-	Delay or refuse for any reason the delivery of a corpse, except when an order from competent authority is required.  

The same sanction shall be imposed on managers or administrators of funeral agencies who unduly delay or refuse the delivery of a corpse, and likewise on managers, employees or dependents of a pharmacy, who when filling a prescription substitute the medicine specifically prescribed for another that causes harm or is evidently inappropriate for the ailment for which it was prescribed.  
Article reformed DOF 13-01-1984

#### CHAPTER II

Crimes of lawyers, counsels and litigants

##### Article 231

Imprisonment of two to six years, a fine of one hundred to three hundred days and suspension and disqualification for a term equal to that of the penalty previously indicated for exercising the profession shall be imposed on lawyers, counsels, or litigants who are not ostensibly represented by lawyers, when they commit any of the following crimes:
Errata to paragraph DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 10-01-1994

I.-	Knowingly allege false facts, or nonexistent or repealed laws; and

II.-  Request terms to prove what notoriously cannot be proven or will not benefit their party; promote articles or incidents that cause the suspension of trial or manifestly inadmissible appeals or in any other manner seek delays that are notoriously illegal.  

III.-  Knowingly and based on false or worthless documents or false witnesses, bring an action or raise defenses against another, before judicial or administrative authorities; and
Section added DOF 10-01-1994

IV.-  Simulate a legal act or a judicial act or writing, or alter elements of evidence and present them in trial, with the purpose of obtaining a judgment, resolution or administrative act contrary to law.  
Section added DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 232

In addition to the penalties mentioned, imprisonment of three months to three years may be imposed.  

I.- For representing or assisting various contenders or parties with opposing interests, in the same matter or in related matters, or when representation of one is accepted and that of the opposing party is subsequently admitted;  

II.- For abandoning the defense of a client or matter without justified reason and causing harm, and

III.- To the defender of an accused, whether private or court-appointed, who only limits himself to accepting the position and requesting the bail release mentioned in section I of article 20 of the Constitution, without promoting further evidence or directing his defense.

##### Article 233

Court-appointed defenders who without foundation do not promote the relevant evidence in defense of the accused who appoint them, shall be removed from their position. For this purpose, judges shall communicate the respective failures to the Chief of Defenders.  
Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931

TITLE THIRTEENTH
Falsity
Title moved DOF 20-01-1967. Moved (formerly Title Fourteenth) DOF 29-07-1970

#### CHAPTER I

Counterfeiting, alteration and destruction of currency
Name of Chapter reformed DOF 11-06-1992

##### Article 234

Whoever commits the crime of counterfeiting currency shall be subject to imprisonment of five to twelve years and up to five hundred days fine.  
For the purposes of this Chapter, currency is understood to mean bills and metal pieces, domestic or foreign, that have legal tender in the issuing country.  
Whoever produces, stores, distributes or introduces into national territory any document or piece that contains images or other elements used in circulating currencies, and that are therefore suitable to deceive the public, by being confusable with legally issued currencies, commits the crime of counterfeiting currency. Whoever commits this crime in the degree of attempt shall be subject to imprisonment of four to eight years and up to three hundred days fine.  

The penalty indicated in the first paragraph of this article shall also be imposed on whoever knowingly makes use of counterfeit currency.  
Article reformed DOF 11-06-1992

##### Article 235.- Imprisonment of one to five years and up to five hundred days fine shall be imposed

I.-  On whoever produces, stores or distributes pieces of paper with size similar to or the same as bills, when such pieces present some of the images or elements contained in those, resulting in pieces with the appearance of bills;  
II.-  On whoever marks currency with legends, seals, dies or in any other manner, that are not removable to disseminate messages directed to the public.  
III.- On whoever permits the use or carries out the alienation, by any means and title, of machines, instruments or tools that can only serve for the manufacture of currency, to persons not legally authorized for it.  
Article reformed DOF 11-06-1992

##### Article 236

Imprisonment of five to twelve years and up to five hundred days fine shall be imposed on whoever alters currency. The same sanction shall be imposed on whoever knowingly circulates altered currency.  
For the purposes of this article, it is understood that whoever forms pieces by joining two or more fractions from different bills alters a bill, and that whoever diminishes the content of gold, silver, platinum or palladium that compose legal tender metal pieces, by means of filings, clippings, dissolution in acids or using any other means, alters a metal currency.  
Article reformed DOF 11-06-1992

##### Article 237

Imprisonment of five to twelve years and up to five hundred days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who provides their services or holds a position or commission in the mint or in any company that manufactures coin blanks, and who by any means causes coins of gold, silver, platinum or palladium to contain metal other than that specified by law, or to have less weight than the legal weight or an alloy fineness lower than required.  
Article amended DOF 11-06-1992

##### Article 238

Imprisonment of three to seven years and up to five hundred days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who unlawfully takes advantage of the metallic content by destroying coins in circulation through their melting or any other procedure.  
Article amended DOF 09-03-1946, 11-06-1992

#### CHAPTER II

Falsification and improper use of bearer instruments, public credit documents and documents relating to credit
Chapter relocated with amended denomination DOF 11-06-1992. Denomination amended DOF 08-02-1999

##### Article 239

Anyone who commits the crime of falsification of bearer instruments and public credit documents shall be subject to imprisonment of four to ten years and a fine of two hundred fifty to three thousand pesos.  
The crime referred to in the preceding paragraph is committed by anyone who falsifies:  
I.-  Bonds or other public credit documents of the treasury, or the interest or dividend coupons of those instruments.  
II.-  Public debt bonds of another nation, interest or dividend coupons of these instruments.  
III.- Bonds and other instruments legally issued by companies or enterprises or by the public administrations of the Federation, the States or any Municipality, and the interest or dividend coupons of the documents mentioned.  
Article amended DOF 09-03-1946

##### Article 240

Anyone who introduces into the Republic or puts into circulation therein the falsified documents referred to in the preceding article shall be subject to the penalty specified therein.  
Article amended DOF 09-03-1946, 11-06-1992

##### Article 240 Bis.- Repealed

Article added DOF 08-02-1999. Repealed DOF 26-06-2008

#### CHAPTER III

Falsification of seals, keys, dies or stamps, marks, weights and measures

##### Article 241

Imprisonment of four to nine years and a fine of four hundred to two thousand pesos shall be imposed:  
I.-	On anyone who falsifies official seals or marks;  

II.-	On anyone who falsifies the hallmarks for marking the fineness of gold or silver;  

III.- On anyone who falsifies the dies or stamps intended for manufacturing coins or the seal, mark or countersign that any authority uses to identify any object or to secure payment of any tax;  

IV.- On anyone who falsifies the punches, matrices, plates or any other object used for the manufacture of shares, bonds, coupons or bills referred to in article 239, and
V.-	On anyone who falsifies the inspection marks of weights and measures.  

The penalty corresponding to the crime provided for in section I shall be increased by up to one half when seals of assignees, contractors, permittees or distributors referred to in the Federal Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes Committed in Matters of Hydrocarbons are falsified.  
Paragraph added DOF 12-01-2016

##### Article 242

Imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of twenty to one thousand pesos shall be imposed:

I.-  On anyone who falsifies keys, the seal of a private individual, a seal, mark, stamp or countersign of a commercial house, a bank or an industrial establishment; or a ticket or token for a public spectacle;  
II.-	On anyone who falsifies in the Republic the seals, hallmarks or marks of a foreign nation;  

III.-	On anyone who sells a false seal, hallmark or mark, concealing this defect;  
IV.- On anyone who, in order to defraud another, alters legitimate weights and measures or removes the true marks from them and transfers them to false weights or measures, or makes use of the latter.  
V.-	On anyone who falsifies national or foreign adhesive seals;  
VI.- On anyone who causes to disappear any of the seals referred to in the preceding section or the indicator mark showing that it has already been used;  
VII.- On anyone who, procuring the true seals, hallmarks, marks, etc., makes improper use of them; and
VIII.- On anyone who knowingly makes use of the seals or any other of the false objects referred to in the preceding article and sections I, II, V and VI of this one.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 09-03-1946

##### Article 242

Bis.- Imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of two hundred to two thousand pesos shall be imposed on anyone who in any way alters the signs, blood marks or fire brands used to distinguish livestock, without authorization from the person who has them legally registered with the competent authority.  
Article added DOF 20-01-1967

#### CHAPTER IV

Falsification of documents in general

##### Article 243

The crime of falsification shall be punished, in the case of public documents, with imprisonment of four to eight years and one hundred eighty to three hundred sixty days' fine. In the case of private documents, with imprisonment of six months to five years and one hundred eighty to three hundred sixty days' fine.  
If the person who carries out the falsification is a public servant, the applicable penalty shall be increased by up to one half more.  

The penalty corresponding to the crime provided for in the first paragraph shall be increased by up to one half when documents of assignees, contractors, permittees or distributors referred to in the Federal Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes Committed in Matters of Hydrocarbons are falsified.  
Paragraph added DOF 12-01-2016 Article amended DOF 30-12-1991, 13-05-1996

##### Article 244

The crime of falsification of documents is committed by any of the following means:  
I.- Placing a false signature or rubric, even if imaginary, or altering a true one;  

II.- Improperly taking advantage of another person's blank signature or rubric, extending an obligation, release or any other document that may compromise the property, honor, person or reputation of another, or cause harm to society, the State or a third party;  
III.- Altering the context of a true document, after it has been completed and signed, if this changes its meaning regarding any circumstance or substantial point, whether by adding, amending or erasing, in whole or in part, one or more words or clauses, or by varying the punctuation;  
IV.- Varying the date or any other circumstance relating to the time of execution of the act expressed in the document;  
V.- The person executing the document attributing to himself, or attributing to the person on whose behalf he does so: a name or an investiture, quality or circumstance that he does not have and that is necessary for the validity of the act;  
VI.- Drafting a document in terms that change the agreement entered into for a different one in which the declaration or disposition of the grantor, the obligations he intended to contract, or the rights he should have acquired are varied;  
Errata notice to the section DOF 31-08-1931

VII.- Adding or altering clauses or declarations, or recording false facts as true, or as confessed those that are not, if the document in which they are recorded is executed to record them and as proof of them;  
Errata notice to the section DOF 31-08-1931

VIII.- Issuing a supposed testimony of documents that do not exist; giving it of another existing one that lacks the legal requirements, falsely supposing that it has them; or of another that does not lack them, but adding or suppressing in the copy something that constitutes a substantial variation, and

IX.- An expert translator or paleographer altering the content of a document when translating or deciphering it.  

X.- Producing plates, badges, insignia, documents or any other official identification, without having the authorization of the corresponding authority.  
Section added DOF 23-12-1985

##### Article 245

For the crime of falsification of documents to be punishable as such, the following requirements must be met:  
I.- That the falsifier intends to obtain some benefit for himself or for another, or to cause harm to society, the State or a third party;  
II.- That harm results or may result to society, the State or an individual, whether in the latter's property or in his person, in his honor or in his reputation, and
Errata notice to the section DOF 31-08-1931

III.- That the falsifier makes the falsification without the consent of the person to whom harm results or may result or without that of the person in whose name the document was made.

##### Article 246.- Also incurs the penalty specified in article 243

I.- The official or employee who, by deceit or surprise, causes someone to sign a public document that he would not have signed knowing its content;  
II.- The Notary and any other public official who, in the exercise of his functions, issues a certification of facts that are not true, or attests to what does not appear in files, registers, protocols or documents;  
Errata notice to the section DOF 31-08-1931

III.- He who, to exempt himself from a service legally due, or from an obligation imposed by law, supposes a certification of illness or impediment that he does not have as issued by a medical surgeon, whether the person to whom he attributes it actually exists, whether this person is imaginary or whether he takes the name of a real person, falsely attributing to them the quality of doctor or surgeon;  
IV.- The doctor who falsely certifies that a person has an illness or other impediment sufficient to excuse him from providing a service required by law, or from fulfilling an obligation that it imposes, or to acquire some right;  
Errata notice to the section DOF 31-08-1931

V.- He who makes use of a true certification issued for another, as if it had been issued in his favor, or alters the one issued to him;  
VI.- Those in charge of the telegraph, telephone or radio service who suppose or falsify a dispatch of that kind;  
Section reformed DOF 07-04-2016

VII.- The certification service provider who performs activities without having the respective accreditation, under the terms established by the Commercial Code and other applicable provisions, and
Section added DOF 07-04-2016

VIII.- He who knowingly makes use of a false document or of a copy, transcription or testimony thereof, whether public or private.  
Section reformed DOF 15-01-1951. Renumbered DOF 07-04-2016

#### CHAPTER V

Falsehood in judicial declarations and in reports given to an authority

##### Article 247

Four to eight years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days fine shall be imposed:
Paragraph reformed DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994. Errata notice DOF 01-02-1994. Reformed DOF 20-06-2008, 23-01-2009

I.- On he who, questioned by any public authority other than the judicial in the exercise of its functions or on account of them, fails to tell the truth.  
II. Repealed.  
Section reformed DOF 10-01-1994. Repealed DOF 23-01-2009

III.- On he who bribes a witness, an expert or an interpreter, so that he produces falsely in trial or obliges or commits him to do so by intimidating him or otherwise;  
IV.- On he who, in accordance with law, with any character except that of witness, is examined and fails to tell the truth to the detriment of another, denying that the signature with which he signed the document is his or affirming a false fact or altering or denying a true one, or its substantial circumstances.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 10-01-1994

What is provided in this section does not include cases in which the party is examined about the amount at which he estimates a thing or when he has the character of accused;  
Reform DOF 05-01-1955: Deleted from the section the then third paragraph, whose content passed to article 248

V.- On he who in amparo proceedings renders reports as responsible authority, in which he affirms a falsehood or denies the truth in whole or in part.  
Section added DOF 05-01-1955 Article reformed DOF 09-03-1946

##### Article 247

Bis.- Five to twelve years of imprisonment and three hundred to five hundred days fine shall be imposed:
On he who, examined by the judicial authority as a witness or expert, fails to tell the truth about the fact being investigated, or aspects, quantities, qualities or other circumstances that are relevant to establish the meaning of an opinion or expert report, whether by affirming, denying or maliciously concealing the existence of any data that may serve as proof of the truth or falsehood of the main fact, or that increases or decreases its gravity, or that serves to establish the nature or particularities of a technical or scientific order that matter for the authority to pronounce a resolution on the matter questioned in the matter where the testimony or expert opinion is given.  

When the convicted person is imposed a penalty of more than twenty years of imprisonment for the false testimony or expert report, the sanction shall be eight to fifteen years of imprisonment and five hundred to eight hundred days fine.  
Article added DOF 23-01-2009

##### Article 248

The witness, expert or interpreter who spontaneously retracts his false declarations rendered before any administrative authority or before the judicial before a sentence is pronounced in the instance in which he gave them, shall only pay 30 to 180 days fine, but if he fails to tell the truth when retracting his declarations, the sanction that corresponds shall be applied to him in accordance with what is provided in this chapter, considering him as a repeat offender.  
Article reformed DOF 05-01-1955, 30-12-1991

##### Article 248

Bis.- Anyone who, with the purpose of incriminating someone as responsible for a crime before the authority, simulates against them the existence of material evidence that would presume their responsibility, shall be subject to imprisonment of two to six years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred days.  
Article added DOF 10-01-1994

#### CHAPTER VI

Variation of name or domicile

##### Article 249

A penalty of ten to one hundred eighty days of community service shall be imposed on:
Paragraph amended DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

I.-   Anyone who conceals their name or surname and takes an imaginary one or that of another person, when declaring before a judicial authority;  
II.-  Anyone who, in order to evade the performance of a judicial proceeding or a notification of any kind or summons from an authority, conceals their domicile, or designates a different one or denies in any way the true one, and
III.- Any public official or employee who, in acts proper to their office, attributes to a person a title or name knowing that it does not belong to them.

#### CHAPTER VII

Usurpation of public functions or profession and improper use of decorations, uniforms, hierarchical ranks, insignia, badges and acronyms
Chapter title amended DOF 21-01-1985

##### Article 250

Anyone who does the following shall be punished with imprisonment of one to six years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred days:
Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994

I.-  Anyone who, without being a public official, attributes such character to themselves and exercises any of the functions thereof;  
II.- Anyone who, without having a professional degree or authorization to exercise a regulated profession, issued by authorities or organisms legally empowered to do so, in accordance with the regulatory provisions of constitutional article 5.  
Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994
a).-	Attributes to themselves the character of a professional.  

b).-	Performs acts proper to a professional activity, with the exception of what is provided in the 3rd paragraph of article 26 of the Regulatory Law of constitutional articles 4 and 5.  
c).-	Publicly offers their services as a professional.  

d).-	Uses a degree or authorization to exercise a professional activity without having the right to do so.  
e).-	With the purpose of profiting, joins legally authorized professionals for the purpose of professional practice or administers a professional association.  
III.-	Any foreigner who exercises a regulated profession without having authorization from a competent authority or after the expiration of the period that the authority had granted them.  

IV.- Anyone who uses credentials of a public servant, decorations, uniforms, hierarchical ranks, insignia, badges or acronyms to which they have no right. The penalty may be increased up to half of its duration and amount when they are for the exclusive use of the Mexican Armed Forces or any police force.  
Section amended DOF 21-01-1985, 10-01-1994 Article amended DOF 30-01-1947

##### Article 250

bis.- Anyone who commits the crime of falsification of uniforms and insignia of the armed forces or of any public security institution shall be subject to five to twelve years of imprisonment and up to five hundred days fine.  
Anyone who, without authorization from the corresponding institution, manufactures, makes, produces, prints or paints any of the uniforms, insignia, identification credentials, medals, insignia, badges, shields, documents, stickers, emblems or pieces containing images, acronyms or other elements used in said institutions, commits the crime of falsification of uniforms and insignia of the armed forces or of any public security institution.  

For the purposes of this article, uniforms, insignia or badges are understood to be those indicated in the applicable provisions of the armed forces or of any public security institution.  
Article added DOF 24-06-2009

##### Article 250

bis 1.- A penalty of one to six years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days fine shall be imposed on anyone who:  
I.-  Stores, distributes, possesses or introduces into national territory falsified uniforms or insignia of the armed forces or of any public security institution;  
II.-  Acquires, alienates or uses by any means or title, falsified uniforms, insignia, markings, identification credentials or badges of the armed forces or any public security institution;  
III.- Obtains, keeps, facilitates or alienates without authorization the true uniforms or insignia of the armed forces or of any public security institution;  
IV.- When vehicles are used with markings, colors, equipment, original, falsified or with such appearance that they resemble vehicles used by the armed forces or public security institutions, and
V.- Anyone who uses uniforms, markings, badges, identification credentials and insignia with a size similar to or equal to the regulatory size of the armed forces or public security institutions, when said pieces, without being a copy of the original, present some of the images or elements contained in those, resulting in objects or pieces with similar appearance, confusable with those legally issued, with the purpose of passing themselves off as a public servant.  

A penalty of five to twelve years of imprisonment and two hundred to six hundred days fine shall be imposed on anyone who performs any of the conducts provided in this article with the purpose of committing a crime or when the active subject is or has been a public servant of the armed forces or of any public security institution.  
For the purposes of this article, uniforms, badges, identification credentials, medals, insignia, markings, badges, shields, documents, stickers, emblems or pieces containing

images, acronyms or other elements used in institutions of the armed forces or public security or administration of justice are understood to be those indicated in the applicable provisions of said institutions and only those that are acquired, distributed or alienated by persons authorized to do so or to whom the respective contract has been awarded by the competent institution, in accordance with applicable provisions, shall be considered authentic.  
Article added DOF 24-06-2009

#### CHAPTER VIII

Provisions common to the preceding chapters

##### Article 251

If the forger makes use of the false documents or objects detailed in this title, the falsification and the crime that the offender had committed by means of it shall be accumulated.

##### Article 252

The provisions contained in this title shall not apply except insofar as they are not provided for in special laws or do not conflict with what is established in them.  
TITLE FOURTEENTH
Crimes against the public economy
Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Fifteenth) DOF 29-07-1970

#### CHAPTER I

Crimes against consumption and National Wealth
Chapter title amended DOF 05-01-1955

##### Article 253

The following acts or omissions that seriously affect national consumption shall be punished with imprisonment of three to ten years and with a fine of two hundred to one thousand days:
Paragraph reformed DOF 13-05-1996

I.- Those related to articles of necessary or widespread consumption or to the raw materials necessary to produce them, as well as to raw materials essential for the activity of national industry, consisting of:
a).- Hoarding, concealment or unjustified refusal to sell, with the purpose of obtaining a price increase or affecting supply to consumers.  
b).- Any act or procedure that prevents or hinders, or is intended to prevent or hinder, free competition in production or commerce.  
c).- The limitation of production or the manipulation thereof, with the purpose of maintaining goods at an unjust price.  

d)	(Repealed)
Subsection repealed DOF 10-05-2011
e).- The suspension of production, processing, distribution, offer or sale of goods or the provision of services, carried out by industrialists, merchants, producers, entrepreneurs or service providers, with the purpose of obtaining a price increase or affecting consumer supply.  
If the unlawful conduct is abandoned within two business days following the moment when the competent administrative authority requires it, the applicable penalty shall be six months to three years of imprisonment, or one hundred to five hundred days fine;  
Paragraph reformed DOF 13-05-1996

f).- Exportation, without permission from the competent authority when such permission is necessary in accordance with applicable legal provisions.  
g).- Sale with immoderate profit, by producers, distributors or merchants in general. In cases where the undue profit is less than the equivalent of sixty days of the general minimum wage in force in the region and at the time where the crime is consummated, it shall be punished with imprisonment of two to six years and sixty to three hundred days fine;  
Section reformed DOF 13-05-1996

h).- Diverting, for different uses, goods that have been supplied for a specific purpose, by a public entity or its distributors, when the price at which the goods were delivered is lower than what it would have if destined for other uses.  
i).- Preventing or attempting to prevent the generation, transmission, transformation, distribution or sale of public service electric energy.  
Subsection added DOF 13-05-1996
j)	Repealed.  
Subsection added DOF 17-05-1999. Repealed DOF 12-01-2016

II.- Packaging or packing goods intended for sale, in a quantity less than that indicated as net content and outside the respective tolerance or without indicating on the packages the maximum official retail price, when there is an obligation to do so.  
III.- Deliberately and repeatedly delivering, when measurement is made at the time of the transaction, goods in quantities less than those agreed upon.  
IV.- Altering or reducing by any means the properties that goods or products should have.  
V.	Reselling to a public organization, at guaranteed minimum prices or at prices authorized by the Ministry of Economy, agricultural, maritime, river and lake products acquired at a lower price. The same penalty shall apply to the employee or official of the public organization who knowingly purchases them in that situation or causes the producer to be forced to sell to third parties at lower prices.  
Section reformed DOF 09-04-2012

In any of the cases mentioned in the preceding sections, the judge may also order the suspension for up to one year or the dissolution of the company of which the offender is a member or representative, if the circumstances mentioned in Article 11 of this Code are present.  
In the cases of subsections a), f) and h), of section I and of section IV of this article, the authority having knowledge of the facts shall immediately proceed to deposit the articles of necessary or widespread consumption, the raw materials to produce them or the raw materials essential for national industrial activity. The deposit shall be made in a general warehouse that is a national auxiliary credit organization and the goods shall be generically designated, in the terms of Article 281 of the General Law of Negotiable Instruments and Credit Operations; when dealing with goods whose special nature does not allow generic deposit, specific deposit shall be established, also indicating the term and conditions under which their sale or destruction shall proceed in accordance with the provisions of Article 282 of the same Law. The deposit certificate issued shall be non-negotiable and shall be sent to the Public Prosecutor or, as applicable, to the Judge hearing the case, for appropriate purposes.  

The provisions of this article shall apply without prejudice to the administrative measures and sanctions established by the corresponding laws.  
Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 31-12-1952, 05-01-1955, 05-12-1979

##### Article 253 Bis.- (Repealed)

Article added DOF 05-01-1955. Repealed DOF 05-12-1979

##### Article 254.- The penalties of Article 253 shall also apply

Paragraph reformed DOF 05-12-1979, 30-12-1980

I.-	For improper destruction of raw materials, agricultural or industrial products or means of production, done to the detriment of national consumption;  
Section reformed DOF 13-12-1996

II.-	When the spread of a disease of plants or animals is caused with danger to the rural economy;  
Section reformed DOF 13-12-1996

III.- When false, exaggerated or biased news is published, or by any other improper means disturbances are produced in the domestic market, whether regarding goods, currencies or commercial instruments and securities.  
IV.- To whoever deliberately, in commercial operations, exports national goods of inferior quality, or in lesser quantity than agreed.  
Section added DOF 05-01-1955

V.- To whoever deliberately acquires, possesses or traffics in seeds, fertilizers, pesticides, implements and other materials intended for agricultural production that have been delivered to producers by a public entity or agency at a subsidized price.  
In irrigation districts, irrigation water shall be considered as subsidized material.  
If the person who delivers the referred inputs is the producer who received them from official institutions, a penalty of 3 days to 3 years of imprisonment shall be applied.  
Section added DOF 30-12-1980

VI.- Officials or employees of any public entity or agency who deliver these inputs to those who are not entitled to receive them; or who improperly deny or delay delivery to those entitled to receive them, shall be subject to the penalties of Article 253.  
Section added DOF 30-12-1980
VII.-	Repealed.  
Section added DOF 13-05-1996. Reformed DOF 17-05-1999, 24-10-2011. Repealed DOF 12-01-2016

VIII.- Repealed.  
Section added DOF 24-10-2011. Repealed DOF 12-01-2016

IX.	To whoever without right carries out any removal or alteration of equipment or installations of the public electric energy service.  
Section added DOF 13-05-1996. Renumbered DOF 24-10-2011

The penalties corresponding to the crimes provided for in this article shall be increased by one-half for the worker or public servant who, by reason of their work, provides information about the installations, equipment or operation of the industry that proves useful or may assist in the commission of the crimes in question.  
Paragraph added DOF 24-10-2011

##### Article 254

bis. Whoever celebrates, orders or executes contracts, agreements, arrangements or combinations among economic agents that compete with each other, whose object or effect is any of the following, shall be punished with imprisonment of five to ten years and with one thousand to ten thousand days of fine:  
I. To fix, raise, concert or manipulate the sale or purchase price of goods or services at which they are offered or demanded in the markets;  
II. To establish the obligation not to produce, process, distribute, commercialize or acquire except only a restricted or limited quantity of goods or the provision or transaction of a restricted or limited number, volume or frequency of services;  
III. To divide, distribute, assign or impose portions or segments of an actual or potential market of goods and services, through clientele, suppliers, determined or determinable times or spaces;  
IV. To establish, concert or coordinate bids or abstention in tenders, contests, auctions or public sales, and
V. To exchange information with any of the objects or effects referred to in the preceding sections.  
The crime provided for in this article shall be prosecuted upon complaint of the Federal Economic Competition Commission or the Federal Telecommunications Institute, as applicable, which may only be filed with the opinion of probable responsibility, in the terms provided in the Federal Economic Competition Law.  

There shall be no criminal liability for economic agents who avail themselves of the benefit referred to in article 103 of the Federal Economic Competition Law, upon resolution of the Federal Economic Competition Commission or the Federal Telecommunications Institute determining that they comply with the terms established in said provision and other applicable ones.  

Proceedings for this crime may be dismissed at the request of the Plenary of the Federal Economic Competition Commission or the Federal Telecommunications Institute, when the defendants comply with the administrative sanctions imposed and, in addition, the requirements provided in the technical criteria issued by the Federal Economic Competition Commission or the Federal Telecommunications Institute are met.  

The criminal action shall prescribe within a period equal to the arithmetic mean of the custodial sentence referred to in the first paragraph of this article.  
Article added DOF 30-12-1991. Repealed DOF 13-12-1996. Added DOF 10-05-2011. Amended DOF 23-05-2014

##### Article 254

bis 1. Whoever, by themselves or through an intermediary, during the practice of a verification visit, by any means alters, destroys or disturbs in whole or in part documents, images or electronic files containing information or data, with the object of diverting, obstructing or impeding the investigation of a possible criminal act or the practice of the administrative proceeding, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three years and with five hundred to five thousand days of fine.  
Article added DOF 23-05-2014

##### Article 254

Ter.- Whoever, without right, obstructs or impedes in whole or in part, the access or operation of any of the equipment, facilities or real property affected to the petroleum industry referred to in the Regulatory Law of Constitutional Article 27 in the Petroleum Sector or of the equipment, facilities or real property affected to the public electric energy service, shall be punished with three months to one year of imprisonment or one hundred to three hundred days of fine.  

If the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph cause any damage, the penalty shall be two to nine years of imprisonment and two hundred fifty to two thousand days of fine.  
Article added DOF 13-05-1996

Vagrants and evildoers

TITLE FIFTEENTH

Crimes against Freedom and Normal Psychosexual Development
Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Sixteenth) DOF 29-07-1970. Denomination amended DOF 21-01-1991

#### Chapter I

Sexual Harassment, Sexual Abuse, Statutory Rape and Rape
Denomination of Chapter amended DOF 21-01-1991

##### Article 259

Bis.- Whoever with lascivious purposes repeatedly harasses a person of any sex, availing themselves of their hierarchical position derived from their labor, teaching, domestic or any other relationship that implies subordination, shall be imposed a sanction of up to eight hundred days of fine. If the harasser were a public servant and used the means or circumstances that the position provides, in addition to the penalties indicated, they shall be removed from office and may be disqualified from holding any other public office for up to one year.  
Paragraph amended DOF 15-06-2018

Sexual harassment shall only be punishable when it causes harm or damage.  

Proceedings shall only be brought against the harasser at the request of the offended party.  
Article added DOF 21-01-1991

##### Article 260

Whoever executes on a person, without their consent, or compels them to execute for themselves or on another person, sexual acts without the purpose of reaching copulation, commits the crime of sexual abuse.  

Whoever commits this crime shall be imposed a penalty of six to ten years of imprisonment and up to two hundred days of fine.  
For the purposes of this article, sexual acts are understood to be obscene bodily touching or fondling, or those that represent explicitly sexual acts or compel the victim to represent them.  

It is also considered sexual abuse when the victim is compelled to observe a sexual act, or to exhibit their body without their consent.  
If physical or psychological violence were used, the penalty shall be increased by one half more in its minimum and maximum.  
Article amended DOF 03-01-1989, 21-01-1991, 30-12-1997, 14-06-2012

##### Article 261

Whoever commits the crime of sexual abuse on a person under fifteen years of age or on a person who does not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act, even with their consent, or who for any reason cannot resist it or compels them to execute it on themselves or on another person, shall be imposed a penalty of six to thirteen years of imprisonment and up to five hundred days of fine.  
If violence were used, the penalty shall be increased by one half more in its minimum and maximum.  
Article amended DOF 03-01-1989, 21-01-1991, 30-12-1997, 14-06-2012

##### Article 262

Whoever has copulation with a person over fifteen years and under eighteen, obtaining their consent by means of deceit, shall be subject to three months to four years of imprisonment.  
Article amended DOF 14-01-1985, 21-01-1991, 14-06-2012

##### Article 263

In the case of the preceding article, proceedings shall not be brought against the active subject, except upon complaint of the offended party or their representatives.  
Article amended DOF 21-01-1991

##### Article 264.- (Repealed)

Article repealed DOF 13-01-1984

##### Artículo 265

The crime of rape is committed by whoever, by means of physical or moral violence, performs sexual intercourse with a person of any sex, and shall be punished with imprisonment of eight to twenty years.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 14-06-2012

For the purposes of this article, sexual intercourse is understood as the introduction of the male member into the body of the victim by vaginal, anal or oral route, regardless of their sex.  

It shall also be considered as rape and shall be punished with imprisonment of eight to twenty years for whoever introduces by vaginal or anal route any element or instrument other than the male member, by means of physical or moral violence, whatever the sex of the victim may be.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 14-06-2012 Artículo reformado DOF 20-01-1967, 13-01-1984, 03-01-1989, 21-01-1991, 30-12-1997

##### Artículo 265

bis.- If the victim of the rape were the wife or concubine, the penalty provided in the preceding article shall be imposed.  
This crime shall be prosecuted upon complaint of the injured party.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 30-12-1997

##### Artículo 266

The following shall be equivalent to rape and shall be punished with eight to thirty years of imprisonment:
Párrafo reformado DOF 14-06-2012

I.	Whoever without violence performs sexual intercourse with a person under fifteen years of age;  
Fracción reformada DOF 14-06-2012

II.- Whoever without violence performs sexual intercourse with a person who does not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or for any reason cannot resist it; and
III. Whoever without violence and with lascivious purposes introduces by anal or vaginal route any element or instrument other than the male member into a person under fifteen years of age or a person who does not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act, or for any reason cannot resist it, whatever the sex of the victim may be.  
Fracción reformada DOF 14-06-2012

If physical or moral violence were exercised, the minimum and maximum of the penalty shall be increased by up to one half.  
Artículo reformado DOF 20-01-1967, 03-01-1989, 21-01-1991, 30-12-1997

##### Artículo 266

Bis.- The penalties provided for sexual abuse and rape shall be increased by up to one half in their minimum and maximum, when:  
I.-	The crime were committed with direct or immediate intervention of two or more persons;  

II.-  The crime were committed by an ascendant against their descendant, the latter against the former, a sibling against their collateral, a guardian against their ward, or by the stepfather or mother's partner against the stepchild. In addition to the prison sentence, the guilty party shall lose parental authority or guardianship, in cases where they exercise it over the victim;  

III.- The crime were committed by someone who holds a public office or employment or exercises their profession, using the means or circumstances that they provide. In addition to the prison sentence, the convicted person shall be removed from office or employment or suspended for a term of five years in the exercise of said profession;  

IV.- The crime were committed by the person who has the victim under their custody, care or education or takes advantage of the trust placed in them.  
V.	The crime were committed after administering narcotics or psychotropic substances to the victim, against their will or without their knowledge.  
Fracción adicionada DOF 15-06-2018 Artículo adicionado DOF 20-01-1967. Reformado DOF 03-01-1989, 21-01-1991

Incesto

##### Artículo 272

The crime of incest shall be punished with a penalty of one to six years of imprisonment when ascendants have sexual relations with their descendants, provided that the latter are of legal age.  
Párrafo reformado DOF 14-06-2012

When the victim is a minor, the conduct shall always be understood as typical of rape.  
Párrafo adicionado DOF 14-06-2012 Reforma DOF 14-06-2012: Derogó del artículo los entonces párrafos segundo y tercero
