#### CAPITULO V

Disposiciones Generales

Artículo derogado DOF 08-06-2011

Capítulo adicionado DOF 13-01-1984

##### Artículo 276

Bis.- When as a consequence of the commission of any of the crimes provided in this Title children result, the reparation of damages shall include the payment of support for them and for the mother, in the terms established by civil legislation for cases of divorce.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 13-01-1984

TITULO DECIMOSEXTO
Delitos Contra el Estado Civil y Bigamia
Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Decimoséptimo) DOF 29-07-1970

##### Artículo 277

One to six years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred to one thousand pesos shall be imposed on those who, with the purpose of altering civil status, incur in any of the following infractions:  
I.- Attributing a newborn child to a woman who is not really their mother;  

II.- Having a birth that did not occur registered in the civil status offices;  

III.- Parents who do not present their child to the Registry with the purpose of making them lose their civil status, or who falsely declare their death, or present them concealing their names or supposing that the parents are other persons;  
IV.- Those who substitute one child for another, or commit concealment of an infant, and

V.- Whoever usurps the civil status of another with the purpose of acquiring family rights that do not correspond to them.

##### Artículo 278

Whoever commits any of the crimes expressed in the preceding article shall lose the right to inherit that they may have with respect to the persons whom they harm in their family rights by the commission of the crime.

##### Artículo 279

Up to five years of imprisonment or 180 to 360 days fine shall be imposed on whoever, being united with a person in a marriage not dissolved nor declared null, contracts another marriage with legal formalities.  
Artículo reformado DOF 30-12-1991

TITULO DECIMOSEPTIMO
Delitos en materia de inhumaciones y exhumaciones
Denominación reformada DOF 15-01-1951. Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Decimoctavo) DOF 29-07-1970

CAPITULO UNICO
Violación de las leyes sobre inhumaciones y exhumaciones
Capítulo adicionado DOF 15-01-1951

##### Artículo 280.- Imprisonment of three days to two years or 30 to 90 days fine shall be imposed

Párrafo reformado DOF 30-12-1991

I.-  On whoever conceals, destroys or buries a corpse or a human fetus, without the order of the authority that must give it or without the requirements demanded by the Civil and Sanitary Codes or special laws;  
II.- On whoever conceals, destroys, or without the corresponding license buries the corpse of a person, provided that the death has been as a consequence of blows, wounds or other injuries, if the accused knew that circumstance.  

In this case no sanction shall be applied to the ascendants or descendants, spouse or siblings of the person responsible for the homicide, and
III.-	On whoever exhumes a corpse without the legal requirements or with violation of rights.  
Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 15-01-1951

##### Artículo 280

Bis.- A penalty of five to eight years of imprisonment and five hundred to one thousand days fine shall be imposed on whoever incinerates, buries, disintegrates or destroys totally or partially the corpse or human remains of an unidentified person, without authorization from the competent authorities in the matter.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 17-11-2017

##### Article 281.- A sentence of one to five years' imprisonment shall be imposed

I.- On whoever violates a tomb, a sepulcher, a grave or coffin, and

II.- On whoever desecrates a corpse or human remains with acts of vilification, mutilation, brutality or necrophilia. If the acts of necrophilia consist of the performance of coitus, the prison sentence shall be four to eight years.  
Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

TITLE EIGHTEENTH
Crimes Against the Peace and Security of Persons
Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Nineteenth) DOF 29-07-1970

#### CHAPTER I

Threats and Illegal Extrajudicial Collection
Chapter heading amended DOF 22-06-2017

##### Article 282

A sanction of three days to one year imprisonment or 180 to 360 days fine shall be applied:
Paragraph amended DOF 30-12-1991

I.- To whoever in any manner threatens another with causing harm to his person, his property, his honor or his rights, or to the person, honor, property or rights of someone with whom he is linked by some bond, and
II.- To whoever by means of threats of any kind attempts to prevent another from doing what he has the right to do.  

If the victim is one of the relatives or persons referred to in articles 343 bis and 343 ter, in the latter case provided they reside in the same domicile, the corresponding penalty shall be increased by up to one third in its minimum and maximum.  
Paragraph added DOF 30-12-1997

If the victim of the threat is a victim or injured party or witness in a criminal proceeding, the penalty shall be four to eight years' imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days fine.  
Paragraph added DOF 23-01-2009

The crimes provided for in this article shall be prosecuted upon complaint, with the exception of that established in the preceding paragraph which shall be prosecuted ex officio.  
Paragraph added DOF 30-12-1991. Amended DOF 23-01-2009

##### Article 283.- A bond to keep the peace shall be required

I.- If the harm threatened is minor or avoidable;  

II.- If the threats are by means of emblems or signs, hieroglyphics or phrases with double meaning, and
III.- If the threat is conditional upon the threatened person not performing an act unlawful in itself. In this case a bond shall also be required of the threatened person, if the judge deems it necessary.  
Whoever does not post the bond to keep the peace shall be sentenced to imprisonment of three days to six months.

##### Article 284

If the person making threats carries out his threat, the sanction for the threat and that of the resulting crime shall be accumulated.  
If the person making threats demanded that the threatened person commit a crime, the sanction corresponding to his participation in the resulting crime shall be accumulated to the sanction for the threat.  
Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 284

Bis. A sentence of one to four years' imprisonment and a fine of fifty thousand to three hundred thousand pesos shall be imposed on whoever carries out the activity of illegal extrajudicial collection.  
If false documents or seals are also used, the penalty and the financial sanction shall be increased by one half.  

If usurpation of functions or profession is committed, the rules of concurrence of crimes set forth in the Federal Penal Code shall apply.  
Illegal extrajudicial collection is understood as the use of unlawful violence or intimidation, whether personally or through any means, to demand payment of a debt arising from activities regulated in federal laws, including credits or financing that were originally granted by persons engaged habitually and professionally in this activity, regardless of the holder of the collection rights at the time of carrying out the collection. It shall not be considered unlawful intimidation to inform of those possible and legally valid consequences of non-payment or the ability to initiate legal actions against the debtor, guarantor, joint obligor or any third party related to them when these are legally possible.  
Article added DOF 22-06-2017

#### CHAPTER II

Trespass of dwelling

##### Article 285

Imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of ten to one hundred pesos shall be imposed on whoever, without justified reason, without order from competent authority and outside the cases in which the law permits it, enters, furtively or by deceit or violence, or without permission from the person authorized to give it, into a department, dwelling, room or dependencies of an inhabited house.

##### Article 286

Whoever in an uninhabited place or in a solitary location uses violence against a person for the purpose of causing harm, obtaining profit or demanding his consent for any purpose and whatever the means and degree of violence employed, and independently of any criminal act that results from being committed, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years.  
Erratum to paragraph DOF 31-08-1931

The penalty shall be ten to thirty years' imprisonment for whoever on general communication routes such as roads, highways, bridges or railways, uses violence against the occupants of a public or private transport vehicle.  
Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994. Amended DOF 18-06-2010

##### Article 287

If the highway robbers attack a town, twenty to thirty years' imprisonment shall be applied to the ringleaders or chiefs, and fifteen to twenty years to the others.  
TITLE NINETEENTH
Crimes against life and bodily integrity
Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Twentieth) DOF 29-07-1970

#### CHAPTER I

Injuries

##### Article 288

Under the name of injury is included not only wounds, abrasions, contusions, fractures, dislocations, burns, but any alteration in health and any other damage that leaves a material trace on the human body, if those effects are produced by an external cause.

##### Article 289

Whoever inflicts an injury that does not endanger the life of the victim and takes less than fifteen days to heal shall be sentenced to three to eight months' imprisonment, or thirty to fifty days

fine, or both sanctions at the judge's discretion. If it takes more than fifteen days to heal, he shall be sentenced to four months to two years' imprisonment and sixty to two hundred seventy days fine.  
In these cases, the crime shall be prosecuted upon complaint, except in the case contemplated in article 295, in which case it shall be prosecuted ex officio.  
Article amended DOF 13-01-1984, 30-12-1991, 13-05-1996

##### Article 290

Imprisonment of two to five years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred pesos shall be imposed on whoever inflicts an injury that leaves the victim with a permanently noticeable scar on the face.

##### Article 291

Imprisonment of three to five years and a fine of three hundred to five hundred pesos shall be imposed on whoever inflicts an injury that permanently disturbs vision, or diminishes the ability to hear, hinders or permanently weakens a hand, a foot, an arm, a leg, or any other organ, the use of speech or any of the mental faculties.

##### Article 292

Imprisonment of five to eight years shall be imposed on whoever inflicts an injury resulting in a certainly or probably incurable disease, the complete disablement or loss of an eye, an arm, a hand, a leg or a foot, or any other organ; when any organic function is permanently impaired or when the victim is left deaf, impotent or with an incorrectable deformity.  

Imprisonment of six to ten years shall be imposed on whoever inflicts an injury as a consequence of which results permanent incapacity to work, mental derangement, loss of sight or speech or sexual functions.

##### Article 293

Whoever inflicts injuries that endanger life shall be sentenced to three to six years' imprisonment, without prejudice to the sanctions that correspond to him according to the preceding articles.

##### Article 294.- (Repealed)

Erratum to article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 295

A person exercising parental authority or guardianship who inflicts injuries on minors or wards under their care may be subject, in addition to the penalty corresponding to the injuries, to suspension or deprivation of the exercise of those rights by the judge.  
Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 296.- (Repealed)

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 23-12-1985

##### Article 297

If the injuries were inflicted in a brawl or in a duel, the sanctions indicated in the preceding articles may be reduced by up to one-half or up to five-sixths, depending on whether it concerns the provoked party or the provoker, and taking into account the greater or lesser importance of the provocation and the provisions of articles 51 and 52.  
Article amended DOF 15-01-1951

##### Article 298

The person responsible for a qualified injury shall have the sanction increased up to double that which corresponds to the simple injury caused.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 17-05-1999

##### Article 299.- Repealed

Article repealed DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 300

If the victim were any of the relatives or persons referred to in articles 343 bis and 343 ter, in the latter case provided they reside in the same domicile, the

penalty that corresponds shall be increased by up to one-third in its minimum and in its maximum, in accordance with the preceding articles, unless the crime of family violence is also established.  
Article amended DOF 30-12-1997

##### Article 301

For injuries that a wild animal causes to a person, the person who incites it with that intention, or releases it, or does the latter through negligence, shall be responsible.

#### CHAPTER II

Homicide

##### Article 302

The crime of homicide is committed by: a person who deprives another of life.

##### Article 303

For the application of the sanctions that correspond to a person who violates the preceding article, an injury shall not be considered mortal unless the following three circumstances are verified:  
I.-  That the death is due to the alterations caused by the injury in the organ or organs involved, one of its immediate consequences or a complication determined by the same injury and that could not be combated, either because it was incurable, or because the necessary resources were not available;  
Errata to the section DOF 31-08-1931

II.-	Repealed.  
Section repealed DOF 10-01-1994
III.- That if the body of the deceased is found, two experts declare after performing the autopsy, when it is necessary, that the injury was mortal, adhering for this purpose to the rules contained in this article, in the following two and in the Code of Criminal Procedure.  
When the body is not found, or for another reason the autopsy is not performed, it shall be sufficient that the experts, in view of the data in the case, declare that the death was the result of the injuries inflicted.

##### Article 304

Whenever the three circumstances of the preceding article are verified, an injury shall be considered mortal, even if it is proven:  
I.- That death would have been avoided with timely assistance;  

II.- That the injury would not have been mortal in another person, and

III.- That it was due to the physical constitution of the victim, or the circumstances in which the injury was received.

##### Article 305

An injury shall not be considered mortal, even if the person who received it dies: when the death is the result of a cause prior to the injury and on which it had no influence, or when the injury was aggravated by subsequent causes, such as the application of positively harmful medications, unfortunate surgical operations, excesses or imprudence of the patient or those around them.

##### Article 306.- (Repealed)

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 08-03-1968. Repealed DOF 30-12-1991

##### Article 307

The person responsible for any simple intentional homicide that does not have a special sanction provided in this Code shall be subject to twelve to twenty-four years of imprisonment.  
Article amended DOF 05-01-1955, 17-05-1999

##### Article 308

If the homicide is committed in a brawl, four to twelve years of imprisonment shall be applied to its perpetrator.  
If the homicide is committed in a duel, two to eight years of imprisonment shall be applied to its perpetrator.  

In addition to the provisions of articles 51 and 52 for the determination of penalties within the minimum and maximum previously indicated, consideration shall be given to who was the provoked party and who the provoker, as well as the greater or lesser importance of the provocation.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 15-01-1951

##### Article 309.- (Repealed)

Article amended DOF 08-03-1968. Repealed DOF 23-12-1985

#### CHAPTER III

Common rules for injuries and homicide

##### Article 310. (Repealed)

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 10-01-1994. Repealed DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 311.- Repealed

Article amended DOF 18-02-1969. Repealed DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 312

A person who provides assistance to or induces another to commit suicide shall be punished with a penalty of one to five years of imprisonment; if the assistance is provided to the point of executing the death themselves, the imprisonment shall be four to twelve years.

##### Article 313

If the deceased or suicide victim were a minor or suffered from any form of mental alienation, the sanctions indicated for qualified homicide or qualified injuries shall be applied to the killer or instigator.

##### Article 314

For all criminal purposes, a brawl is understood to be: a physical confrontation and not a verbal one, between two or more persons.

##### Article 315

Injuries and homicide are understood to be qualified when they are committed with premeditation, with advantage, with treachery or by betrayal.  
Errata to the paragraph DOF 31-08-1931

There is premeditation: whenever the perpetrator intentionally causes an injury, after having reflected on the crime they are going to commit.  
Errata to the paragraph DOF 31-08-1931

Premeditation shall be presumed when the injuries or homicide are committed by flooding, fire, mines, bombs or explosives; by means of poisons or any other substance harmful to health, venereal contagion, asphyxiation or narcotics or for remuneration given or promised; by torture, depraved motives or brutal ferocity.

##### Article 315

Bis.- The penalty of article 320 of this Code shall be imposed when the homicide is committed intentionally, in connection with a rape or a robbery by the perpetrator thereof, against their victim or victims.  
The penalty referred to in Article 320 of this Code shall also apply when the homicide is committed intentionally in a dwelling, having entered it furtively, with deceit or violence, or without permission from the person authorized to give it.  
Article added DOF 03-01-1989

##### Article 316.- Advantage is understood to exist

I.- When the offender is superior in physical strength to the victim and the victim is not armed;  

II.- When the offender is superior by reason of the weapons employed, by greater skill in handling them, or by the number of those accompanying him;  
III. When the offender takes advantage of some means that weakens the victim's defense;  
Fraction reformed DOF 14-06-2012

IV. When the victim is unarmed or fallen and the offender armed or standing;  
Fraction reformed DOF 14-06-2012

V. The active party is a man superior in physical strength and the passive party is a woman or person under eighteen years of age;  
Fraction added DOF 14-06-2012

VI. The homicide and injuries are caused in situations of family violence; and
Fraction added DOF 14-06-2012

VII. There exists a situation of vulnerability motivated by physical or mental condition or by discrimination.  
Fraction added DOF 14-06-2012

Advantage shall not be taken into consideration in the first three cases, if the one who has it acted in legitimate defense, nor in the fourth, if the one who is armed or standing was the one attacked, and, furthermore, his life was in danger by not taking advantage of that circumstance.  
Errata to the paragraph DOF 31-08-1931

##### Article 317

Advantage shall only be considered as an aggravating circumstance of the offenses referred to in the preceding chapters of this title: when it is such that the offender runs no risk of being killed or injured by the victim and the offender does not act in legitimate defense.

##### Article 318

Treachery consists: in intentionally surprising someone unexpectedly, or employing ambush or other means that does not give him a chance to defend himself or avoid the harm intended to be done to him.

##### Article 319

One is said to act with betrayal: who not only employs treachery but also perfidy, violating the faith or security that he had expressly promised to his victim, or the tacit faith that the victim should have expected from him by reason of their relations of kinship, gratitude, friendship or any other that inspires confidence.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931

##### Article 320

The person responsible for an aggravated homicide shall be sentenced to thirty to sixty years of imprisonment.  
Article reformed DOF 15-01-1951, 05-01-1955, 03-01-1989, 17-05-1999

##### Article 321.- Repealed

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 13-05-1996

##### Article 321

Bis.- No proceedings shall be brought against one who culpably causes injuries or homicide to the detriment of an ascendant or descendant related by consanguinity in the direct line, sibling, spouse, concubine, adopter or adoptee, unless the perpetrator is under the influence of intoxicating beverages, narcotics or psychotropic substances, without medical prescription, or fails to assist the victim.  

Article added DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 322

In addition to the sanctions set forth in the two preceding chapters, judges may, if they deem it appropriate:  
I.- Declare the offenders subject to police surveillance, and

II.- Prohibit them from going to a certain place, Municipality, District or State, or residing therein.

#### CHAPTER IV

Homicide by reason of kinship or relationship
Title of the Chapter reformed DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 323

One who deprives of life his ascendant or descendant related by consanguinity in the direct line, sibling, spouse, cohabitant, civil partner, concubine, adopter or adoptee, with knowledge of that relationship shall be sentenced to thirty to sixty years of imprisonment.  
If such knowledge is lacking, the punishment provided in article 307 shall apply, without prejudice to observing any circumstance that aggravates or attenuates the sanction referred to in the preceding chapters II and III.  
Article reformed DOF 10-01-1994, 14-06-2012

##### Article 324.- Repealed

Article reformed DOF 05-01-1955, 03-01-1989. Repealed DOF 10-01-1994

#### Chapter V Feminicide

Title of the Chapter reformed DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 325

One who deprives a woman of life for a gender-based reason commits the crime of feminicide.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 25-04-2023

A gender-based reason is considered to exist when any of the following circumstances is present:
Heading of the paragraph added DOF 25-04-2023

I. The victim shows signs of sexual violence of any kind;  
II. The victim has been inflicted with infamous or degrading injuries or mutilations, prior or subsequent to the deprivation of life or acts of necrophilia;  
III. There exist antecedents or data of any type of violence in the family, labor, community, political or school sphere, by the active subject against the victim;  
Fraction reformed DOF 25-04-2023

IV. There has existed between the active subject and the victim kinship by consanguinity or affinity or a sentimental, affective, labor, teaching, trust relationship or some de facto relationship between the parties;  
Fraction reformed DOF 25-04-2023

V. There exist data establishing that there were direct or indirect threats related to the criminal act, harassment or injuries by the active subject against the victim;  
Fraction reformed DOF 25-04-2023

VI. The victim has been held incommunicado, whatever the time prior to the deprivation of life;  
VII. The victim's body is exposed, thrown, deposited or exhibited in a public place, or
Fraction reformed DOF 25-04-2023

VIII. The active subject has forced the victim to perform an activity or work or has exercised over her any form of exploitation.  
Fraction added DOF 25-04-2023

One who commits the crime of feminicide shall be sentenced to forty to sixty years of imprisonment and five hundred to one thousand days' fine.  
The penalty shall be increased by up to one third when the victim is a minor woman, pregnant, elderly or with a disability, as well as when the active subject is a public servant and has committed the conduct by taking advantage of this condition.  
Paragraph added DOF 25-04-2023

In addition to the sanctions described in this article, the active subject shall lose all rights in relation to the victim, including those of a succession nature. Where applicable, he shall also lose all rights in relation to the victim's children, guaranteeing the best interests of children in terms of the provisions of the General Law on the Rights of Girls, Boys and Adolescents.  
Paragraph reformed DOF 25-04-2023

A public servant who maliciously or negligently delays or obstructs the procurement or administration of justice shall be sentenced to three to eight years of imprisonment and five hundred to one thousand five hundred days' fine, and shall furthermore be removed from office and disqualified for three to ten years from holding another employment, position or public commission.  
Reform DOF 25-04-2023: Repealed from the article the then fourth paragraph Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 10-01-1994. Added DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 326.- Repealed

Article repealed DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 327.- Repealed

Article repealed DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 328.- Repealed

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 10-01-1994

#### CHAPTER VI

Abortion

##### Article 329

Abortion is the death of the product of conception at any time during pregnancy.

##### Article 330

One who causes a woman to abort shall be sentenced to one to three years of imprisonment, whatever the means employed, provided it is done with her consent. When consent is lacking, the imprisonment shall be three to six years and if physical or moral violence is involved, the offender shall be sentenced to six to eight years of imprisonment.

##### Article 331

If the abortion is caused by a physician, surgeon, midwife or birth attendant, in addition to the sanctions corresponding to him under the preceding article, he shall be suspended for two to five years in the exercise of his profession.

##### Artículo 332

A sentence of six months to one year of imprisonment shall be imposed on the mother who voluntarily procures her abortion or consents to another causing her to abort, if these three circumstances concur:  
I.- That she does not have a bad reputation;  

II.- That she has managed to conceal her pregnancy, and

III.- That it is the fruit of an illegitimate union.  

If any of the mentioned circumstances is lacking, a sentence of one to five years of imprisonment shall be applied to her.

##### Artículo 333

Abortion caused only by the imprudence of the pregnant woman is not punishable, nor when the pregnancy is the result of rape.

##### Artículo 334

No sanction shall be applied: when, if the abortion is not induced, the pregnant woman's life is in danger, in the judgment of the attending physician, who shall hear the opinion of another physician, provided this is possible and the delay is not dangerous.  
Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931

#### CAPITULO VII

Abandonment of persons

##### Artículo 335

Anyone who abandons a child incapable of caring for himself or herself or a sick person, having an obligation to care for them, shall be subject to one month to four years of imprisonment, if no harm results, also depriving him or her of parental authority or guardianship, if the offender is an ascendant or guardian of the victim.

##### Artículo 336

Anyone who without justified reason abandons his or her children or spouse, without resources to meet their subsistence needs, shall be subject to one month to five years of imprisonment, or 180 to 360 days' fine; deprivation of family rights, and payment, as reparation for damages, of the amounts not timely provided by the accused.  
Artículo reformado DOF 26-12-1977, 30-12-1991

##### Artículo 336

Bis.- Anyone who deliberately places himself or herself in a state of insolvency with the purpose of evading compliance with the support obligations determined by law, shall be subject to a prison sentence of six months to three years. The judge shall decide on the application of the proceeds of work performed by the agent to the satisfaction of the agent's support obligations.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 13-01-1984. Reformado DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

##### Artículo 337

The crime of abandonment of a spouse shall be prosecuted at the request of the aggrieved party. The crime of abandonment of children shall be prosecuted ex officio and, when appropriate, the Public Prosecutor shall promote the appointment of a special guardian to represent the victims of the crime before the trial judge, who shall have powers to appoint him or her. In the case of the crime of abandonment of children, the criminal action shall be declared extinguished, with the judicial authority previously hearing the representative of the minors, when the accused pays the overdue support, and provides sufficient guarantee in the judge's judgment for the subsistence of the children.  
Artículo reformado DOF 26-12-1977

##### Artículo 338

In order for the pardon granted by the offended spouse to produce the release of the accused, the accused must pay all amounts that he or she has failed to provide as support and give bond or other security that he or she will henceforth pay the amount corresponding to him or her.  
Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931

##### Artículo 339

If any injury or death results from the abandonment referred to in the preceding articles, these shall be presumed to be premeditated for the purposes of applying the sanctions corresponding to these crimes.

##### Artículo 340

Anyone who finds abandoned in any place a minor incapable of caring for himself or herself or an injured, disabled person or one threatened by any danger, shall be subject to ten to sixty days of community service if he or she does not give immediate notice to the authority or fails to provide them with the necessary assistance when he or she could do so without personal risk.  
Artículo reformado DOF 10-01-1994

##### Artículo 341

Anyone who, having run over a person, culpably or fortuitously, does not provide assistance or does not request the assistance required, being able to do so, shall be subject to fifteen to sixty days of community service, independently of the penalty that may apply for the crime committed by the running over.  
Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

##### Artículo 342

Anyone who places in a foundling home a child under seven years of age who has been entrusted to him or her, or delivers him or her to another charitable institution or to any other person, without the consent of the person who entrusted him or her or of the authority in his or her absence, shall be subject to one to four months of imprisonment and a fine of five to twenty pesos.  
Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931

##### Artículo 343

Ascendants or guardians who deliver to a foundling home a child who is under their authority, shall lose by that fact alone the rights they have over the person and property of the foundling.  
CAPÍTULO OCTAVO
Violencia familiar
Capítulo adicionado DOF 30-12-1997

##### Artículo 343

Bis. Anyone who carries out acts or conduct of domination, control or physical, psychological, patrimonial or economic aggression against any person with whom he or she is or has been united by marital bond, kinship by consanguinity, affinity or civil relationship, concubinage, or a relationship as a couple inside or outside the family home, commits the crime of family violence.  
Anyone who commits the crime of family violence shall be subject to six months to four years of imprisonment and shall lose the right to support payments. Likewise, he or she shall be subject to specialized psychological treatment.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 30-12-1997. Reformado DOF 14-06-2012

##### Artículo 343

Ter. It is equivalent to family violence and shall be punished with six months to four years of imprisonment for anyone who performs any of the acts indicated in the preceding article against the person who is subject to the custody, care, protection, education, instruction or care of said person.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 30-12-1997. Reformado DOF 14-06-2012

##### Artículo 343

quáter.- In all cases provided for in the two preceding articles, the Public Prosecutor shall exhort the probable perpetrator to refrain from any conduct that could be offensive to the victim and shall agree on the necessary preventive measures to safeguard the physical or psychological integrity of the victim. The administrative authority shall monitor compliance with these measures. In all cases the Public Prosecutor must request the precautionary measures he or she considers pertinent.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 30-12-1997

TITULO VIGESIMO

Delitos Contra el Honor
Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Vigésimo Primero) DOF 29-07-1970

TITLE TWENTY-FIRST
Illegal deprivation of liberty and other guarantees
Title added DOF 15-01-1951. Title moved DOF 20-01-1967. Moved with amended title (formerly Title Twenty-Second) DOF 29-07-1970

SOLE CHAPTER
Errata to the title DOF 31-08-1931. Chapter renumbered with amended title (formerly Chapter I) DOF 15-01-1951.  
Title deleted DOF 29-07-1970

##### Article 364

A penalty of six months to three years of imprisonment and twenty-five to one hundred days' fine shall be imposed:
Paragraph amended DOF 29-07-1970, 13-05-1996

I.-	On any private individual who deprives another of his liberty. If the deprivation of liberty exceeds twenty-four hours, the prison sentence shall be increased by one additional month for each day.  
Paragraph amended DOF 19-05-2006

The prison sentence shall be increased by up to one-half when the deprivation of liberty is carried out with violence, when the victim is under sixteen or over sixty years of age, or when due to any circumstance, the victim is in a situation of physical or mental inferiority with respect to the person who executes it.  
Amendment DOF 19-05-2006: Repealed the then third paragraph of the section
Section amended DOF 29-07-1970, 13-05-1996

II.-	(Repealed)
Errata to the section DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 19-05-2006

##### Article 365. (Repealed)

Article repealed DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 365

Bis. (Repealed)
Article added DOF 21-01-1991. Repealed DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 366. Repealed

Article amended DOF 09-03-1946, 15-01-1951, 05-01-1955, 29-07-1970, 13-01-1984, 03-01-1989, 13-05-1996, 17-05-1999, 12-06-2000,
16-06-2005. Repealed DOF 30-11-2010

##### Article 366

Bis. Repealed.  
Article added DOF 13-05-1996. Amended DOF 12-06-2000. Repealed DOF 30-11-2010

##### Article 366

Ter.- The offense of trafficking in minors is committed by anyone who unlawfully transports a minor under sixteen years of age or delivers him to a third party, outside the national territory, for the purpose of obtaining an undue economic benefit from the transport or delivery of the minor.  
The offense referred to in the preceding paragraph is committed by:  
I.	Those who exercise parental authority or custody over the minor, even if it has not been declared, when they materially carry out the transport or delivery or have given their consent thereto;  
II.	Ascendants without limitation of degree, collateral relatives and relatives by affinity up to the fourth degree, as well as any third party who has no kinship with the minor.  
The persons referred to in the preceding paragraph shall be deemed to act unlawfully when they have knowledge that:
a)	Those who exercise parental authority or custody of the minor have not given their express consent for the transport or delivery, or
b)	Those who exercise parental authority or custody of the minor will obtain an undue economic benefit from the transport or delivery.  
III.	The person or persons who receive the minor.  

Those who commit the offense referred to in this article shall be subject to a penalty of three to ten years of imprisonment and four hundred to one thousand days' fine.  
In addition to the penalties set forth in the preceding paragraph, those who, where applicable, having the exercise thereof commit the offense referred to in this article shall be deprived of the rights of parental authority, guardianship or custody.  

Up to two-thirds of the penalties referred to in this article shall be applied when the transport or delivery of the minor takes place within the national territory.  
Article added DOF 13-01-1984. Moved (formerly article 366 bis) DOF 13-05-1996. Amended DOF 12-06-2000

##### Article 366

quáter.- The penalties referred to in the preceding article shall be reduced by one-half when:  
I.	The transport or delivery of the minor is carried out without the purpose of obtaining an undue economic benefit, or
II.	The person who receives the minor has the purpose of incorporating him into his family nucleus.  

The penalties referred to in this article shall be imposed on the father or mother of a minor under sixteen years of age who unlawfully or without the consent of the person or persons exercising parental authority or custody of the minor, without the purpose of obtaining undue profit, transports him outside the national territory for the purpose of changing his habitual residence or preventing the mother or father, as the case may be, from living with or visiting the minor.  

In addition, those who, where applicable, having the exercise thereof commit the offense referred to in this article shall be deprived of the rights of parental authority, guardianship or custody.  
In the cases referred to in this article, the offense shall be prosecuted at the request of the injured party.  
Article added DOF 30-12-1997. Amended DOF 12-06-2000

TITLE TWENTY-SECOND
Offenses against persons in their property
Title moved DOF 20-01-1967. Moved (formerly Title Twenty-Third) DOF 29-07-1970

#### CHAPTER I

Robbery

##### Article 367

The offense of robbery is committed by: anyone who takes possession of another's movable property, without right and without the consent of the person who may dispose of it in accordance with the law.

##### Article 368.- The following are equivalent to robbery and shall be punished as such

I.-  The appropriation or willful destruction of one's own movable property, if it is in the possession of another person by any lawful title and there is no consent; and
Section amended DOF 10-01-1994. Errata DOF 01-02-1994

II.-  The use or exploitation of electrical, magnetic, electromagnetic energy, of any fluid, or of any means of transmission, without right and without the consent of the person who may legally dispose of them.  
Section amended DOF 17-05-1999
III.-	Repealed.  
Section added DOF 13-05-1996. Repealed DOF 17-05-1999

##### Article 368

Bis.- A penalty of three to ten years of imprisonment and up to one thousand days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who, after the execution of the robbery and without having participated in it, possesses, alienates or traffics in any manner, acquires or receives, the instruments, objects or products of the robbery, knowing this circumstance and the intrinsic value thereof exceeds five hundred times the salary.  
Article added DOF 13-05-1996

##### Article 368

Ter.- Anyone who habitually trades in stolen objects, knowing this circumstance and the intrinsic value thereof exceeds five hundred times the salary, shall be subject to a penalty of imprisonment of six to thirteen years and one hundred to one thousand days' fine.  
Article added DOF 13-05-1996

##### Article 368

Quáter. Repealed.  
Article added DOF 17-05-1999. Amended DOF 24-10-2011. Repealed DOF 12-01-2016

##### Article 368

Quinquies.- Anyone who commits the offense of robbery of radioactive material, nuclear material, nuclear fuel, radioactive mineral or radiation source, shall be subject to twelve to twenty years of imprisonment and twelve thousand to twenty thousand days' fine.  
Article added DOF 14-03-2014

##### Article 369

For the application of the penalty, the robbery shall be deemed consummated from the moment the thief has the stolen property in his possession; even if he abandons it or is dispossessed of it. As for the determination of the value of what was stolen, as well as the fine imposed, the salary at the time of the execution of the offense shall be taken into consideration.  
Article amended DOF 29-12-1981

##### Article 369

Bis.- To establish the amount corresponding to the crimes provided for in this Title, the general minimum wage in force at the time and place where the crime was committed shall be taken into consideration.  
Article added DOF 29-12-1981. Amended DOF 14-01-1985

##### Article 370

When the value of the stolen property does not exceed one hundred times the salary, up to two years of imprisonment and a fine of up to one hundred times the salary shall be imposed.  
When it exceeds one hundred times the salary, but not five hundred, the sanction shall be two to four years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred to one hundred eighty times the salary.  
Errata to paragraph DOF 13-01-1982

When it exceeds five hundred times the salary, the sanction shall be four to ten years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred eighty to five hundred times the salary.  
Errata to paragraph DOF 13-01-1982 Article amended DOF 09-03-1946, 15-01-1951, 30-12-1975, 29-12-1981

##### Article 371

To estimate the amount of the theft, only the intrinsic value of the object of the appropriation shall be considered, but if for any circumstance it is not estimable in money or if by its nature it is not possible to fix its value, imprisonment of three days to five years shall be applied.  
In cases of attempted theft, when it is not possible to determine its amount, three days to two years of imprisonment shall be applied.  

When the theft is committed by two or more subjects, regardless of the amount stolen, through violence, ambush or any other circumstance that diminishes the victim's possibilities of defense or places them in conditions of disadvantage, the applicable penalty shall be five to fifteen years of imprisonment and up to one thousand days fine. Prohibition from going to a determined place or surveillance by the authority may also be applied, for a term equal to that of the custodial sanction imposed.  
Paragraph added DOF 13-05-1996 Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 15-01-1951

##### Article 372

If the theft is executed with violence, to the penalty corresponding to simple theft shall be added six months to five years of imprisonment. If the violence constitutes another crime, the rules of accumulation shall apply.  
Article amended DOF 03-01-1989

##### Article 373.- Violence against persons is distinguished as physical and moral

Physical violence in theft is understood as: the material force applied to a person to commit it.  

There is moral violence: when the thief threatens or menaces a person with a serious harm, present or imminent, capable of intimidating them.

##### Article 374

For the imposition of the sanction, theft shall also be considered as committed with violence:  
I.- When it is done to a person other than the one robbed, who is in their company, and

II.- When the thief exercises it after the theft is consummated, to secure their escape or defend the stolen property.

##### Article 375

When the value of the stolen property does not exceed ten times the salary, is spontaneously returned by the offender and the offender pays all damages and losses, before the Authority becomes aware of the crime, no sanction shall be imposed, if the theft has not been executed by means of violence.  
Article amended DOF 30-12-1975, 29-12-1981

##### Article 376

In any case of theft, if the judge deems it just, they may suspend the offender from one month to six years, in the rights of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, expert, depositary or judicial receiver, trustee or receiver in insolvency or bankruptcy proceedings, advisor and representative of absentees, and in the exercise of any profession requiring a degree.

##### Article 376

bis.- When the stolen object is a land motor vehicle that is subject to registration under the law on the matter, with the exception of motorcycles, the penalty shall be seven to fifteen years of imprisonment and one thousand five hundred to two thousand days fine.  

The penalty provided in the preceding paragraph shall be increased by one half, when a public servant who has functions of prevention, prosecution or punishment of the crime or execution of sentences participates in the theft and, in addition, dismissal and disqualification from holding any public employment, position or commission for a time equal to that of the imprisonment penalty imposed shall be applied.  
Article added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Article 376

Ter.- Whoever commits the crime of theft against persons who provide or use by themselves or through a third party the services of federal freight, passenger, tourism or private transportation, shall be imposed a penalty of 6 to 12 years of imprisonment, when the object of the theft is the merchandise and 2 to 7 years of imprisonment, when it is only luggage or valuables of tourists or passengers, at any place during the journey, regardless of the value of the stolen property.  

When the object of the theft is the motor vehicle, the provisions of articles 376 Bis and 377 of this Code shall apply, without prejudice to the accumulation of penalties corresponding to other unlawful conducts that concur in the commission of the crime, including those provided in the first paragraph of this article.  

When the conduct referred to in this article is committed by a criminal association, gang or band, it shall be sanctioned in terms of articles 164 or 164 Bis, as applicable.  
Article added DOF 21-02-2018

##### Article 376

Quáter.- In addition to the penalty corresponding under the first paragraph of the preceding article, those provided in this article shall apply in the following cases:  
I.- The imprisonment penalty shall be increased by one third when there is appropriation of the trailer or semi-trailer and it is used to commit another unlawful act, and
II.-  The imprisonment penalty shall be increased by one half when, knowing their functions, the public servant commits or participates in the theft and has or performs functions of prevention, investigation, prosecution of the crime or execution of sentences, regardless of the criminal sanction, they shall be disqualified from holding any public employment, position or commission for a time equal to that of the imprisonment penalty imposed.  
Article added DOF 21-02-2018

##### Article 377

A penalty of five to fifteen years of imprisonment and up to one thousand days fine shall be imposed on whoever knowingly and regardless of the penalties corresponding to them for the commission of other crimes:  
I.	Dismantles any stolen vehicle or vehicles or markets jointly or separately their parts;  

II.	Alienates or traffics in any manner with a stolen vehicle or vehicles;  
III.	Holds, possesses, keeps, alters or modifies in any manner the documentation that proves the ownership or identification of a stolen vehicle;  
IV.	Transfers the stolen vehicle or vehicles to another federative entity or abroad, and
V.	Uses the stolen vehicle or vehicles in the commission of another crime or crimes.  

Whoever provides economic resources or of any kind, for the execution of the activities described in the preceding sections, shall be considered a co-participant in the terms of article 13 of this Code.  

If any public servant who has functions of prevention, prosecution or punishment of the crime or execution of sentences participates in the aforementioned acts, in addition to the sanctions referred to in this article, the imprisonment penalty shall be increased by up to one half more and they shall be disqualified from holding any public employment, position or commission for a period equal to the imprisonment penalty imposed.  
Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 13-01-1984. Added DOF 13-05-1996

##### Article 378

Anyone who manufactures or alters without permission from the competent authority a license plate, sticker, circulation card or other official documents issued to identify motor vehicles or trailers shall be subject to four to eight years of imprisonment and a fine of three hundred to one thousand days.  
The same penalties shall be imposed on anyone who possesses, uses, acquires or disposes of any of the objects referred to in the preceding paragraph, knowing that they are stolen, falsified or were obtained improperly.  
The same penalties shall likewise be imposed on anyone who knowingly uses for a stolen vehicle or one that is illegally in the country, the license plates, sticker or other official documents issued to identify another vehicle.  
Article repealed DOF 13-01-1984. Added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Article 379

Anyone who, without employing deceit or violent means, takes possession on a single occasion of objects strictly indispensable to satisfy his personal or family needs of the moment shall not be punished.

##### Article 380

Anyone charged with having taken another's property without the consent of the owner or lawful possessor and who proves having taken it temporarily and not to appropriate it or sell it, shall be subject to one to six months of imprisonment or a fine of 30 to 90 days, provided that he proves not having refused to return it, if required to do so. In addition, he shall pay the offended party, as reparation for damages, double the rental, lease or interest of the thing used.  
Article amended DOF 30-12-1991

##### Article 381

In addition to the penalty corresponding to him under articles 370, 371 and the first paragraph of article 376 Ter, the penalties provided in this article shall be applied to the offender, in the following cases:
Paragraph amended DOF 13-01-1984, 03-01-1989, 17-05-1999, 21-02-2018

I.-	When the crime is committed in an enclosed place.  
Errata to the fraction DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 05-01-1955

II.-  When committed by an employee or a domestic servant against his employer or a member of the latter's family, wherever he commits it.  
By domestic servant is understood; the individual who for a salary, for food alone or other stipend or service, wages or emoluments serves another, even if he does not live in the latter's house;  
III.- When a guest or diner or a member of his family or the servants accompanying him, commits it in the house where they receive hospitality, courtesy or entertainment;  
IV.- When committed by the owner or a member of his family in the former's house, against his employees or domestic servants or against any other person;  
V.- When committed by the owners, employees, managers or servants of commercial enterprises or establishments, in the places where they provide their services to the public, and on the property of guests or clients, and
VI.- When committed by workers, artisans, apprentices or students, in the house, workshop or school where they habitually work or learn or in the dwelling, office, warehouse or other place to which they have free entry by virtue of the indicated character.  
VII.-  When committed while the victim is in a private vehicle or public transportation;  
Fraction added DOF 13-01-1984

VIII.- When committed by taking advantage of the conditions of confusion produced by catastrophe or public disorder;  
Fraction added DOF 13-01-1984

IX.-	When committed by one or more armed persons, or who use or carry other dangerous objects;  
Fraction added DOF 13-01-1984

X.-	When committed against a banking office, tax collection office or other office where funds are kept, against persons who guard or transport them.  
Fraction added DOF 13-01-1984

XI.-	When it involves parts of vehicles parked on public roads or in another place intended for their storage or repair;  
Fraction added DOF 03-01-1989

XII.-  When carried out on vessels or things found on them;  
Fraction added DOF 03-01-1989

XIII.- (Repealed)
Fraction added DOF 03-01-1989. Repealed DOF 21-02-2018

XIV.- When it involves files or protocol documents, public office or archives, documents containing obligation, release or transmission of duties that appear in judicial files, affecting some public function. If the crime is committed by the public servant of the office where the file or document is located, he shall also be subject to dismissal and disqualification from holding another public employment, position or commission, from six months to three years;  
Fraction added DOF 03-01-1989. Amended DOF 08-02-1999

XV.	When the agent uses false identifications or supposed orders from some authority;  
Fraction added DOF 03-01-1989. Amended DOF 08-02-1999, 18-06-2010

XVI.	When committed on roads or highways, and
Fraction added DOF 08-02-1999. Amended DOF 18-06-2010

XVII.	When the object of taking is railway tracks, their parts or railway equipment, the goods, valuables or merchandise transported by this means.  
Fraction added DOF 18-06-2010. Amended DOF 25-01-2013

In the cases referred to in fractions I, II, III, IV, V, VI, XI, XII, XIV and XV, up to five years of imprisonment.  
Paragraph added DOF 17-05-1999. Amended DOF 21-02-2018

In the cases referred to in fractions VII, VIII, IX, X, XVI and XVII, from two to seven years of imprisonment.  
Paragraph added DOF 17-05-1999. Amended DOF 18-06-2010

##### Article 381

Bis.- Without prejudice to the sanctions that according to articles 370, 371 and 372 must be imposed, three days to ten years of imprisonment shall be applied to anyone who steals in buildings, dwellings, rooms or quarters that are inhabited or intended for habitation, this denomination including not only those that are fixed to the ground, but also mobile ones, whatever the material of which they are constructed, as well as in those places or establishments intended for commercial activities.  
Article added DOF 05-01-1955. Amended DOF 20-01-1967, 13-01-1984, 17-05-1999, 23-06-2017

##### Article 381

Ter.- Anyone who by himself or through an intermediary takes possession of one or more head of livestock, without the consent of whoever may legally dispose of them, commits the crime of cattle rustling.  
For the purposes of this crime, livestock shall be considered the following species: bovine, equine, asinine, mule, ovine, caprine, porcine or one or more bee colonies in an apiary; as well as domesticated, wild, hoofed, large livestock or small livestock, regardless of the typical activity of the animal.  

For such crime, two to ten years of imprisonment shall be imposed.  
The slaughter of livestock without the consent of whoever may legally grant it shall be equivalent to the crime of cattle rustling and shall be punished with the same penalty as the latter.  
Article added DOF 23-06-2017

##### Article 381

Quáter.- The crime of cattle rustling is considered aggravated and the penalty shall be increased by up to one half, when committed by someone who has an employment relationship, or kinship by consanguinity or affinity up to the fourth degree with the owner of the livestock.  
The penalty established in the preceding paragraph shall likewise be imposed when the crime is executed through physical or moral violence, or when committed by a public servant.  
When the conduct referred to in this article is committed by a criminal association, gang or band, it shall be punished under the terms of articles 164 or 164 Bis, as applicable.  
Article added DOF 23-06-2017

#### CHAPTER II

Breach of trust

##### Article 382

Whoever, to the detriment of another, disposes for himself or for another, of any movable property belonging to another, of which possession but not ownership has been transferred to him, shall be punished with imprisonment up to

1 year and a fine up to 100 times the salary, when the amount of the abuse does not exceed 200 times the salary.  
If it exceeds this amount, but not 2000, the imprisonment shall be from 1 to 6 years and the fine from 100 up to 180 times the salary.  
Errata to the paragraph DOF 13-01-1982

If the amount is greater than 2,000 times the salary, the imprisonment shall be from 6 to 12 years and the fine of 120 times the salary.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 09-03-1946, 15-01-1951, 30-12-1975, 29-12-1981

##### Article 383.- The following are considered as breach of trust for purposes of the penalty

I.-  The act of disposing of or removing a thing by its owner, if it has been seized and he holds it in the capacity of judicial depositary, or if he has given it as a pledge and keeps it in his possession as depositary by virtue of a contract entered into with a Credit Institution, to the detriment of the latter.  
Section amended DOF 03-01-1980

II.-	The act of disposing of the deposited thing, or its removal by the judicial depositary or the one designated by or before the administrative or labor authorities.  
Section amended DOF 24-03-1944

III.-	The act of a person making a deposit appear as his own that guarantees the bail bond of an accused person and of which ownership does not correspond to him.

##### Article 384

The illegitimate possession of the retained thing is deemed as breach of trust if the holder or possessor thereof does not return it despite being formally required by whoever has the right, or does not deliver it to the authority, so that the latter may dispose of it in accordance with the law.  
Article amended DOF 09-03-1946

##### Article 385

It is considered as breach of trust and shall be punished with six months to six years of imprisonment and a fine up to one hundred times the salary whoever improperly disposes of or refuses without justification to deliver a vehicle received in deposit from a competent authority, related to offenses for vehicle traffic, having been required by the authority that knows or continues to know of the case.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 13-01-1984. Added DOF 19-11-1986

#### CHAPTER III

Fraud

##### Article 386

Whoever commits the crime of fraud by deceiving someone or taking advantage of the error in which the latter finds himself unlawfully obtains some thing or achieves an undue profit.  
The crime of fraud shall be punished with the following penalties:  
I.-	With imprisonment from 3 days to 6 months or from 30 to 180 days fine, when the value of what was defrauded does not exceed ten times the salary;  
Section amended DOF 30-12-1991

II.-	With imprisonment from 6 months to 3 years and a fine from 10 to 100 times the salary, when the value of what was defrauded exceeds 10, but not 500 times the salary;  
Errata to the section DOF 13-01-1982, 15-01-1982

III.-	With imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine up to one hundred twenty times the salary, if the value of what was defrauded is greater than five hundred times the salary.  
Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 09-03-1946. Errata DOF 16-07-1946. Amended DOF 30-12-1975,
29-12-1981

##### Article 387.- The same penalties indicated in the preceding article shall be imposed on

I.-   Anyone who obtains money, securities or any other thing by offering to undertake the defense of an accused person or a convict, or the direction or representation in a civil or administrative matter, if he does not carry out the former or does not perform the latter, whether because he does not legally take charge of it, or because he renounces or abandons the business or the case without justified reason;  
II.-  Anyone who for valuable consideration alienates something with knowledge that he has no right to dispose of it, or leases, mortgages, pledges or encumbers it in any other way, if he has received the price, the rent, the amount for which he encumbered it, part of them or an equivalent profit;  
III.-  Anyone who obtains from another a sum of money or any other profit, by granting or endorsing in his own name or another's, a nominative, order or bearer document against a supposed person or one whom the grantor knows will not pay him;  
IV.-   Anyone who has something served to him or accepts a service in any commercial establishment and does not pay the amount;  
V.-  Anyone who purchases a movable thing offering to pay its price in cash and refuses after receiving it, to make payment or return the thing, if the seller demands the former from him within fifteen days of having received the thing from the purchaser;  

VI.-  Anyone who has sold a movable thing and received its price, if he does not deliver it within fifteen days of the agreed term or does not return its amount within the same term, in the event that the latter is demanded of him.  
VII.-  Anyone who sells to two persons the same thing, whether movable or real property, and receives the price of the first or second alienation, of both or part of it, or any other profit to the detriment of the first or second purchaser.  
VIII.- Anyone who taking advantage of the ignorance or poor economic conditions of a person, obtains from the latter usurious advantages by means of contracts or agreements in which interest or profits higher than those usual in the market are stipulated.  
IX.-  Anyone who in order to obtain an undue profit, puts into circulation tokens, cards, metal plates or other objects of any material as conventional signs in substitution of legal currency;  

X.-  Anyone who simulates a contract, an act or judicial writing, to the detriment of another or to obtain any undue benefit.  
Reform DOF 10-01-1994: Repealed from the fraction the then second paragraph

XI.-   Anyone who by drawings, raffles, lotteries, promises of sale or by any other means, keeps all or part of the amounts received, without delivering the merchandise or object offered.  
XII.-  The manufacturer, entrepreneur, contractor, or builder of any work, who employs in the construction thereof, materials in quantity or quality inferior to that agreed upon or labor inferior to that stipulated, provided that he has received the price or part of it;  

XIII.-  The seller of construction materials or any kind, who having received the price thereof, does not deliver them in their agreed totality or quality;  
XIV.-  Anyone who sells or transfers a business without authorization of its creditors, or without the new acquirer undertaking to answer for the credits, provided that the latter remain unpaid. When the alienation is made by a legal entity, those who authorize it and the directors, administrators or agents who carry it out shall be criminally liable;  
Fraction reformed DOF 05-01-1955

XV.-	Anyone who exploits the preoccupations, superstition or ignorance of the people, by means of supposed evocation of spirits, divinations or cures.  
XVI.-	(Repealed).  
Fraction reformed DOF 05-01-1955. Repealed DOF 24-12-1996

XVII.-  Anyone who taking advantage of the ignorance or poor economic conditions of a worker in his service, pays him amounts lower than those legally corresponding to him for the work he performs or makes him grant receipts or payment vouchers of any kind that cover sums of money higher than those actually delivered.  
Fraction added DOF 05-01-1955

XVIII.- Anyone who having received merchandise with subsidy or franchise to give them a determined destination, diverts them from this destination or in any way distorts the purposes pursued with the subsidy or franchise.  
Fraction added DOF 05-01-1955

XIX.- Intermediaries in operations of transfer of ownership of real property or of real encumbrances thereon, who obtain money, securities or securities for the amount of their price, on account of it or to constitute such encumbrance, if they do not destine them, in whole or in part, to the object of the operation arranged, by their disposition for their own benefit or that of another.  
For the purposes of this offense it shall be understood that an intermediary has not given its destination, or has disposed, in whole or in part, of the money, securities or securities obtained for the amount of the price or on account of the real property object of the transfer of ownership or of the real encumbrance, if he does not make a deposit in Nacional Financiera,  
S. A. or in any Deposit Institution, within 30 days following its receipt in favor of its owner or possessor, unless he has delivered it, within that term, to the seller or to the debtor of the real encumbrance, or returned it to the purchaser or to the creditor of the same encumbrance.  

The same sanctions shall be imposed on managers, directors, agents with powers of ownership or administration, administrators of legal entities who do not comply with or cause to be complied with the obligation referred to in the preceding paragraph.  
The deposit shall be delivered by Nacional Financiera,  
S. A. or the Deposit Institution in question, to its owner or to the purchaser.  

When the active subject of the offense returns to the interested parties the amounts of money obtained with his action, before conclusions are formulated in the respective proceeding, the penalty to be applied to him shall be three days to six months imprisonment.  
Fraction added DOF 08-03-1968

XX.- Builders or sellers of condominium buildings who obtain money, securities or securities for the amount of their price or on account of it, if they do not destine them, in whole or in part, to the object of the operation arranged by their disposition for their own benefit or that of another.  
What is provided in this fraction is applicable to what is determined in the second to fifth paragraphs of the preceding fraction.  
Fraction added DOF 08-03-1968

XXI.- Anyone who draws a check against a bank account, which is rejected by the corresponding institution or national credit society, under the terms of applicable legislation, because the drawer does not have an account in the respective institution or society or because the latter lacks sufficient funds for payment. The certification relating to the nonexistence of the account or the lack of sufficient funds for payment, must be made exclusively by personnel specifically authorized for such purpose by the institution or national credit society in question.  

No proceedings shall be taken against the agent when the drawing was not intended to unlawfully procure a thing or obtain an undue profit.  
Fraction added DOF 13-01-1984

Institutions, national societies and Auxiliary Credit Organizations, Surety and Insurance organizations, as well as Official and Decentralized agencies, legally authorized to operate with real property, are excepted from the obligation to constitute the deposit referred to in fraction XIX.  
Paragraph added DOF 08-03-1968. Reformed DOF 13-01-1984
Article reformed DOF 09-03-1946

##### Article 388

Anyone who, for any reason, having charge of the administration or care of another's property, with intent to profit, harms the owner thereof by altering accounts or contract conditions, making non-existent or exaggerated operations or expenses appear, concealing or retaining assets or using them improperly, or knowingly carries out operations detrimental to the owner's patrimony for their own benefit or that of a third party, shall be subject to the penalties provided for the crime of fraud.  
Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 30-12-1975. Added DOF 13-01-1984. Amended DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 388

Bis.- Anyone who places themselves in a state of insolvency, with the purpose of evading their obligations with respect to their creditors, shall be subject to a penalty of six months to four years imprisonment and fifty to three hundred days' fine.  
In case of bankruptcy, the provisions of the special law shall apply.  
Article added DOF 10-01-1994

##### Article 389

The following is equivalent to the crime of fraud and shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to ten years and a fine of four hundred to four thousand pesos: using one's position in government, in a decentralized or state-owned enterprise, or in any labor union organization, or one's relationships with officials or leaders of such organizations, to obtain money, securities, gifts, presents or any other benefit, in exchange for promising or providing employment, a promotion or salary increase in such organizations.  
Article repealed DOF 09-03-1946. Added DOF 05-01-1955. Amended DOF 30-12-1975, 07-01-1980

##### Article 389

Bis.- Anyone who, personally or through an intermediary, causes public or private harm by subdividing and transferring or promising to transfer ownership, possession or any other right over urban or rural land, their own or another's, with or without constructions, without prior permission

from the competent administrative authorities, or when such permission exists but the requirements specified therein have not been satisfied, commits the crime of fraud. This crime shall be punished even in the case of total or partial lack of payment.  
For criminal purposes, subdividing is understood as the division of land into lots.  

This crime shall be punished with the penalties provided in article 386 of this Code, with the exception of the fine mentioned in the third fraction of said provision, which shall be increased up to fifty thousand pesos.

#### CHAPTER III BIS

Extortion

Article added DOF 11-01-1972

Chapter added DOF 13-01-1984

##### Article 390

Anyone who, without right, compels another to give, do, refrain from doing or tolerate something, obtaining a profit for themselves or another or causing someone patrimonial harm, shall be subject to two to eight years imprisonment and forty to one hundred sixty days' fine.  
The penalties shall be increased by up to one half if the coercion is carried out by a criminal association, or by a public servant or former public servant, or by a member or former member of any police force or the Mexican Armed Forces. In this case, the public servant or former public servant and the member or former member of any police force shall also be subject to removal from employment, position or commission and disqualification for one to five years from holding public office or commission, and if it concerns a member of the Mexican Armed Forces in retired, reserve or active status, definitive discharge from the Armed Force to which they belong and disqualification for one to five years from holding public office or commission.

#### Chapter III Ter Family Fraud

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994

Chapter added DOF 14-06-2012

##### Article 390

Bis. Anyone who, to the detriment of the marital community or common patrimony generated during marriage or cohabitation, conceals, transfers or acquires property in the name of third parties, shall be subject to a penalty of one to five years imprisonment and up to three hundred days' fine.  
Article added DOF 14-06-2012

#### CHAPTER IV

Crimes committed by merchants subject to insolvency proceedings

##### Article 391.- (Repealed)

Errata to article DOF 12-09-1931. Repealed DOF 14-01-1985

##### Article 392.- (Repealed)

Article repealed DOF 14-01-1985

##### Article 393.- (Repealed)

Article repealed DOF 14-01-1985

##### Article 394.- (Repealed)

#### CHAPTER V

Article repealed DOF 14-01-1985

Dispossession of immovable property or water

##### Article 395

A penalty of three months to five years imprisonment and a fine of fifty to five hundred pesos shall be imposed:  
I.-  On anyone who, on their own authority and using violence or stealth, or employing threat or deceit, occupies another's immovable property or makes use of it, or of a real right that does not belong to them;  
II.-  On anyone who, on their own authority and using the means indicated in the preceding fraction, occupies immovable property of their own, in cases where the law does not permit it because it is in the possession of another person, or exercises acts of dominion that harm legitimate rights of the occupant, and

III.-	On anyone who, under the terms of the preceding fractions, commits dispossession of water.  

The penalty shall be applicable even when the right to possession of the usurped property is doubtful or in dispute. When the dispossession is carried out by a group or groups that together exceed five persons, in addition to the penalty specified in this article, a penalty of one to six years imprisonment shall be imposed on the intellectual authors and those who lead the invasion.  

Those who repeatedly engage in promoting the dispossession of urban immovable property in the Federal District shall be subject to a penalty of two to nine years imprisonment. Those who have been previously convicted for this form of participation in dispossession, or who have had an order of formal imprisonment issued against them on more than two occasions for this same crime, are considered to repeatedly engage in promoting the dispossession of urban immovable property, except when the corresponding proceeding has resulted in the dismissal of evidence, dismissal of the case, or acquittal of the accused.  
Paragraph added DOF 14-01-1985 Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 09-03-1946

##### Article 396

To the penalties specified in the preceding article shall be added those corresponding to the violence or threat.

#### CHAPTER VI

Damage to another's property

##### Article 397

Imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of one hundred to five thousand pesos shall be imposed on those who cause fire, flood or explosion with damage or danger to:  
I.- A building, dwelling or room where any person is present;  

II.- Clothing, furniture or objects in such a manner that they may cause serious personal harm;  

III.- Public or notarial archives;  
IV.- Libraries, museums, temples, schools or public buildings and monuments, and

V.- Mountains, forests, jungles, pastures, crops or cultivation of any kind.

##### Article 398

If, in addition to the direct damages, any other crime is consummated, the rules of accumulation shall apply.

##### Article 399

When by any means damage, destruction or deterioration of another's property, or of one's own property to the detriment of a third party, is caused, the penalties for simple theft shall apply.

##### Article 399

Bis.- The offenses provided for in this title shall be prosecuted upon complaint by the offended party when they are committed by an ascendant, descendant, spouse, relatives by consanguinity up to the second degree, concubine or cohabitant, adopter or adoptee and relatives by affinity likewise up to the second degree. A complaint shall also be required for the prosecution of third parties who have participated in the execution of the offense with the subjects referred to in the preceding paragraph. If any other act is committed that by itself constitutes an offense, the sanction established by law for such offense shall be applied.

The offenses provided for in articles 380 and 382 to 399 shall be prosecuted upon complaint, except for article 390 and the cases referred to in the last two paragraphs of article 395.  
Paragraph amended DOF 30-12-1991 Amendment DOF 30-12-1991: Deleted from the article the then third paragraph (previously added DOF 14-01-1985)
Article added DOF 13-01-1984
TITLE TWENTY-THIRD
Concealment and Operations with Resources of Illicit Origin
Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Twenty-Fourth) DOF 29-07-1970. Denomination amended DOF 13-05-1996

#### CHAPTER I

Concealment
Chapter added DOF 13-05-1996

##### Article 400

Imprisonment of three months to three years and fifteen to sixty days' fine shall be imposed on whoever:

I.-	With intent to profit, after the execution of the offense and without having participated in it, acquires, receives or conceals the product thereof knowing this circumstance.  
If the person who received the thing by sale, pledge or under any concept, had no knowledge of its illicit origin, for not having taken the indispensable precautions to ensure that the person from whom he received it had the right to dispose of it, the penalty shall be reduced by up to one half;  
Amendment DOF 30-12-1991: Repealed from the fraction the then third paragraph (previously added DOF 23-12-1985)

II.- Provides aid or cooperation of any kind to the perpetrator of an offense, with knowledge of this circumstance, by agreement subsequent to the execution of said offense;  
III.- Conceals or favors the concealment of the person responsible for an offense, the effects, objects or instruments thereof or prevents its investigation;  
Fraction amended DOF 23-12-1985

IV.	When required by the authorities, does not provide assistance for the investigation of offenses or for the prosecution of offenders;  
Fraction amended DOF 23-01-2009

V.	Does not attempt, by the lawful means at his disposal and without risk to his person, to prevent the consummation of offenses that he knows are going to be committed or are being committed, unless he has an obligation to face the risk, in which case the provisions of this article or other applicable rules shall apply;  
Fraction amended DOF 23-01-2009

VI.	Unlawfully alters, modifies or disturbs the place, traces or vestiges of the criminal act, and
Fraction added DOF 23-01-2009

VII.	Diverts or obstructs the investigation of the criminal act in question or favors the accused's evasion of justice.  

Fraction added DOF 23-01-2009

The penalty provided in this article shall not apply in the cases of fractions III, regarding the concealment of the offender, and IV, when it concerns:
a)	Ascendants and descendants by consanguinity or affinity;  

b)	The spouse, concubine, cohabitant and collateral relatives by consanguinity up to the fourth degree, and by affinity up to the second; and
c)	Those who are bound to the offender by love, respect, gratitude or close friendship derived from noble motives.  
Paragraph with subsections added DOF 23-12-1985

The provisions contained in the preceding paragraph and the absolute excuses provided in subsections
a)	to c) shall not be applicable when the offender who is concealed is responsible for the offense of feminicide or homicide.  
Paragraph added DOF 25-04-2023

The judge, taking into account the nature of the action, the personal circumstances of the accused and the others indicated in article 52, may impose in cases of concealment referred to in fractions I, first paragraph and II to IV of this article, instead of the sanctions indicated, up to two thirds of those that would correspond to the perpetrator of the offense; and must state in the judgment the reasons on which he bases the application of the sanction authorized by this paragraph.  
Paragraph added DOF 13-05-1996 Article amended DOF 09-03-1946, 20-01-1967, 14-01-1985

#### CHAPTER II

Operations with resources of illicit origin
Chapter added DOF 13-05-1996

##### Article 400

Bis. Five to fifteen years' imprisonment and one thousand to five thousand days' fine shall be imposed on whoever, by himself or through an intermediary, performs any of the following conducts:  
I.
