Acquires, alienates, administers, holds in custody, possesses, exchanges, converts, deposits, withdraws, gives or receives for any reason, invests, transfers, transports or conveys, within the national territory, from it to abroad or vice versa, resources, rights or property of any nature, when he has knowledge that they proceed from or represent the product of an illicit activity, or

II.	Conceals, covers up or attempts to conceal or cover up the nature, origin, location, destination, movement, ownership or title of resources, rights or property, when he has knowledge that they proceed from or represent the product of an illicit activity.  
For purposes of this Chapter, resources, rights or property of any nature shall be understood to be the product of an illicit activity when there are founded indications or certainty that they come directly or indirectly from, or represent the profits derived from the commission of any offense and their legitimate origin cannot be proven.  

In case of conducts provided for in this Chapter, in which services of institutions that comprise the financial system are used, to proceed criminally the prior complaint of the Ministry of Finance and Public Credit shall be required.  

When the Ministry of Finance and Public Credit, in the exercise of its audit powers, finds elements that allow presumption of the commission of any of the offenses referred to in this Chapter, it must exercise with respect to them the verification powers conferred upon it by law and report the facts that may probably constitute such illicit acts.  
Article added DOF 09-03-1946. Amended DOF 20-01-1967, 14-01-1985, 13-05-1996, 14-03-2014

##### Article 400

Bis 1. The penalties provided for in this Chapter shall be increased from one-third up to one-half, when the person who carries out any of the conducts provided for in Article 400 Bis of this Code has the character of counselor, administrator, official, employee, attorney-in-fact or service provider of any person subject to the regime for prevention of operations with resources of illicit origin, or carries them out within the two years following separation from any of said positions.

In addition, they shall be disqualified from holding employment, office or commission in legal entities subject to the prevention regime for a time equal to that of the prison sentence imposed. The disqualification shall begin to run from the time the prison sentence has been served.  
The penalties provided for in this Chapter shall be doubled, if the conduct is committed by public servants in charge of preventing, detecting, reporting, investigating or judging the commission of crimes or executing criminal sanctions, as well as former public servants in charge of such functions who commit said conduct within two years after their termination. In addition, they shall be disqualified from holding employment, office or commission for a time equal to that of the prison sentence imposed. The disqualification shall begin to run from the time the prison sentence has been served.

Likewise, the penalties provided for in this Chapter shall be increased up to one-half if the person who carries out any of the conducts provided for in Article 400 Bis, sections I and II, uses persons under eighteen years of age or persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or who do not have the capacity to resist it.  
Article added DOF 14-03-2014

TITLE TWENTY-FOURTH
Electoral Crimes and regarding the National Registry of Citizens
Title added DOF 15-08-1990

SOLE CHAPTER
Chapter added DOF 15-08-1990

##### Article 401.- For the purposes of this Chapter, the following is understood as

I.	Public Servants, persons who fall within the assumptions established by Article 212 of this Code.  
Officials and employees of the State and Municipal Public Administration shall also be understood as Public Servants;  
II.	Electoral officials, those who in the terms of federal electoral legislation integrate the bodies that fulfill electoral functions;  
III.	Party officials, the leaders of national political parties and political groups, and their representatives before the electoral bodies, in the terms of federal electoral legislation;  
IV.	Candidates, citizens formally registered as such by the competent authority;  

V.	Public electoral documents, the records of election day, those relating to the scrutiny and count of each of the elections, electoral packages and polling station files, the detailed records of the count sessions of the local and district councils, and those of the counts of plurinominal constituencies and, in general all documents and records issued in the exercise of their functions by the bodies of the Federal Electoral Institute; and

VI.	Electoral materials, the physical elements, such as ballot boxes, voting booths or modular elements for casting votes, credential markers, indelible liquid, stationery and other equipment authorized for use in polling stations during election day.  
Article added DOF 15-08-1990. Amended DOF 22-11-1996

##### Article 402

For the commission of any of the crimes included in this Chapter, in addition to the penalty indicated, disqualification from one to five years may be imposed, and where applicable, removal from office.  
Article added DOF 15-08-1990. Amended DOF 25-03-1994

##### Article 403

A fine of ten to one hundred days and imprisonment of six months to three years shall be imposed on whoever:  
I.	Votes knowing that he does not meet the requirements of the law;  

II.	Votes more than once in the same election;  

III.	Engages in proselytism or objectively pressures voters on election day inside polling stations or at the place where voters are lined up, in order to influence the direction of their vote;  

IV.	Willfully obstructs or interferes with the normal development of voting, scrutiny and counting, the transport and delivery of packages and electoral documentation, or the adequate exercise of the tasks of electoral officials;  
V.	Collects at any time, without cause provided by law, voting credentials from citizens;  
VI.	Solicits votes for pay, gift, promise of money or other reward during electoral campaigns or on election day;  
VII.	On election day violates, in any manner, the citizen's right to cast his vote in secret;  
VIII.	Votes or attempts to vote with a voting credential of which he is not the holder;  
IX.	On election day carries out the transportation of voters, restricting or attempting to restrict their freedom to cast their vote;  
X.	Unlawfully introduces into or removes from ballot boxes one or more ballots, or seizes, destroys or alters ballots, documents or electoral materials, or in any way prevents their transport or delivery to the competent bodies;  
XI.	Obtains or requests a signed declaration from the voter regarding his intention or the direction of his vote, or through threat or promise of pay or gift, commits his vote in favor of a particular political party or candidate;  

XII.	Violently prevents the installation of a polling station, or willfully engages in any conduct that has the purpose of preventing the normal installation of the polling station; or
XIII.	During the eight days prior to the election and until the official closing time of the polling stations located in the most western time zone areas of the national territory, publishes or disseminates by any means the results of polls or opinion surveys that reveal the preferences of citizens.  
Article added DOF 15-08-1990. Amended DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

##### Article 404

A fine of up to 500 days shall be imposed on ministers of religious worship who, in the conduct of public acts proper to their ministry, expressly induce the electorate to vote for or against a candidate or political party, or to abstain from exercising the right to vote.  
Article added DOF 15-08-1990. Amended DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

##### Article 405

A fine of fifty to two hundred days and imprisonment of two to six years shall be imposed on the electoral official who:
Paragraph amended DOF 25-03-1994

I.-	Alters in any manner, substitutes, destroys or makes improper use of documents relating to the Federal Register of Voters;  
II.	Fails to comply, without justified cause, with the obligations inherent to his or her position, to the detriment of the electoral process;  
Section amended DOF 22-11-1996

III.-	Obstructs the normal development of voting without justified cause;  

IV.	Alters electoral results, removes or destroys ballots, documents or electoral materials;  
Section amended DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

V.	Does not deliver or prevents the timely delivery of documents or electoral materials, without justified cause;  
Section amended DOF 22-11-1996

VI.	In the exercise of his or her functions exerts pressure on voters and objectively induces them to vote for a particular candidate or party, inside the polling station or in the place where the voters themselves are lined up;  
Section amended DOF 22-11-1996

VII.-	Installs, opens or closes a polling station fraudulently outside the times and forms provided by the law on the matter, installs it in a place other than that legally designated, or prevents its installation;  
Section amended DOF 25-03-1994

VIII.	Without cause provided by law expels or orders the removal from the polling station of representatives of a political party or restricts the rights granted to them by law;  
Section amended DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

IX.-	Repealed.  
Section added DOF 25-03-1994. Repealed DOF 22-11-1996
X.	Permits or tolerates that a citizen cast his or her vote knowing that he or she does not meet the legal requirements or that one or more ballots be illicitly introduced into the ballot boxes; or
Section added DOF 25-03-1994. Amended DOF 22-11-1996

XI.	Spreads, publicly and fraudulently, false news regarding the development of election day or regarding its results.  
Section added DOF 25-03-1994. Amended DOF 22-11-1996
Article added DOF 15-08-1990

##### Article 406

A fine of one hundred to two hundred days and imprisonment of one to six years shall be imposed on the party official or candidate who:
Paragraph amended DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

I.	Exerts pressure on voters and induces them to abstain or to vote for a particular candidate or party inside the polling station or in the place where the voters themselves are lined up;  
Section amended DOF 22-11-1996

II.-	Conducts electoral propaganda while performing his or her functions during election day;  
III.	Removes, destroys, alters or makes improper use of documents or electoral materials;  
Section amended DOF 22-11-1996

IV.	Obstructs the normal development of voting or of acts subsequent thereto without justified cause, or for that purpose threatens or exercises physical violence against electoral officials;  
Section amended DOF 22-11-1996

V.	Spreads, publicly and fraudulently, false news regarding the development of election day or regarding its results;  
Section amended DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

VI.	Prevents with violence the installation, opening or closing of a polling station; or
Section amended DOF 22-11-1996

VII.	Obtains and uses knowingly and in his or her capacity as candidate, funds from illicit activities for his or her electoral campaign.  
Section added DOF 22-11-1996 Article added DOF 15-08-1990

##### Article 407

A fine of two hundred to four hundred days and imprisonment of one to nine years shall be imposed on the public servant who:  
I.	Compels his or her subordinates, expressly and making use of his or her authority or hierarchy, to cast their votes in favor of a political party or candidate;  
Section amended DOF 22-11-1996

II.	Conditions the provision of a public service, the implementation of programs or the execution of public works, within the scope of his or her competence, on the casting of votes in favor of a political party or candidate;  
Section amended DOF 22-11-1996

III.	Allocates, illegally, funds, goods or services at his or her disposal by virtue of his or her position such as vehicles, real estate and equipment, to the support of a political party or a candidate, without prejudice to the penalties that may apply for the crime of embezzlement; or
Section amended DOF 22-11-1996

IV.	Provides support or renders any service to political parties or their candidates, through his or her subordinates, using time corresponding to their work, illegally.  
Section added DOF 22-11-1996

Article added DOF 15-08-1990. Amended DOF 25-03-1994

##### Article 408

A sanction of suspension of political rights for up to six years shall be imposed on those who, having been elected deputies or senators, do not appear, without justified cause in the judgment of the respective Chamber, to perform the position within the time period indicated in the first paragraph of Article 63 of the Constitution.  
Article added DOF 15-08-1990

##### Article 409

A fine of twenty to one hundred days and imprisonment of three months to five years shall be imposed on whoever:
Paragraph amended DOF 25-03-1994

I.-  Provides false documents or information to the National Register of Citizens to obtain the document that accredits citizenship; and
II.-  Alters in any manner, substitutes, destroys or makes improper use of the document that accredits citizenship, which in the terms of the law on the matter, is issued by the National Register of Citizens.  
Article added DOF 15-08-1990

##### Article 410

The penalty referred to in the preceding article may be increased by one fourth if the conduct is committed by personnel of the body responsible for the service of the National Register of Citizens in accordance with the law on the matter, or if the person is of foreign nationality.  
Article added DOF 15-08-1990

##### Article 411

A fine of seventy to two hundred days and imprisonment of three to seven years shall be imposed on whoever by any means alters or participates in the alteration of the Federal Register of Voters, the nominal lists or in the illicit issuance of Voter credentials.  
Article added DOF 25-03-1994. Amended DOF 22-11-1996

##### Article 412

Imprisonment of two to nine years shall be imposed on the party official or the organizers of campaign events who, knowingly, illicitly take advantage of funds, goods or services in the terms of section III of Article 407 of this Code. In the commission of this crime there shall be no benefit of provisional release.  
Article added DOF 25-03-1994

##### Article 413

Those responsible for the crimes contained in this chapter for having agreed to or prepared their commission in the terms of section I of Article 13 of this Code may not enjoy the benefit of provisional release.  
Article added DOF 25-03-1994

TITLE TWENTY-FIFTH
Crimes Against the Environment and Environmental Management
Title added DOF 13-12-1996. Denomination added DOF 06-02-2002

#### CHAPTER ONE

On technological and hazardous activities
Chapter added DOF 13-12-1996. Chapter renumbered and denomination amended DOF 06-02-2002 (formerly Single Chapter)

##### Article 414

A penalty of one to nine years of imprisonment and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who unlawfully, or without applying prevention or security measures, carries out activities of production, storage, trafficking, import or export, transportation, abandonment, disposal, discharge, or carries out any other activity with substances considered hazardous due to their corrosive, reactive, explosive, toxic, flammable, radioactive or other analogous characteristics, or

orders or authorizes it, that causes damage to natural resources, to flora, to fauna, to ecosystems, to water quality, to soil, to subsoil or to the environment.  
The same penalty shall apply to anyone who unlawfully carries out the conducts with the substances listed in the preceding paragraph, or with ozone-depleting substances and causes a risk of damage to natural resources, to flora, to fauna, to ecosystems, to water quality or to the environment.  

In the event that the activities referred to in the preceding paragraphs are carried out in a protected natural area, the prison sentence shall be increased by up to three years and the economic penalty by up to one thousand days' fine, with the exception of activities carried out with ozone-depleting substances.  

When the conducts referred to in the first and second paragraphs of this article are carried out in urban areas with waste oils or ozone-depleting substances in quantities not exceeding 200 liters, or with waste considered hazardous due to their biological-infectious characteristics, up to half of the penalty provided in this article shall be applied, unless they are repeated conducts with quantities less than those indicated when they exceed said quantity.  
Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Amended DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 415

A penalty of one to nine years of imprisonment and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who, without applying prevention or security measures:  
I.	Emits, releases, discharges into the atmosphere, authorizes or orders it, gases, fumes, dust or pollutants that cause damage to natural resources, to fauna, to flora, to ecosystems or to the environment, provided that such emissions come from stationary sources of federal jurisdiction, in accordance with the provisions of the General Law of Ecological Balance and Environmental Protection, or

II.	Generates emissions of noise, vibrations, thermal or light energy, coming from emitting sources of federal jurisdiction, in accordance with the regulation indicated in the preceding section, that cause damage to natural resources, to flora, to fauna, to ecosystems or to the environment.  

The same penalties shall apply to anyone who unlawfully carries out the activities described in the preceding sections, that cause a risk to natural resources, to flora, to fauna, to ecosystems or to the environment.  
In the event that the activities referred to in this article are carried out in a protected natural area, the prison sentence shall be increased by up to three years and the economic penalty by up to one thousand days' fine.  
Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Amended DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 416

A penalty of one to nine years of imprisonment and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who unlawfully discharges, deposits, or infiltrates, authorizes or orders it, wastewater, chemical or biochemical liquids, waste or pollutants in soils, subsoils, marine waters, rivers, basins, vessels or other deposits or water currents of federal jurisdiction, that causes a risk of damage or damages natural resources, flora, fauna, water quality, ecosystems or the environment.  

When it involves waters that are deposited, flow in or toward a protected natural area, the imprisonment shall be increased by up to three more years and the economic penalty by up to one thousand days' fine.  
Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Amended DOF 06-02-2002

CHAPTER TWO
On biodiversity
Chapter added DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 417

A penalty of one to nine years of imprisonment and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who introduces into national territory, or traffics with forest resources, wild flora or fauna, living or dead, their products or derivatives, that carry, suffer or have suffered, as applicable, some contagious disease, that causes or may cause its dissemination or propagation or contagion to flora, to fauna, to forest resources or to ecosystems.  
Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Amended DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 418

A penalty of six months to nine years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred to three thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update in force shall be imposed, provided that such activities are not carried out in urban areas, on anyone who, without having prior authorization from the competent authority:
Paragraph amended DOF 08-05-2023

I.	Clears or destroys forest vegetation;  
Section amended DOF 08-05-2023

II.	Cuts, uproots, fells or cuts down one or more trees, or

III.	Changes the land use in forest lands without the authorization issued by the competent authority.  
Section amended DOF 08-05-2023

The prison sentence must be increased by up to four more years and the fine by up to five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update in force in the event that the conducts referred to in the sections of the first paragraph of this article affect a protected natural area.  
Paragraph amended DOF 08-05-2023

When the conducts referred to in this article are carried out using firearms or by any other violent means against persons or to obtain a profit or economic benefit, a penalty of three to twelve years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update in force shall be imposed.  
Paragraph added DOF 08-05-2023 Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Amended DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 419

Anyone who, without an administrative act authorizing it or not having documentation proving legal origin, transports, trades, alienates, distributes, supplies, collects, buys, receives, acquires, stores, safeguards, possesses or transforms forest raw materials or timber forest products, shall be imposed the following penalties:  
I.	When the volume does not exceed two cubic meters, two to five years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to three thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update in force shall be imposed.  
II.	If the volume is greater than two cubic meters, six to twelve years of imprisonment and a fine of one thousand to five thousand times the value of the Unit of Measurement and Update in force shall be imposed.  
The custodial sentences referred to in the preceding sections shall be increased by up to four years of imprisonment and a fine of up to five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update in force, when the forest raw materials or timber forest products come from a protected natural area.  
Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Amended DOF 24-12-1996, 06-02-2002, 08-05-2023

##### Article 419

Bis.- A penalty of six months to five years of imprisonment and the equivalent of two hundred to two thousand days' fine shall be imposed on whoever:  
I. Breeds or trains a dog with the purpose of having it participate in any exhibition, show or activity that involves a fight between two or more dogs for recreational, entertainment or any other purposes;  
II. Possesses, transports, purchases or sells dogs with the purpose of involving them in any exhibition, show or activity that implies a fight between two or more dogs;  
III. Organizes, promotes, advertises, sponsors or sells tickets to attend shows that involve dog fights;  
IV. Possesses or manages a property where dog fights are held with knowledge of such activity;  
V. Causes minors to attend or witness any exhibition, show or activity that involves a fight between two or more dogs, or  
VI. Performs with or without profit any act with the objective of involving dogs in any exhibition, show or activity that implies a fight between two or more dogs.  
The sanction referred to in the preceding paragraph shall be increased by one half when it involves public servants.  

Criminal liability is also incurred by whoever attends as a spectator any exhibition, show or activity that involves a fight between two or more dogs, with knowledge of this circumstance. In such cases one third of the penalty provided in this article shall be imposed.  
Article added DOF 22-06-2017

##### Article 420

A penalty of one to nine years of imprisonment and the equivalent of three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on whoever unlawfully:  
I. Captures, harms or deprives of life any specimen of turtle or marine mammal, or collects or stores in any manner their products or by-products;  
II. Captures, transforms, gathers, transports or harms specimens of aquatic species declared under closed season;  
II Bis. Intentionally captures, transforms, gathers, transports, destroys or trades with the aquatic species denominated abalone, shrimp, sea cucumber and lobster, within or outside closed season periods, without having the corresponding authorization, in a quantity exceeding 10 kilograms of weight.  
Subsection added DOF 08-02-2006. Amended DOF 07-04-2017

III. Carries out hunting, fishing or capture activities with a non-permitted means, of any specimen of a wildlife species, or puts at risk the biological viability of a population or wild species;  
IV. Carries out any activity for trafficking purposes, or captures, possesses, transports, gathers, introduces into the country or extracts from it, any specimen, its products or by-products and other genetic resources, of a species of wild flora or fauna, terrestrial or aquatic under closed season,

considered endemic, threatened, in danger of extinction, subject to special protection, or regulated by any international treaty to which Mexico is a party, or  
V. Harms any specimen of the species of wild flora or fauna, terrestrial or aquatic referred to in the preceding subsection.  
An additional penalty of up to three more years of imprisonment and up to one thousand additional days' fine shall be applied when the conducts described in this article are carried out in or affect a natural protected area, or when they are carried out for commercial purposes.  
In the cases provided in subsection IV of this article and subsection X of Article 2 of the Federal Law against Organized Crime, the penalty of five to fifteen years of imprisonment and the equivalent of three thousand to six thousand days' fine shall be imposed when it involves any specimen, parts, derivatives, products or by-products of the species totoaba macdonaldi.  
Paragraph added DOF 19-02-2021 Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Amended DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 420

Bis.- A penalty of two to ten years of imprisonment and the equivalent of three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on whoever unlawfully:  
I. Harms, drains or fills wetlands, mangroves, lagoons, estuaries or swamps;  

II. Harms reefs;  
III. Introduces or releases into the natural environment any specimen of exotic flora or fauna that harms an ecosystem, or that hinders, alters or affects native or migratory species in the natural cycles of their reproduction or migration, or  
IV. Provokes a fire in a forest, jungle, natural vegetation or forestlands, that harms natural elements, flora, fauna, ecosystems or the environment.  

An additional penalty of up to two years of imprisonment and up to one thousand additional days' fine shall be applied when the conducts described in this article are carried out in or affect a natural protected area, or the author or participant in the crime provided in subsection IV carries out the conduct to obtain profit or economic benefit.  

CHAPTER THREE
On biosafety

Article added DOF 06-02-2002

Chapter added DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 420

Ter.- A penalty of one to nine years of imprisonment and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on whoever in contravention of what is established in the applicable regulations, introduces into the country, or extracts from it, trades, transports, stores or releases into the environment, any genetically modified organism that alters or may negatively alter the components, structure or functioning of natural ecosystems.  

For purposes of this article, genetically modified organism shall be understood as any organism that possesses a new combination of genetic material that has been obtained through the application of biotechnology, including derivatives of genetic engineering techniques.  
Article added DOF 06-02-2002
CHAPTER FOUR

Crimes against environmental management
Chapter added DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 420

Quáter.- A penalty of one to four years of imprisonment and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on whoever:  
I. Transports or consents, authorizes or orders the transport of any waste considered hazardous due to its corrosive, reactive, explosive, toxic, flammable, biological infectious or radioactive characteristics, to a destination for which there is no authorization to receive, store, dispose of or abandon it;  
II. Enters false data in records, logbooks or any other document used with the purpose of simulating compliance with obligations derived from federal environmental regulations;  
III. Destroys, alters or conceals information, records, reports or any other document that is required to be maintained or archived in accordance with federal environmental regulations;  
IV. While providing services as a technical auditor, specialist or expert in environmental impact, forestry, wildlife, fishing or other environmental matters, falsifies the truth causing harm to natural resources, flora, fauna, ecosystems, water quality or the environment, or  
V. Does not carry out or comply with the technical, corrective or safety measures necessary to avoid environmental damage or risk that the administrative or judicial authority orders or imposes.  
The crimes provided in this Chapter shall be prosecuted upon complaint by the Federal Attorney for Environmental Protection.  
Article added DOF 06-02-2002

CHAPTER FIVE
Common provisions for crimes against the environment
Chapter added DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 421

In addition to what is established in the previous chapters of the Twenty-Fifth Title, the following penalties and security measures shall be imposed:
Paragraph reformed DOF 07-06-2013

I. The repair and, where applicable, compensation for damage to the environment, in accordance with the provisions of the Federal Environmental Liability Law;
Clause reformed DOF 07-06-2013

II. The suspension, modification or demolition of constructions, works or activities, as appropriate, that gave rise to the respective environmental crime;
III. The reincorporation of natural elements, specimens or species of wild flora and fauna, to the habitats from which they were removed, provided that their reincorporation does not constitute a danger to ecological balance or hinder the reproduction or migration of wild flora or fauna species;

IV. The return of hazardous materials or waste or specimens of threatened or endangered wild flora and fauna, to the country of origin, considering the provisions of international treaties and conventions to which Mexico is a party, or

V. Disqualification, when the author or participant in the crime has the status of public servant, for a time equal to that which has been fixed as a custodial sentence, which must run at the moment the convicted person has served the imprisonment or it has been deemed served.

The community service referred to in article 24 of this ordinance shall consist of activities related to environmental protection or the restoration of natural resources.
For the purposes referred to in this article, the judge must request from the competent federal agency or from higher education or scientific research institutions, the issuance of the corresponding technical opinion.

The competent public administration agencies must provide the public prosecutor or the judge with the technical or expert opinions required in connection with complaints filed for the commission of the crimes referred to in this Title.
The minimum and maximum parameters of the prison sentences referred to in this Title shall be reduced by half, when the accused or defendant voluntarily repairs or compensates for the damage to the environment before such obligation has been imposed by administrative resolution or judicial sentence. Such reduction shall also proceed when the repair or compensation of the damage is carried out or guaranteed in terms of the provisions of Title Two of the Federal Environmental Liability Law.
Paragraph reformed DOF 07-06-2013

Persons legitimized in terms of the provisions of the Federal Environmental Liability Law shall be considered victims with the right to request repair or compensation for environmental damage and to assist in the criminal process.
Paragraph added DOF 07-06-2013 Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Reformed DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 422

In the case of crimes against the environment, when the author or participant has the status of guarantor with respect to the protected assets, the prison sentence shall be increased by up to three years.
Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Reformed DOF 06-02-2002

##### Article 423

No penalty shall be applied to anyone who engages in the conduct indicated in the first paragraph, second clause, of article 418, nor to anyone who transports the firewood or dead wood referred to in article 419, when they carry out the activity for domestic use within the rural, indigenous or Afro-Mexican community to which they belong.
Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Reformed DOF 06-02-2002, 08-05-2023

TITLE TWENTY-SIXTH
Crimes in the Matter of Copyright
Title added DOF 24-12-1996

##### Article 424

Imprisonment of six months to six years and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed:

I. On anyone who speculates in any form with the free textbooks distributed by the Ministry of Public Education;
II. On the editor, producer or recorder who knowingly produces more copies of a work protected by the Federal Copyright Law than those authorized by the rights holder;

III. On anyone who uses in a fraudulent manner, for profit and without the corresponding authorization, works protected by the Federal Copyright Law.
Clause reformed DOF 19-05-1997, 17-05-1999

IV. Repealed.

Clause repealed DOF 17-05-1999 Article added DOF 24-12-1996

##### Article 424

bis.- Imprisonment of three to ten years and two thousand to twenty thousand days' fine shall be imposed:

I. On anyone who produces, reproduces, introduces into the country, stores, transports, distributes, sells or leases copies of works, phonograms, videograms or books, protected by the Federal Copyright Law, in a fraudulent manner, for the purpose of commercial speculation and without the authorization that in the terms of the cited Law must be granted by the holder of copyright or related rights.

The same penalty shall be imposed on those who, knowingly, contribute or provide in any way, raw materials or inputs intended for the production or reproduction of works, phonograms, videograms or books referred to in the preceding paragraph;
Paragraph reformed DOF 01-07-2020

II. On anyone who manufactures for profit a device or system whose purpose is to deactivate the electronic protection devices of a computer program, or
Clause reformed DOF 01-07-2020

III. On anyone who records, transmits or makes a total or partial copy of a protected cinematographic work, exhibited in a movie theater or places that serve as such, without the authorization of the copyright holder or related rights.
Clause added DOF 01-07-2020 Article added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Article 424

ter.- Imprisonment of six months to six years and five thousand to thirty thousand days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who sells to any final consumer in public roads or places, in a fraudulent manner, for purposes of commercial speculation, copies of works, phonograms, videograms or books, referred to in clause I of the preceding article.
If the sale is carried out in commercial establishments, or in an organized or permanent manner, the provisions of article 424 Bis of this Code shall apply.
Article added DOF 17-05-1999

##### Article 425

Imprisonment of six months to two years or three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who knowingly and without right exploits for profit a performance or an execution.
Article added DOF 24-12-1996

##### Article 426

Imprisonment of six months to four years and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed in the following cases:
I. On anyone who manufactures, modifies, imports, distributes, sells or leases a device or system to decrypt an encrypted satellite signal, carrying programs, without authorization from the legitimate distributor of said signal;
Clause reformed DOF 01-07-2020

II. On anyone who carries out for profit any act with the purpose of decrypting an encrypted satellite signal, carrying programs, without authorization from the legitimate distributor of said signal;

Clause reformed DOF 01-07-2020

III. On anyone who manufactures or distributes equipment intended for the reception of an encrypted cable signal carrying programs, without authorization from the legitimate distributor of said signal, or
Clause added DOF 01-07-2020

IV. On anyone who receives or assists another to receive an encrypted cable signal carrying programs without the authorization of the legitimate distributor of said signal.
Clause added DOF 01-07-2020 Article added DOF 24-12-1996

##### Article 427

Imprisonment of six months to six years and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who knowingly publishes a work substituting the author's name with another name.
Article added DOF 24-12-1996

##### Artículo 427

Bis.- Whoever, knowingly and for profit, circumvents without authorization any effective technological protection measure used by phonogram producers, artists, performers or executants, as well as authors of any work protected by copyright or related rights, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to six years and a fine of five hundred to one thousand days.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 01-07-2020

##### Artículo 427

Ter.- Whoever, for profit, manufactures, imports, distributes, rents or in any manner commercializes devices, products or components intended to circumvent an effective technological protection measure used by phonogram producers, artists, performers or executants, as well as authors of any work protected by copyright or related rights, shall be subject to six months to six years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to one thousand days.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 01-07-2020

##### Artículo 427

Quáter.- Whoever, for profit, provides or offers services to the public intended primarily to circumvent an effective technological protection measure used by phonogram producers, artists, performers or executants, as well as authors of any work protected by copyright or related rights, shall be subject to six months to six years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to one thousand days.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 01-07-2020

##### Artículo 427

Quinquies.- Whoever, knowingly, without authorization and for profit, removes or alters, by himself or through another, any rights management information, shall be subject to six months to six years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to one thousand days.  
The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever for profit:  
I.	Distributes or imports for distribution rights management information, knowing that it has been removed or altered without authorization, or
II.	Distributes, imports for distribution, transmits, communicates or makes available to the public copies of works, performances, executions or phonograms, knowing that the rights management information has been removed or altered without authorization.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 01-07-2020

##### Artículo 428

The pecuniary sanctions provided for in this title shall be applied without prejudice to the reparation of damages, the amount of which may not be less than forty percent of the public sale price of each product or the provision of services that involve violation of one or more of the rights protected by the Federal Copyright Law.  
Artículo adicionado DOF 24-12-1996

##### Article 429

The offenses provided for in this Title shall be prosecuted ex officio, except as provided in articles 424, section II, 424 Bis, section III and 427.  
Article added DOF 24-12-1996. Amended DOF 28-06-2010, 01-07-2020

TRANSITORY ARTICLES:
1º.- This Code shall enter into force on the 17th day of September 1931.  

2º.- From that same date the Penal Code of December 15, 1929, is abrogated, as well as all other laws that are contrary to the present one; but both that Code and the one of December 7, 1871, shall continue to be applied for acts executed, respectively, during their validity, unless the accused manifest their will to avail themselves of the ordinance they deem most favorable, between the present Code and the one that was in force at the time of the perpetration of the offense.  
Erratum to the article DOF 31-08-1931

3º.- The provisions of a penal nature contained in special laws remain in force in all that is not provided for in this Code.  

Therefore, I order that it be printed, published, circulated and duly complied with.  

Given at the Palace of the Federal Executive Power, in Mexico, on the thirteenth day of the month of August nineteen hundred thirty-one.- P. Ortiz Rubio.- Signature.- The Undersecretary of the Interior, In Charge of the Office, Octavio Mendoza González.- Signature.  

Which I communicate to you for its publication and other purposes. Effective Suffrage. No Reelection.  
Mexico,  
D.F., on August 14, 1931.- The Undersecretary of the Interior. In Charge of the Office,
Octavio Mendoza González.- Signature.

LAW of General Provisions relating to the suspension of individual guarantees.
Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 13, 1942

##### ARTICLE 12

The restriction of the guarantees of articles 16, 19 and 20, is applicable only to crimes of federal jurisdiction included in book II, titles I to VI and title IX of the Federal Penal Code, as well as those enumerated in the decree of October 30, 1941, which reformed the Penal Code and those that are created in emergency legislation.
TRANSITORY:
1°.- This law shall enter into force on the day of its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

2°.- Amparo proceedings in process upon the entry into force of this law shall follow their normal course, whatever their nature may be and shall be resolved in accordance with the laws in force without being affected by the provisions of this law.
3º.- The guarantee enshrined in the third paragraph of constitutional article 22 is not restricted for now.

DECREE that declares as Emergency Law chapter 1º, title 7º, book 2º, of the Penal Code for the Federal District and Territories and Federal throughout the Republic in matters of this jurisdiction relating to the possession and trafficking of narcotics.
Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on May 12, 1945

##### ARTICLE FIRST

TRANSITORY:
This Decree shall take effect from its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

DECREE that lifts the suspension of guarantees decreed on June 1, 1942, and restores constitutional order, ratifying and declaring in force the provisions that it specifies.
Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on December 28, 1945

##### ARTICLE 1°

As of the first day of October, the suspension of guarantees decreed on the first of June 1942 is lifted and, therefore, constitutional order is restored in full.
TRANSITORY:

##### ARTICLE 1°

This law shall enter into force on the first day of October of the current year.

##### ARTICLE 2°

This law repeals legal and regulatory provisions that in any way oppose it.

##### ARTICLE 3°

The Executive of the Union shall issue the regulatory provisions for the execution of this law.

LAW creating the Tutelary Councils for Minor Offenders of the Federal District and Territories.
Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on August 2, 1974

TRANSITORY PROVISIONS
ARTICLE 1 Transitory.- This Law shall enter into force thirty days after its publication in the Official Gazette, and from the same date articles 119 to 122 of the Criminal Code for the Federal District and Territories in matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in matters of Federal Jurisdiction, of August 13, 1931, shall be repealed only with respect to the Federal District and Territories, the Organic Law and Rules of Procedure of the Juvenile Courts and their Auxiliary Institutions in the Federal District and Territories, of April 22, 1941 and other provisions that oppose this Ordinance.

ARTICLE 2 Transitory.- The first rotation among the Counselors for the purposes of the instruction of the proceeding shall be established according to the chronological order of their appointment.
ARTICLE 3 Transitory.- The Counselors who are appointed upon the entry into force of this Law, in the terms of article 5, shall serve in their position until December 31, 1976.
ARTICLE 4 Transitory.- Both the proceedings that are being developed at the commencement of its validity and the measures imposed prior to the same, that are in the process of execution, shall be subject to the provisions of this Law.
ARTICLE 5 Transitory.- While the Auxiliary Councils are established, the Qualifying Judges shall have jurisdiction over violations of police and good government regulations and the Tutelary Council over the other cases indicated in article 48.

TRANSITORY

##### ARTICLE ONE

This Decree shall enter into force 90 days after its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.  
ARTICLE TWO.- With respect to persons who are being prosecuted or sentenced on the day this Decree enters into force, the provisions of article 56 of the Penal Code itself for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction shall apply.  

ARTICLE THREE.- For the imposition of fines under the day-fine system referred to in article 29 of the Penal Code, reformed in the terms of this Decree, the judge shall adhere to the following rules:

I.-  When a fine is imposed in pesos, the respective conversion shall be made taking into account the maximum fine set by law, with the corresponding ones indicated below: when the maximum is five hundred pesos, for one day-fine; if it exceeds this amount, but not ten thousand pesos, between two and twenty day-fines; if it is greater than ten thousand pesos, but does not exceed one hundred thousand, from twenty-one to two hundred day-fines; and if it exceeds one hundred thousand pesos, between two hundred one and five hundred day-fines.  

II.-  When a fine is established on the basis of days of minimum wage, it shall be converted at the rate of one day of wage per one day-fine.  
ARTICLE FOUR.- With respect to the regime applicable to the unimputables referred to in Article 15 section II of the Penal Code, reformed in the terms of this decree, the provisions for the mentally ill, in the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures, shall apply, the same regime that shall apply for infractions of common jurisdiction.  
ARTICLE FIVE.- The measures of surveillance by the authority and in compliance with the substitutes for imprisonment referred to in the Penal Code, reformed in the terms of this decree, shall be the responsibility of the agency of the Federal Executive, in charge of the execution of sanctions.  
ARTICLE SIX.- For the purposes of recognition of the innocence of the subject referred to in Article 96 of the Penal Code, reformed in the terms of this decree, the provisions for necessary pardon, both in the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures, and in the Code of Criminal Procedures for the Federal District, as applicable, shall apply.

LAW for the treatment of Minor Offenders, for the Federal District in Common Matters and for the entire Republic in Federal Matters.  
Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on December 24, 1991

TRANSITORY
FIRST.- This Law shall enter into force sixty days following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.  
SECOND.- The Law creating the Guardianship Council for Minor Offenders of the Federal District, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on August 2, 1974, is hereby abrogated.  
THIRD.- Articles 119 to 122, of the Criminal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction; 73 to 78 of the Organic Law of the Judicial Power of the Federation, and 503 of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures; as well as articles 673 and 674, sections II and X, of the Code of Criminal Procedures for the Federal District, solely with regard to minor offenders, are hereby repealed.  

FOURTH.- Matters that are in process on the date this Law enters into force shall be transferred to the competent areas that correspond to hear them, in accordance with the new determination of competencies.  
FIFTH.- The regulations of the diagnostic and treatment centers must be issued within ninety days following the date of installation of the Council for Minors.  
SIXTH.- The currently existing auxiliary councils shall hear administrative violations of police and good government regulations committed by minors, until the competent body is established. These councils may only apply the guidance and protection measures provided for in this Law.  

SEVENTH.- Until the Council for Minors has integrated its expert services, it may be assisted by the corresponding bodies of the Attorney General's Office of the Republic, the Attorney General's Office of Justice of the Federal District and the Superior Court of Justice of the Federal District.

DECREE reforming and adding various provisions of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction; of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures; of the Federal Law on Firearms and Explosives; of the Code of Criminal Procedures for the Federal District; and of the Fiscal Code of the Federation.  
Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on July 22, 1994

##### ARTICLE ONE

Appendix 1 referred to in article 195 bis of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction, and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction, is hereby reformed to read as follows:
………
TRANSITORY ARTICLES

FIRST.- This decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.  
SECOND.- Persons who have committed an offense contemplated in this decree, prior to its entry into force, including those prosecuted or sentenced, shall be subject to the provisions of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction in force at the time the offense was committed, without prejudice to applying, when appropriate, the provisions of article 56 of said substantive Code.  

THIRD.- All legal, regulatory and administrative provisions that oppose this Decree are hereby repealed.  
Errata notice DOF 01-08-1994

DECREE reforming, adding and repealing various articles of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction, the Fiscal Code of the Federation, the Code of Criminal Procedures for the Federal District and the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures.  
Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on May 13, 1996

##### ARTICLE ONE

Articles 7, section III, 64, second and third paragraphs, 65, 70, sections I, II and III, 185, 189, 243, 253 first paragraph and section I, in its subsections e) second paragraph and g), 289, 364 first paragraph and section I, 366 first paragraph with its sections, second and third paragraphs, and 400 bis are hereby reformed; a third paragraph to article 63, a second and third paragraph to article 65, a final paragraph to article 70 and another to article 158, article 196 ter, a second paragraph to article 243, subsection i) to section I of article 253, sections VII and VIII to article 254, article 254 ter, a second and third paragraph to section I of article 364, a new article 366 bis, whereupon the current article 366 bis is renumbered to become article 366 ter, section III to article 368, articles 368 bis and 368 ter, a third paragraph to article 371, article 377 and a final paragraph to article 400 are hereby added; the denomination of Title Twenty-Third of Book Two is modified and divided into two chapters; and article 321 of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction, and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction, is hereby repealed, to read as follows:

..........  
TRANSITORY

FIRST.- This decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.  
SECOND.- Article 115 bis of the Fiscal Code of the Federation, in force until the entry into force of this decree, shall continue to apply for acts performed during its validity. Likewise, said provision shall continue to apply to persons prosecuted or sentenced for offenses provided for and punished by the aforementioned article.  
To proceed criminally in the cases referred to in article 115 bis of the Fiscal Code of the Federation, under the terms of the preceding paragraph, the complaint of the Ministry of Finance and Public Credit shall continue to be required.  

For purposes of applying the respective penalties, the provisions of article 56 of the aforementioned Penal Code shall govern, without this implying the extinction of the criminal types.  
THIRD.- For the assumptions, subjects and effects of the preceding article, the offenses provided for in article 115 bis of the Fiscal Code of the Federation shall continue to be classified as serious, under the terms of article 194 of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures, for all applicable legal effects.  
Mexico City,  
F.D., April 29, 1996.- Sen. Miguel Alemán Velasco, President.- Rep. María Claudia Esqueda Llanes, President.- Sen. Raúl Juárez Valencia, Secretary.- Rep. Sergio Vázquez Olivas, Secretary.- Signatures".  
In compliance with the provisions of section I of Article 89 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, and for its due publication and observance, I issue this Decree at the residence of the Federal Executive Power, in Mexico City, Federal District, on the ninth day of

the month of May of the year one thousand nine hundred ninety-six.- Ernesto Zedillo Ponce de León.- Signature.- The Secretary of the Interior, Emilio Chuayffet Chemor.- Signature.

DECREE by which various provisions of the Federal Code of Electoral Institutions and Procedures; of the Regulatory Law of Sections I and II of Article 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States; of the Organic Law of the Judicial Power of the Federation; of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction; of the Statute of Government of the Federal District are reformed, added and repealed; and the General Law of the System of Means of Challenge in Electoral Matters is issued.  
Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on November 22, 1996

ARTICLE FIFTH.- Articles 401; 403 and 404; sections II, IV through VI, VIII, X and XI of article 405; the first paragraph and sections I, III through VI of article 406; sections I through III of article 407; and article 411 ARE REFORMED. Section XIII is ADDED to article 403; section VII to article 406; and a section IV to article 407. Section IX of article 405 IS REPEALED; all of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction, and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction, to read as follows:

..........  
TRANSITORY

Sole.- The reforms to the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction referred to in article FIFTH of this Decree, shall enter into force on the day of its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.  
TRANSITORY ARTICLES OF THE DECREE BY WHICH VARIOUS PROVISIONS OF THE FEDERAL CODE OF ELECTORAL INSTITUTIONS AND PROCEDURES; OF THE REGULATORY LAW OF SECTIONS I AND II OF ARTICLE 105 OF THE POLITICAL CONSTITUTION OF THE UNITED MEXICAN STATES; OF THE ORGANIC LAW OF THE JUDICIAL POWER OF THE FEDERATION; OF THE PENAL CODE FOR THE FEDERAL DISTRICT IN MATTERS OF COMMON JURISDICTION AND FOR THE ENTIRE REPUBLIC IN MATTERS OF FEDERAL JURISDICTION; OF THE STATUTE OF GOVERNMENT OF THE FEDERAL DISTRICT ARE REFORMED, ADDED AND REPEALED; AND THE GENERAL LAW OF THE SYSTEM OF MEANS OF CHALLENGE IN ELECTORAL MATTERS IS ISSUED
FIRST.- The reforms referred to in this Decree shall enter into force on the day of its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation, with the particularities established in the transitory provisions of each of the articles of this Decree.  

SECOND.- All provisions that oppose this Decree are repealed.  
THIRD.- On July 6, 1997, exclusively the Head of Government and the Deputies to the Legislative Assembly of the Federal District shall be elected for the Federal District.  
All articles of the Statute of Government of the Federal District referring to the election of Citizen Councilors are repealed.  
The rules that regulate the substantive functions of the current Citizen Councilors established in the regulations in force, shall continue to apply until the end of the period for which they were elected.  

Based on the new Statute of Government of the Federal District, the Legislative Assembly shall issue the provisions relating to citizen participation in the Federal District.  
FOURTH.- The head of the Federal Executive Power is authorized so that through the Secretariat of Finance and Public Credit, the necessary budgetary transfers may be made, so that the corresponding authorities may comply with the obligations and carry out the new activities that these reforms and additions impose on them.  
FIFTH.- The jurisprudential criteria held by the Central Chamber and the Chamber of Second Instance of the Federal Electoral Tribunal, as applicable, shall continue to be applicable as long as they do not oppose the reforms established in articles SECOND, THIRD and FOURTH of this Decree.  
For the jurisprudential criteria referred to in the preceding paragraph to be binding, the formal declaration of the Superior Chamber of the Electoral Tribunal shall be required. Once the declaration is made, the jurisprudence shall be immediately notified to the Regional Chambers, to the Federal Electoral Institute and, where applicable, to the local electoral authorities.  
Mexico City,  
F.D., on November 19, 1996.- Rep. Heriberto M. Galindo Quiñones, President.- Sen. Angel Sergio Guerrero Mier, President.- Rep. Josué Valdés Mondragón, Secretary.- Sen. Jorge Gpe. López Tijerina, Secretary.- Signatures".  

In compliance with the provisions of section I of Article 89 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, and for its due publication and observance, I issue this Decree at the residence of the Federal Executive Power, in Mexico City, Federal District, on the nineteenth day of the month of November nineteen hundred ninety-six.- Ernesto Zedillo Ponce de León.- Signature.- The Secretary of the Interior, Emilio Chuayffet Chemor.- Signature.

FIRST.- This Decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.  

SECOND.- Persons who at the date of entry into force of this Decree are subject to criminal proceedings for any of the offenses contained in the provisions of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction, mentioned in Article One of this Decree, shall be subject to the provisions of said articles in force at the time of commission of the offense.  

Criminal and amparo proceedings not concluded on the date indicated in the preceding transitory provision shall continue to be processed in accordance with the provisions of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures and the Amparo Law in force prior to the date of entry into force of this Decree.  
THIRD.- Resolutions of the judicial authority in which house arrest originally decreed has been extended shall continue to subsist for the entire term that has been indicated in the extension.

TRANSITORY PROVISIONS

FIRST.- This Decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.  
SECOND.- Persons who have committed an offense contemplated in this Decree prior to its entry into force, including those prosecuted or sentenced, shall be subject to the provisions of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction and the Industrial Property Law in force at the time the offense was committed.

FIRST.- This decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.  

SECOND.- The articles of Title Twenty-Fifth of the Federal Penal Code, in force until the entry into force of this decree, shall continue to apply for acts performed during their validity. Likewise, said provisions shall continue to apply to persons prosecuted or sentenced for crimes provided for and punished by the same articles. The foregoing without prejudice to applying, when appropriate, the provisions of article 56 of the aforementioned Federal Penal Code.  
Mexico City,  
Federal District, December 27, 2001.- Rep. Beatriz Elena Paredes Rangel, President.- Sen. Diego Fernández de Cevallos Ramos, President.- Rep. Martha Silvia Sánchez González, Secretary.-Sen. Yolanda E. González Hernández, Secretary.- Signatures".  

In compliance with the provisions of section I of Article 89 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, and for its due publication and observance, I issue this Decree at the Residence of the Federal Executive Power, in Mexico City, Federal District, on the first day of February two thousand two.- Vicente Fox Quesada.- Signature.- The Secretary of the Interior, Santiago Creel Miranda.-Signature.

Article Fourth.- Subsections i) and j) of section I of article 85 are reformed; subsections k) and l) are added to section I of article 85, and article 240 Bis of the Federal Penal Code is repealed, to read as follows:
……….  
Transitory

First. This Decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.  
Second. The offenses provided for in articles 112 Bis of the Law on Credit Institutions and 240 Bis of the Federal Penal Code in force until the entry into force of this decree shall continue to apply for acts committed during their validity. Likewise, said provisions shall continue to apply to persons prosecuted or sentenced for the offenses provided for and punished by the same articles. The foregoing without prejudice to applying, when appropriate, the provisions of article 56 of the aforementioned Federal Penal Code.  

Third. For the purposes, subjects and effects of the preceding article, the offenses provided for in article 240 Bis of the Federal Penal Code shall continue to be classified as serious under the terms of article 194 of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures, for all applicable legal effects.  
Mexico City,  
D.F., April 22, 2008.- Rep. Ruth Zavaleta Salgado, President.- Sen. Santiago Creel Miranda, President.- Rep. Esmeralda Cardenas Sanchez, Secretary.- Sen. Adrián Rivera Pérez, Secretary.- Signatures."

Fernando Francisco Gómez Mont Urueta.- Signature.  

DECREE reforming, adding and repealing various provisions of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector, the Law on Public Works and Related Services, the Federal Law on Administrative Responsibilities of Public Servants and the Federal Penal Code.  
Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on May 28, 2009

Article Fourth.- Article 217 second paragraph is reformed and its third and fourth paragraphs are repealed, of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

……….  
TRANSITORY ARTICLES

FIRST. This Decree shall enter into force thirty calendar days following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation, except for the provisions that for their application require modifications to the CompraNet electronic system referred to in the Tenth transitory article of this Decree.  
SECOND. All provisions that oppose the provisions of this Decree are repealed.  
THIRD. Until the corresponding reforms to the Regulations of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and to the Regulations of the Law on Public Works and Related Services are issued, as well as the other administrative provisions derived from this Decree, the regulations of said laws and administrative provisions in force on the matter shall continue to apply, insofar as they do not oppose this Decree.  

FOURTH. The Secretariat of Public Function shall conduct an annual evaluation to determine the increase in coverage of participation of social witnesses referred to in articles 26 Ter of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and 27 Bis of the Law on Public Works and Related Services. Likewise, that Secretariat shall implement measures for the purpose of expanding said coverage to guarantee, within a period of ten years from the entry into force of this Law, total coverage of social witnesses in public contracting.  

FIFTH. For the adequate application of the criterion for evaluation of proposals through the mechanism of points and percentages referred to in articles 36 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and 38 of the Law on Public Works and Related Services, the Secretariat of Public Function shall issue within a period no greater than ninety calendar days, counted from the entry into force of this Decree, the corresponding guidelines.  

SIXTH. The guidelines referred to in the second paragraph of articles 48 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and of the Law on Public Works and Related Services, must be issued within a period no greater than six months, counted from the entry into force of the aforementioned legal provisions.  
SEVENTH. Contracting procedures that are in process as of the date of entry into force of this Decree shall continue to be processed until their conclusion in accordance with the provisions of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and the Law on Public Works and Related Services, as applicable, in force at the time of their initiation.  

EIGHTH. Contracts entered into before the entry into force of this Decree shall continue to be governed until their termination by the provisions of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and the Law on Public Works and Related Services, as applicable, in force at the time of their execution.  

NINTH. Conciliation, complaint and sanction procedures that are in process or pending resolution as of the date of entry into force of this Decree must be substantiated and concluded in accordance with the provisions in force at the time such procedures were initiated.  

TENTH. The modifications to the CompraNet government contracting electronic system that allow the application of the reforms made by this Decree to the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and to the Law on Public Works and Related Services must be completed within a period no greater than one year, counted from the date of entry into force of this Decree.  

The following articles shall enter into force within said period as the modifications referred to in the preceding paragraph are made: article 37 fifth paragraph regarding notification of the award in CompraNet; 48 second paragraph regarding the obligation of agencies and entities to consider the compliance records of suppliers in contracts for the purpose of determining the percentages of guarantees; 50 last paragraph, 56 and 69 second paragraph, of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector; 39 penultimate paragraph regarding notification of the award in CompraNet; 48 second paragraph regarding the obligation of agencies and entities to consider the compliance records of contractors in contracts for the purpose of determining the percentages of guarantees; 51 last paragraph, 74 and 87 second paragraph, of the Law on Public Works and Related Services.  

Until the provisions referred to in the preceding paragraph enter into force, the acts indicated therein shall continue to be carried out in accordance with the regulations in force.  
Within a period of six months counted from the entry into force of this Decree, the information relating to annual programs on acquisitions, leases and services and public works and related services of agencies and entities, registry of social witnesses, registry of sanctioned suppliers and contractors, and testimonies of social witnesses, referred to in articles 56 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and 74 of the Law on Public Works and Related Services, shall be available in CompraNet.  

In the case of agencies and entities that have a database on the compliance of suppliers and contractors in contracts they have entered into with them, they may apply upon the entry into force of this Decree the provisions of the second paragraph of article 48 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and the second paragraph of article 48 of the Law on Public Works and Related Services, in accordance with the guidelines issued by the Secretariat of Public Function.  
ELEVENTH. The Secretariat of Economy shall progressively increase the percentage of national content referred to in section I of article 28 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector, up to sixty-five percent, within a period of three years, counted from the entry into force of this Decree.  
TWELFTH. The administrative unit of the Secretariat of Public Function referred to in articles 2 section II and 56 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public

Sector and 2 section II and 74 of the Law on Public Works and Related Services shall not imply the creation of new organic and occupational structures, nor the creation of budgetary positions.  
THIRTEENTH. The Secretariat of Public Function shall issue guidelines that promote the expediting of payments to suppliers, which are regulated in article 51 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector, within a period no greater than six months, counted from the entry into force of this Decree.  
FOURTEENTH. Regardless of the exceptions to the bidding procedure provided for in article 42 of the Law on Public Works and Related Services, from the day following the publication of this Decree in the Official Gazette of the Federation and until December 31, 2009, any contracting of construction, maintenance or repair of works shall also be excepted from the public bidding procedure, in which it is proven to have intensive use of labor representing at least 50% of the total cost of the project.  

FIFTEENTH. The sum of the amounts of contracts made during the year 2009, under article 43 of the Law on Public Works and Related Services, may not exceed thirty-five percent of the authorized budget to carry out public works and related services; the amount assigned to each contractor may not exceed five percent of said budget.  

SIXTEENTH. The Federal Executive must report to the Budget and Public Account Commission of the Chamber of Deputies, as applicable, through the Permanent Commission of the Congress of the Union, on the progress in savings generated as a result of the application of measures relating to the rationalization of expenditure provided for in the Medium-Term Program, in accordance with the provisions of articles 45 and 61 of the Federal Law on Budget and Fiscal Responsibility; in the second article of the Decree reforming, adding and repealing various provisions of the Federal Law on Budget and Fiscal Responsibility; Organic Law of the Federal Public Administration, Fiscal Coordination, Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector, and Public Works and Related Services, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on October 1, 2007; as well as article 16 of the Decree of Expenditure Budget of the Federation for fiscal year 2009.  

The Budget and Public Account Commission of the Chamber of Deputies shall take into account the information provided by the Federal Executive, regarding the concepts indicated in article 16 of the Decree of Expenditure Budget of the Federation for fiscal year 2009, for purposes of the approval process of the Expenditure Budget of the Federation for fiscal year 2010.  
SEVENTEENTH.- The Federal Executive, through the Secretariat of Public Function and the Secretariat of Environment and Natural Resources, within a period of 30 calendar days counted from the entry into force of this Decree, shall issue the guidelines for the proper application of the provisions of the fourth paragraph of article 26 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector.  

Mexico City,  
D.F., April 30, 2009.- Rep. Cesar Horacio Duarte Jaquez, President.- Sen. Gustavo Enrique Madero Muñoz, President.- Rep. Maria Eugenia Jimenez Valenzuela, Secretary.- Sen. Gabino Cue Monteagudo, Secretary.- Signatures."

two thousand nine.- Felipe de Jesús Calderón Hinojosa.- Signature.- The Secretary of the Interior, Lic.  
Fernando Francisco Gómez Mont Urueta.- Signature.

FIRST.- This Decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.  
For the purpose of the provisions of article 474 of the General Health Law, the local legislatures and the Legislative Assembly of the Federal District shall have a period of one year from the entry into force of this Decree to make the appropriate amendments to the legislation.  

The Federation and the federal entities shall have a period of three years from the entry into force of this Decree to carry out the necessary actions, as applicable, in order to duly comply with the powers contained therein.  
SECOND.- Criminal proceedings being substantiated at the entry into force of this Decree shall continue in accordance with the provisions in force at the time of the commission of the acts.  
THIRD.- Persons who have committed an offense contemplated in this Decree prior to its entry into force, including those prosecuted or sentenced, shall be subject to the provisions in force at the time the offense was committed.  
FOURTH.- The competent authorities shall finance the actions derived from compliance with this Decree with the resources annually provided for in the Expenditure Budget of the Federation, without detriment to the resources that the federal entities contribute for such purposes.  
FIFTH.- All provisions that oppose this Decree are repealed.  
Mexico City,
